TL;DR: 本文提出SciQNet，一个两阶段多模态适配框架用于科学图像质量评估。第一阶段进行领域自适应预训练，第二阶段通过联合评分与理解监督进行任务特定微调。在ICME 2026科学图像质量评估挑战赛中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图像是传达实验观察、定量证据和概念知识的重要媒介。与传统自然图像不同，科学图像的质量不仅取决于视觉清晰度，还取决于科学信息量，这使得其质量评估更具挑战性。现有方法难以同时兼顾这两个维度。

Method: SciQNet采用两阶段多模态适配框架：第一阶段在科学文档图像上进行领域自适应预训练；第二阶段进行任务特定微调，联合使用评分导向监督和语义理解导向监督。评分监督结合了指令微调与基于评分词logits的Huber损失，理解监督则采用多项选择视觉问答形式。

Result: 实验表明，使用领域自适应数据的40%分层子集可获得最佳性能，说明预训练数据的相关性可能比规模更重要。最终模型在SIQA-S上达到92.21分，SIQA-U上达到47.38分，综合得分为69.80分，在ICME 2026挑战赛评分赛道中排名第二。

Conclusion: SciQNet证明了两阶段多模态适配框架在科学图像质量评估中的有效性，并揭示了预训练数据相关性优于数据规模这一重要发现，为科学图像评估领域提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Scientific images are essential for communicating experimental observations, quantitative evidence and conceptual knowledge. Unlike natural images, their quality depends on both visual clarity and scientific informativeness, making assessment challenging. In this work, we present SciQNet, a two-stage multimodal adaptation framework for scientific image quality assessment. The first stage performs domain-adaptive pretraining on scientific document images and the second stage conducts task-specific fine-tuning with joint scoring and understanding supervision. For scoring-oriented supervision, we combine instruction tuning with a Huber loss derived from rating-word logits, while understanding-oriented supervision is formulated as multiple-choice visual question answering. Experiments show that using a 40% stratified subset of the domain-adaptive data gives the best performance among the evaluated pretraining fractions, suggesting that pretraining-data relevance may be as important as pretraining-data scale. The final model achieves an SIQA-S score of 92.21, an SIQA-U score of 47.38 and a combined score of 69.80. This work presents our solution to the ICME 2026 Scientific Image Quality Assessment Challenge, which ranked 2nd in the scoring track.

</details>


### [36] [Shape-Aware Oriented Bounding Box (OBB) to Horizontal Bounding Box (HBB) Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05858)
*Badha Rathna Sabhapathy,Gotam Dahiya,Vishesh Vatsal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向遥感船舶检测的形状感知OBB到HBB转换方法，利用船体形状、饱满度和边界框方向生成更紧凑的轴对齐边界框，优于三种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空和卫星图像中，物体检测的准确性依赖于边界框表示。对于船舶、飞机等空间定向物体，有向边界框（OBB）比水平边界框（HBB）具有更紧密的拟合度和更鲁棒的非极大值抑制。然而，现有的OBB转HBB后处理转换要么引入多余的空白/背景空间，要么丢失检测数据，因此需要一种更优的转换方法。

Method: 提出一种形状感知的OBB到HBB转换方法，综合考虑船体形状（hull shape）、船体饱满度（hull fullness）和边界框方向（bounding box orientation），从而生成更紧凑的轴对齐HBB表示。

Result: 该方法与三种基线方法进行了基准对比：Outer HBB（使用最小最大值的最大外接框）、Area Equivalent HBB（面积等效HBB）和GBB Marginalized HBB，结果显示所提方法在生成更紧致的HBB方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 形状感知的OBB到HBB转换能够有效减少多余背景空间并避免数据丢失，为遥感图像中的船舶检测提供更准确的边界框表示。

Abstract: Accurate object detection in aerial and satellite imagery is dependent upon the bounding box representation. This is especially true for spatially oriented objects such as ships or aircrafts. Oriented Bounding Boxes (OBB) have a tighter fit and more robust non-max suppression compared to Horizontal Bounding Boxes (HBB), any current post-processing conversion from OBB to HBB either introduces excess empty and background space or removes data from the detection. This paper introduces a novel approach for a shape-aware OBB-to-HBB conversion for ship detection in remote sensing imagery. It leverages hull shape, hull fullness, and the bounding box orientation to produce a tighter axis-aligned HBB representation. The proposed method is benchmarked against three baselines methods for OBBto-HBB conversion, Outer HBB which uses minimum and maximum, Area Equivalent HBB and GBB Marginalized HBB.

</details>


### [37] [TAU-Bench: From Anomaly Instance Tracking to Fine-Grained Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05699)
*Kepeng Yang,Dongxuan Liu,Rongxin Gao,Zixin Su,Rui Wu,Shuzhao Xie,Chenxin Li,Panwang Pan,Yuzhi Huang,Yue Huang,Jingyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAU-Bench，一个以轨迹为中心的基准测试，用于联合评估视频异常理解中的异常实例跟踪与细粒度语义理解，并揭示当前VLM在语义推理与视觉锚定之间存在持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常理解（VAU）中，视觉语言模型（VLM）虽然能生成流畅且合理的异常描述，但其语义流畅性并不保证解释始终锚定在正确的异常实例上。现有基准将轨迹跟踪与语义理解分开评测，导致实例-语义不一致问题未被系统度量。

Method: 构建TAU-Bench基准：包含1,118个视频、1,454条轨迹、202,438个像素级掩码，覆盖49种事件和45种场景类别，并提供以轨迹为中心的标注（连接实例识别、事件理解与场景推理）。同时开发了自动化数据引擎，集成异常适宜性过滤、异常实例轨迹构建、分层标题标注和人工质量控制。

Result: 对代表性VLM系列的评估表明，能够生成合理异常解释的模型仍可能在定位和跟踪正确异常实例方面失败，揭示了语义推理与视觉锚定之间的持久差距。

Conclusion: 实例锚定的评估是迈向更忠实、更可靠的视频异常理解系统的重要一步。

Abstract: Humans understand anomalous events through a coherent perceptual process in which they identify the focal instance, follow its behavior as the event unfolds, and interpret why it violates the expectations of the surrounding scene. Video anomaly understanding (VAU) seeks to endow models with a similar capability, moving beyond deciding whether a video is anomalous toward explaining how the event develops and why it matters. Although recent vision--language models (VLMs) can generate detailed and plausible anomaly descriptions, their semantic fluency does not ensure that these interpretations remain grounded in the correct anomaly instance over time. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate tracking and semantic understanding through separate protocols, leaving such instance--semantic inconsistency largely unmeasured. We therefore introduce TAU-Bench, a track-centric benchmark for jointly evaluating anomaly instance tracking and fine-grained anomaly understanding. TAU-Bench contains 1,118 videos, 1,454 tracks, and 202,438 pixel-level masks spanning 49 event and 45 scene categories, together with track-centric annotations that connect instance-level identification, event-level understanding, and scene-level reasoning. To build TAU-Bench at scale, we developed an automated data engine integrating anomaly suitability filtering, anomaly instance track construction, hierarchical caption annotation, and human quality control. Evaluations across representative VLM families show that models producing plausible anomaly interpretations may still fail to localize and track the correct instance reliably, revealing a persistent gap between semantic reasoning and visual grounding. These findings therefore highlight instance-grounded evaluation as an important step toward more faithful and reliable VAU systems.

</details>


### [38] [Robust-WAM: Bridging Generative Pretraining and Semantic Foresight in World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05903)
*Haodong Yan,Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,MingMing Yu,Wenxuan Song,Jiaguan Zhu,Yangyang Zheng,Yuqiao Du,Jiadi You,Yingjie Cai,Xu Yan,Guanyi Zhao,Bingbing Liu,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Robust-WAM是一种通用的后训练方法，通过在视频生成式世界行动模型（WAM）的动作流上添加轻量级语义前景对齐目标，保留VAE潜在空间的大规模预训练优势，同时将动作预测锚定在对外观变化（如光照偏移）不敏感的动态语义上，从而提升模型在分布外条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流WAM在VAE潜在空间中工作，该空间为像素重建优化，偏好精细外观细节，导致动作预测在视觉偏移下脆弱；而语义潜在空间虽然对外观变化更鲁棒，但无法利用仅在VAE空间中存在的大规模视频生成模型预训练。

Method: Robust-WAM保留VAE生成路径，在动作流上添加轻量级语义前景对齐目标。具体地，使用可学习的查询token将未来场景语义引入动作流，通过将输出隐藏状态与未来真实帧的语义前景对齐来实现；同时为每个查询token赋予对应动作token的位置编码，以建立查询与其描述的未来步骤之间的时间对应关系。

Result: 在分布外泛化仿真基准和真实机器人设置上的实验表明，Robust-WAM能一致地提升多个WAM基线的成功率，且不牺牲分布内性能。

Conclusion: Robust-WAM有效解决了VAE潜在空间与语义潜在空间之间的权衡问题，在保留大规模VGM预训练优势的同时，通过语义前景对齐提升了动作预测对外观偏移的鲁棒性，是一种通用且不损失分布内性能的后训练方法。

Abstract: Mainstream World-Action Models (WAMs) adapt pretrained video generation models (VGMs) for robot control, transferring their learned dynamics prior for action prediction. These VGMs are typically trained in a variational autoencoder (VAE) latent space. However, the VAE latent space is optimized for pixel reconstruction, which rewards fine appearance detail and leaves the action prediction fragile under visual shifts. Recent works build WAMs in semantic latent space, which are more robust to appearance shifts. However, these models cannot leverage the large-scale VGM pretraining that exists only in VAE space. To overcome this dilemma, we propose Robust-WAM, a general post-training method for video-generation-based WAMs that preserves the VAE-based generative path and adds a lightweight semantic foresight alignment objective on the action stream. This retains the large-scale VGM pretraining while grounding actions in appearance-invariant dynamics that stay reliable under illumination shifts and other visual out-of-distribution conditions. Specifically, we employ learnable query tokens to bring future-scene semantics into the action stream by aligning their output hidden states with the semantic foresight of future ground-truth frames. To establish the temporal correspondence between each query and the future step it describes, we give it the positional encoding of the matching action tokens. Experiments on out-of-distribution generalization simulation benchmarks and a real-robot setup show that our Robust-WAM consistently improves the success rates of multiple WAM baselines without sacrificing in-distribution performance.

</details>


### [39] [StreamArena: Toward Continuous, Interactive, and Long-Horizon Agentic Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05703)
*Xichen Zhang,Guankai Li,Yinghao Zhu,Shijian Wang,Sitong Wu,Shaozuo Yu,Meng Chu,Yuan Lu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamArena基准，用于评估小时级交互式流视频理解，包含243个平均88.8分钟的视频和3,646个开放式问答对；同时提出StreamMind双层级架构，通过前端工作者处理延迟关键交互和主动监控，后端工作者异步构建持久多模态记忆，在四项能力上均优于现有流式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主多模态智能体评估主要依赖短视频片段和选择题格式，存在两个关键缺陷：一是仅处理最后四帧的简单基线就能超过复杂流式模型，二是答案选项暴露了语言捷径。这导致现有基准无法真实衡量模型在连续实世界环境中长时记忆和流式理解的能力。

Method: 构建StreamArena基准（243个全长视频、平均时长88.8分钟、3,646个严格标注的开放式问答对），评估实时感知、历史回顾、主动交互和工具利用四种能力；针对连续交互与长视界理解的矛盾，提出StreamMind双层级架构——延迟关键交互和主动监控由独立调度的前端工作者承担，后端工作者异步构建持久多模态记忆并执行历史回忆与外部搜索。

Result: 评估揭示连续交互与长视界多模态理解之间的张力：仅保留最近帧的方法无法恢复远端事件，将历史观测转为文本的方法丢失视觉证据，反复压缩视觉记忆的方法难以随时间保留细粒度细节。StreamMind在全部四项能力上优于现有流式基线，并通过复用持久状态降低了查询到回答的延迟。

Conclusion: StreamArena提供了更真实的时长级流式视频理解评估框架，暴露了现有方法的缺陷；StreamMind证明了双层级架构能够有效平衡实时交互响应与长期多模态记忆，是该方向的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous multimodal agents in continuous, real-world environments requires them to ingest unbounded audio-visual streams and maintain hour-scale memory. However, current evaluations predominantly rely on brief clips and multiple-choice formats. This design allows minimal baselines that process only the last four frames to match or surpass complex streaming models, while answer options also expose language shortcuts. We introduce StreamArena, a benchmark for hour-scale, interactive streaming video understanding. StreamArena contains 243 full-length videos averaging 88.8 minutes and 3,646 rigorously annotated, open-ended question-answer pairs that evaluate real-time perception, historical retrospection, proactive interaction, and multimodal tool utilization. Evaluation across diverse systems exposes a tension between continuous interaction and long-horizon multimodal comprehension. Methods that retain only recent frames cannot recover distant events, methods that convert past observations into text lose visual evidence, and methods that repeatedly compress visual memory struggle to preserve fine-grained details over time. We address this tension with StreamMind, a two-tier architecture that assigns latency-critical interaction and proactive monitoring to independently scheduled frontend workers, while backend workers asynchronously construct persistent multimodal memory and perform historical recall and external search. StreamMind outperforms existing streaming baselines across all four capabilities and reduces query-to-answer latency by reusing persistent state.

</details>


### [40] [G$^2$ARD-GS: Geometry-Guided Anchor-Regularized Gaussian Splatting Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05704)
*Puyuan Zhang,Jianming Huang,Wenkai Ye,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G²ARD-GS是一种几何引导的蒸馏方法，将稠密的彩色LiDAR地图（或密集高斯先验）转换为紧凑、可复用的3DGS表示，在5x-30x压缩率下达到最佳PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS，并优于现有方法3.2-6.8dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密彩色LiDAR地图提升为3DGS后保留数百万个图元，导致存储、传输、渲染和自适应成本高昂；而激进的图元削减虽能减轻负担，却会移除局部表面支撑，破坏新视角合成稳定性和下游几何应用。

Method: G²ARD-GS将稠密高斯先验（训练无关的点云提升或已训练GS模型）逐步整合为表面感知的代表性图元，然后在施工期锚点约束下在固定拓扑上恢复外观，恢复阶段不增减图元。在有限监督下，采用几何感知视角选择来分配可用的视角预算。

Result: 在MatrixCity上，G²ARD-GS在匹配的5x-30x压缩预算下获得最佳PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS，PSNR优于PUP 3.2-6.8dB；冻结几何复用时，离轨迹外观适应比PUP 3D-GS提升3.7-4.9dB，并在30x压缩下保持Cambridge KingsCollege上图像到模型配准精度。

Conclusion: G²ARD-GS通过几何引导蒸馏实现了高压缩率下紧凑可复用的3DGS表示，既保持了渲染质量和几何稳定性，又支持下游几何任务，为大规模LiDAR城市级场景的表示压缩提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Dense colored LiDAR maps provide accurate city-scale geometry, but lifting them into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) retains millions of primitives, making the resulting models costly to store, transmit, render, and adapt. Aggressive primitive reduction alleviates this burden, but can remove the local surface support needed for stable novel-view synthesis and downstream geometric use. We introduce G$^2$ARD-GS, a geometry-guided distillation method that converts a dense Gaussian prior instantiated either as a training-free point-cloud lift or a trained GS model into a compact, reusable representation. G$^2$ARD-GS progressively consolidates the prior into surface-aware representatives, then recovers appearance on the resulting fixed topology under construction-time anchor constraints, with no primitives added or removed during recovery. Under limited supervision, geometry-aware view selection allocates the available view budget. On MatrixCity, G$^2$ARD-GS achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS across matched $5\times$--$30\times$ compression budgets, outperforming PUP by $3.2$--$6.8$,dB in PSNR. When reused as frozen geometry, the compact model improves off-trajectory appearance adaptation by $3.7$--$4.9$,dB over PUP 3D-GS and preserves image-to-model registration accuracy on Cambridge KingsCollege at $30\times$ compression. Project page: https://patrick1159.github.io/gardGS-page/.

</details>


### [41] [LiteKD-Net: Lightweight Knowledge-Distilled Network for Mobile Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05739)
*Zhou Zhiyi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteKD-Net是一种面向移动端图像去噪的轻量级知识蒸馏网络，通过物理引导的噪声模拟管线生成训练数据，并利用基于深度可分离卷积的Lite-RRDB模块构建轻量学生网络，通过特征级知识蒸馏从教师网络（适配的Real-ESRGAN）中学习复原能力，在保持良好复原质量的同时显著降低计算开销，在真实数据集上优于SwinIR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端图像去噪需要在复原质量和计算成本之间取得平衡，同时大规模收集LQ-GT干净配对数据既费时又令人困扰。现有方法难以同时满足移动端对低计算成本和良好复原质量的需求。

Method: 1) 提出物理引导的噪声模拟管线，通过添加像素串扰（pixel crosstalk）生成配对训练数据；2) 将Real-ESRGAN适配到同分辨率去噪任务作为教师网络；3) 基于深度可分离卷积设计Lite-RRDB块构建轻量学生网络；4) 通过特征级知识蒸馏将教师网络的复原能力迁移至学生网络，且不增加推理成本。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，模型在运行时间和推理速度上大幅提升，同时保持良好的复原质量；在所有指标上均优于SwinIR，实现了复原质量与计算效率之间的良好权衡。

Conclusion: LiteKD-Net为移动端图像去噪提供了良好的复原质量与计算效率之间的最佳折中方案，验证了知识蒸馏与轻量网络设计在移动端去噪任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile image denoising requires both good restoration quality and low computational cost. In addition, it's annoying to collect large-scale LQ-GT clean pairs. As a result, we propose LiteKD-Net, a lightweight knowledge-distilled network for mobile image denoising. First, a physics-guided noise simulation pipeline generates paired training data by adding pixel crosstalk compared with pipelines applied to cameras. Next, we adapt the Real-ESRGAN to identity-resolution denoising and construct a lightweight Student using Lite-RRDB blocks based on depthwise separable convolutions. Third, feature-level knowledge distillation is applied to transfer the Teacher's restoration capability to the Student without introducing additional inference cost. Experiments on real-world datasets show that our model reaches great reduction in runtime and increase in the inference rate with good restoration quality. Our model also reaches the best in all metrics compared with SwinIR. These results indicate that LiteKD-Net provides a great trade-off between restoration quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [42] [ConceptADapt: Concept-guided Adaptive Feature Reconstruction with Dynamic Attention for Few-Shot Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05743)
*Yufei Li,Yicheng Ruan,Long Tian,Dongsheng Wang,Liang Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConceptADapt，一种概念引导的自适应特征重建模型，用于小样本工业异常检测。通过预学习固定正常概念、动态注意力机制与稀疏自编码器，以及轻量级LoRA模块，在MVTec-AD、VisA和MPDD三个基准上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型特征具有很强的表征能力，但在小样本工业异常检测（FS-IAD）场景下，由于正常训练样本极度稀缺，模型泛化能力仍然脆弱。此外，低数据条件下特征捷径（feature shortcut）问题尤为严重，阻碍了模型的鲁棒性。

Method: ConceptADapt包含三个核心组件：(1) 从有限支持特征中预学习一组固定正常概念，用于挖掘与查询特征的关系并重校准其统计量；(2) 动态注意力机制与稀疏自编码器结合，在训练过程中学习鲁棒正常概念，缓解特征捷径问题；(3) 将LoRA集成到注意力模块中，仅引入极小参数更新量，实现推理时的快速适应。

Result: 在MVTec-AD、VisA和MPDD三个广泛采用的FS-IAD基准上，ConceptADapt在检测和定位任务上均一致优于现有SOTA方法，在不同shot设置下均获得显著提升。

Conclusion: ConceptADapt通过概念引导的统计量重校准、动态注意力与稀疏自编码器联合学习正常概念，以及LoRA轻量化适配策略，有效解决了小样本工业异常检测中的泛化脆弱性和特征捷径问题，验证了该方法在多种设置下的优越性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Few-shot industrial anomaly detection (FS-IAD) focuses on detecting and localizing visual defects in industrial inspection during the cold-start phase, where only a limited number of normal training samples are available per category. Recent advances in this field predominantly leverage visual features from foundation-model and have achieved promising performance. Despite the strong representational power of foundation-model features, the model generalization remains fragile due to the extreme scarcity of normal training data.To address this pivotal issue, we propose ConceptADapt, a concept-guided adaptive feature reconstruction model with dynamic attention. Specifically, our model pre-learns a set of fixed normal concepts from the limited support features and leverages them to mine relationships with query features, thereby recalibrating their statistics for improved anomaly detection at test time. To mitigate the prevalent feature shortcut problem, which is particularly severe under low-data regimes, we further develop a dynamic attention mechanism integrated with sparse autoencoders to learn robust normal concepts during training. Moreover, to enable fast adaptation during inference, our model remains lightweight by incorporating LoRA into the attention module, which introduces only minimal updating parameters.Extensive experiments on three widely adopted FS-IAD benchmarks, including MVTec-AD, VisA, and MPDD, demonstrate that our model consistently outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches across both detection and localization tasks, achieving significant improvements under various shot settings.

</details>


### [43] [UniVVT: A Unified End-to-End Framework for High-Fidelity Video Virtual Try-on](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05745)
*Yushe Cao,Shikun Feng,Fei Shen,Haikuo Peng,Jianqiang Xia,Yiheng Zhu,Dianxi Shi,Chun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniVVT是一个统一的端到端视频虚拟试穿框架，将VVT重构为语义条件视频生成任务，通过多模态大语言模型构建的场景-任务感知器隐式编码源视频、目标服装和任务指令，消除了推理时的mask、姿态和形变模块，并采用三阶段渐进训练策略实现最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法依赖多阶段流水线（人体解析、姿态估计、服装形变），这种分离式设计不仅部署复杂，更关键的是显式几何先验中的错误会不可逆地传播到生成的视频中，影响最终生成质量。

Method: UniVVT将VVT重新定义为语义条件视频生成任务。核心组件包括：(1) 基于多模态大语言模型的场景-任务感知器，联合编码源视频、目标服装和任务指令为紧凑的任务感知潜变量令牌，隐式捕获迁移内容和迁移方式；(2) 轻量级语义桥接模块，将这些令牌与基于扩散的视频生成器的条件空间对齐；(3) 三阶段渐进训练策略：语义对齐、联合任务适配和灵活分辨率精化，以稳健地耦合异构组件。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，UniVVT取得了最先进的性能，证明隐式语义引导作为端到端虚拟试穿中脆弱几何预处理的简单有效替代方案。

Conclusion: UniVVT验证了统一端到端架构在视频虚拟试穿中的可行性，隐式语义引导能够替代传统显式几何预处理流水线，实现更简洁且鲁棒的服装迁移。

Abstract: Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) synthesizes a video of a person wearing a target garment while preserving identity, motion, and scene dynamics. Dominant approaches cast VVT as mask-conditioned video inpainting and rely on separate modules for human parsing, pose estimation, and garment warping. This multi-stage design complicates deployment and, more critically, allows errors in explicit geometric priors to propagate irreversibly into the generated video. We present UniVVT, a unified end-to-end framework that reframes VVT as semantically conditioned video generation, eliminating mask, pose, and warping modules at inference. At its core, a scene-task perceiver built on a Multimodal Large Language Model jointly encodes the source video, target garment, and task instruction into compact, task-aware latent tokens, implicitly capturing what to transfer and where and how to transfer it. A lightweight semantic bridge then aligns these tokens with the conditioning space of a diffusion-based video generator, enabling coherent garment transfer. To robustly couple the heterogeneous components, we devise a three-stage progressive training strategy comprising semantic alignment, joint task adaptation, and flexible-resolution refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UniVVT achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, validating implicit semantic guidance as a simple and effective alternative to fragile geometric preprocessing for end-to-end virtual try-on.

</details>


### [44] [GST-Bench: Can VLMs Develop Global Spatial Awareness from Video?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05747)
*Qifeng Zhang,Kaixiang Huang,Heng Dong,Huang Fang,Junting Chen,Junjie Zhu,Yonghang Chen,Zhiyu Zhang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GST-Bench，一个用于评估视频理解中全局空间智能的VQA基准，包含6,790分钟合成视频的人类验证问题，要求模型从新颖视角做空间推理并将第一人称观察映射到全局俯视图。评估22个SOTA视觉语言模型发现最强零样本模型仅得42.68分，远低于人类79.08分；进一步构建GST-Bench-Local证明模型在局部空间理解上表现良好，但无法将长时序观察整合为全局一致的场景表示，并提供GST-Train数据集作为补充资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能基准仅关注从单一或少数视角进行的局部空间感知，忽视了在连续长时程视觉流上的全局空间意识。这一局限性阻碍了对具身智能体全局空间推理能力的评估，亟需一个能够衡量模型从视频流中构建全局空间表示的基准。

Method: 构建包含6,790分钟合成视频的GST-Bench基准，问题由人工验证，要求模型（1）从输入视频中未见的新颖视角进行准确空间推理，（2）将自我中心（第一人称）观察映射到全局俯视图像上。同时构建GST-Bench-Local以隔离局部与全局空间理解能力，并额外提供GST-Train训练数据集。

Result: 对22个SOTA视觉语言模型进行全面评估显示模型与人类之间存在显著差距：最强零样本模型得分仅42.68，远低于人类79.08分。GST-Bench-Local实验表明模型在相同任务公式下具备较强的局部空间理解能力，但无法将长时程观察巩固为全局一致的场景表示。

Conclusion: 全局空间智能是当前视觉语言模型的核心短板，局部空间理解能力并不能自动迁移到全局场景表示构建上。GST-Bench及其配套的GST-Train数据集为弥合这一差距提供了基准和资源基础，挑战在于提升长时序观察的全局整合能力。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is fundamental to embodied agents, yet existing benchmarks focus on local spatial perception from single or few viewpoints, overlooking global spatial awareness over continuous, long-horizon visual streams. To address this limitation, we introduce the Global-Spatial-Temporal Benchmark (GST-Bench), a VQA benchmark for global spatial intelligence in video understanding, comprising human-verified questions derived from 6,790 minutes of synthetically generated video. It requires models to perform accurate spatial inference from novel viewpoints unseen in the input video and to map egocentric observations onto global top-down images. A comprehensive evaluation of 22 state-of-the-art VLMs exposes a striking gap between models and humans: the strongest zero-shot model attains only 42.68, far below the human score of 79.08. To probe the cause of this gap, we construct GST-Bench-Local and find that models, despite strong local spatial understanding under the same task formulation, still fail to consolidate long-horizon observations into a globally consistent scene representation. We further provide GST-Train, a dataset for global spatial reasoning, as a complementary resource to facilitate future research on this challenge.

</details>


### [45] [Beyond Relevance: Bayesian Evidence Acquisition for Agentic Whole-Slide Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05757)
*Bryan Wong,Xun Xu,Huazhu Fu,Nancy F. Chen,Mun Yong Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEACON是一个即插即用的智能体框架，将全切片图像（WSI）推理重构为贝叶斯证据获取问题，通过最大化期望信息增益（EIG）顺序获取patches以减少诊断不确定性，无需训练或微调，在五个WSI-VQA基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义相关性的检索方法忽略了计算病理学中竞争性诊断常具有相似重叠的形态学模式这一事实，导致许多语义相关但不具诊断区分度的patch被冗余获取，无法有效解决诊断不确定性。

Method: BEACON构建了一个贝叶斯证据获取框架：(1) 维护对竞争性诊断假设的概率信念；(2) 通过最大化期望信息增益（EIG）顺序选择要获取的patch；(3) 利用证据控制器决定是回答、获取更多证据还是进行更高分辨率的检查；全部基于现成的foundation models构建，无需额外的训练或微调。

Result: 在五个WSI-VQA基准上的零样本实验表明，BEACON在所有训练-free智能体框架中取得最强的整体性能，同时显著提升了证据获取效率。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯证据获取为不确定性感知的智能体WSI推理提供了一种有原则的新范式，在准确性和效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Whole-slide image (WSI) reasoning requires an agent to sequentially acquire visual evidence before answering a diagnostic question. Existing training-free agentic frameworks formulate this process as iterative patch retrieval based on semantic relevance to the question. However, semantic relevance does not necessarily imply diagnostic informativeness in computational pathology, where competing diagnoses often exhibit similar and overlapping morphological patterns, making many patches semantically relevant yet diagnostically non-discriminative. Consequently, relevance-based retrieval may acquire redundant observations and leave diagnostic uncertainty unresolved. We propose BEACON, a plug-and-play agentic framework that reformulates WSI reasoning as a Bayesian evidence acquisition problem. BEACON maintains a probabilistic belief over competing diagnostic hypotheses and sequentially acquires patches by maximizing expected information gain (EIG) to reduce diagnostic uncertainty. An evidence controller then determines whether to answer, acquire additional evidence, or perform higher-resolution inspection. Built entirely from off-the-shelf foundation models, BEACON requires no additional training or fine-tuning. Extensive zero-shot experiments across five WSI-VQA benchmarks demonstrate that BEACON achieves the strongest overall performance among training-free agentic frameworks while substantially improving evidence acquisition efficiency, establishing Bayesian evidence acquisition as a principled paradigm for uncertainty-aware agentic WSI reasoning. The code is available at https://github.com/bryanwong17/BEACON

</details>


### [46] [Flow-Map Distillation on Relation Manifolds for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05769)
*Zihao He,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FoRM（流映射蒸馏），将知识蒸馏中基于关系矩阵的静态对齐重新表述为关系流形上的连续流映射问题，通过学习流映射算子预测任意时刻的关系状态，配合安全半群一致性约束和端点锚定损失，在五种图像恢复任务上超越现有蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法通常将中间特征或关系矩阵作为静态目标进行对齐，忽略了知识迁移过程的动态结构。由于关系蒸馏中知识迁移是渐进过程，静态约束无法捕获轨迹级别的信息，限制了蒸馏效果和训练稳定性。

Method: 1) 将关系蒸馏重构为关系流形上的连续流映射问题，学习流映射算子F_θ(z,t,s)，给定当前状态z在时刻t可直接预测目标时刻s的关系状态，提供轨迹级监督；2) 引入安全半群一致性约束，利用真实桥接状态强制组合一致性，消除幻影状态误差累积；3) 设计端点锚定损失防止算子从教师目标漂移。

Result: 在超分辨率、去雨、去噪、去模糊和低光增强五项图像恢复任务上，跨多种骨干架构均获得优于SOTA蒸馏基线的效果，且相比朴素流匹配蒸馏将训练方差降低约50%。

Conclusion: FoRM通过将关系蒸馏提升为连续流映射学习范式，有效利用动态轨迹信息提升了学生网络的恢复质量和训练稳定性，为知识蒸馏提供了新的理论视角和实用方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation for image restoration typically aligns intermediate features or relation matrices between teacher and student networks as static targets, ignoring the dynamic structure of the knowledge transfer process. In this paper, we propose Flow-Map Distillation on Relation Manifolds (FoRM), which reformulates relation-based knowledge transfer as a continuous flow mapping problem on the relation manifold. Rather than regressing a constant velocity field between student and teacher relation states, FoRM learns a flow map operator $\mathcal{F}_θ(\mathbf{z}, t, s)$ that directly predicts the relation state at any target time $s$ given the current state at time $t$, enabling richer trajectory-level supervision. To ensure global self-consistency of the learned flow map, we introduce a safe semigroup consistency constraint that enforces compositional agreement using ground-truth bridge states, eliminating phantom-state error accumulation. An endpoint anchoring loss further prevents the operator from drifting away from the teacher target. Extensive experiments on five image restoration tasks, including super-resolution, deraining, denoising, deblurring, and low-light enhancement, demonstrate consistent gains over state-of-the-art distillation baselines across multiple backbone architectures, reducing training variance by approximately 50\% compared to naive flow matching distillation while achieving superior restoration quality.

</details>


### [47] [HyTBE: Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert Model for Cross-Domain Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05771)
*Aohua Li,Jin Kuang,Yubing Lu,Pingping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HyTBE（Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert）模型，通过目标-背景关系干预扩充可观测的关系模式，利用双曲空间建模目标与背景的相对距离关系，并借助双曲引导的MoE适配器实现多尺度视觉特征的校准与聚合，从而解决红外小目标检测中因目标-背景关系偏移导致的跨域泛化性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IRSTD方法在源域训练后，其决策规则仅建立在有限的源域目标-背景关系模式之上，导致新出现的未见红外域中关系模式与训练时不一致，检测性能显著退化。作者将这种跨域失败形式化为"目标-背景关系偏移"，旨在突破源域关系模式的局限性以提升跨域泛化能力。

Method: HyTBE包含三个关键组件：(1) Target-Background Relation Intervention通过选择性扰动目标或背景来扩充训练期间可观测的关系模式，同时保持有效的监督信号；(2) Hyperbolic Relation Modeling将多尺度视觉线索映射到庞加莱球中，根据每个特征token到目标锚点和背景锚点的相对距离来刻画其目标-背景关系；(3) Hyperbolic-guided MoE Adapter利用双曲关系表示校准多尺度视觉特征，并为不同关系模式聚合专家特定的特征修正。

Result: 在NUAA-SIRST、NUDT-SIRST和IRSTD-1K三个数据集上的留一域（leave-one-domain-out）实验中，HyTBE相比竞争基线取得了更强的跨域泛化性能。

Conclusion: HyTBE通过显式利用关系线索来扩充源域关系模式并自适应调整视觉表示，有效缓解了红外小目标检测中的目标-背景关系偏移问题，验证了双曲空间建模和MoE架构在跨域IRSTD中的有效性。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) has achieved substantial progress under domain-consistent evaluation, yet detector performance often degrades markedly when generalizing to unseen infrared domains. Existing methods primarily improve detection by enhancing target responses and suppressing background interference. However, when trained on only a limited set of source domains, their learned decision rules are inevitably established from a restricted range of source-domain target-background relation patterns. We formulate this cross-domain failure as target-background relation shift: unseen domains may exhibit relation patterns that are not observed during training, thereby weakening the discriminative capability learned from the source domains. To address this problem, we propose HyTBE, a Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert model that expands source-domain relation patterns and adaptively adjusts visual representations using explicit relation cues. The Target-Background Relation Intervention selectively perturbs either targets or backgrounds, broadening the observable relation patterns during training while maintaining valid supervision. Subsequently, the Hyperbolic Relation Modeling maps multi-scale visual cues into a Poincaré ball and characterizes the target-background relation of each feature token according to its relative distances to the target and background anchors. The Hyperbolic-guided MoE Adapter further uses these hyperbolic relation representations to calibrate multi-scale visual features and aggregate expert-specific feature corrections for different relation patterns. Leave-one-domain-out experiments on NUAA-SIRST, NUDT-SIRST, and IRSTD-1K demonstrate that HyTBE achieves stronger cross-domain generalization than competitive baselines.

</details>


### [48] [SR-JEPA: Learning Predictive Latent State in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05774)
*Zihan Zhou,Qifu Wen,Xi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SR-JEPA，一种针对场景级点云的点原生联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），其冻结的预测通路可以在指定位置被查询。当整个物体从场景中移除后，该模型在物体质心处通过32点查询来补全该物体的隐表示，在ARKitScenes物体上达到43.13%的语义身份宏准确率，在Sr3D支撑对上达到41.15 AP，揭示了可查询、可组合的3D预测状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码JEPA主要关注编码器质量，而忽略了预测通路本身的推理能力——当场景中整个实体缺失时，预测通路能推断出什么内容尚不明确。作者旨在探究JEPA预测通路是否能在缺少整个物体的场景中推断出该物体的语义身份等上下文相关的实体内容。

Method: SR-JEPA采用点原生（point-native）设计，直接处理场景级点云。训练过程仅使用自包含的3D EMA（指数移动平均）目标，不依赖重建、语义标签、语言或升维2D特征。在评估时，移除场景中某物体的所有点，并在其质心处放置一个固定的、与形状无关的32点查询，然后查询冻结的预测通路以获得缺失物体的隐表示。

Result: 在5,953个ARKitScenes物体上，补全的隐表示达到43.13%的语义身份宏准确率，比最强基线高出22.18个百分点；随机化预测路径降低9.78个百分点；替换为匹配的供体上下文则降低21.98个百分点。在8,570个Sr3D支撑对上，完整隐表示达到41.15 AP，从缺失物体隐表示解码的身份与锚点身份和几何结合后达到39.37 AP，留下1.78个百分点的未解释残差。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明JEPA的预测通路学习了一个可查询、可组合的3D预测状态：模型能够根据场景上下文补全与上下文相关的实体内容（如语义身份），且下游计算可以将该内容与度量几何相结合。这一发现揭示了3D预测架构中预测通路自身的推理价值。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn by predicting latent representations of missing observations, yet many masked JEPAs are evaluated primarily through the encoders they produce. We ask what a trained predictive pathway itself infers when an entire entity is absent from a native 3D scene. We introduce SR-JEPA, a point-native JEPA for scene-scale point clouds whose original frozen predictive pathway can be queried at a supplied location. At evaluation, every point of one object is removed before encoding and replaced by the same shape-free 32-point query at its centroid. Training uses only self-contained 3D EMA targets: no reconstruction, semantic labels, language, or lifted 2D features. On 5,953 held-out ARKitScenes objects, the imputed latent reaches 43.13% semantic-identity macro accuracy, 22.18 points above the strongest floor. Randomizing the prediction path removes 9.78 points, while substituting matched donor context removes 21.98 points. On 8,570 Sr3D support pairs, the full latent reaches 41.15 AP; identity decoded from the missing-object latent, combined with anchor identity and geometry, reaches 39.37 AP, leaving an unresolved 1.78-point residual. These results reveal a queryable, compositional 3D predictive state: the model completes context-dependent entity content, which downstream computation combines with metric geometry.

</details>


### [49] [Vorch-Director: Interactive World Story Model via Noise-Aware Error Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05776)
*Lisai Zhang,Yidi Wu,Qi Liu,Xin Ma,Yang Ding,Gang Yue,Siqian Yang,Jingyuan Chen,Lin Ma,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vorch-Director，一种面向分钟级音视频自回归生成的噪声层级感知残差校正策略。该方法将每个预测残差关联其来源噪声水平，并在训练时从匹配的噪声区间注入残差，以缓解训练/推理分布不匹配导致的身份漂移、过平滑和音画不同步问题。基于LTX-2扩散Transformer，引入任务嵌入统一条件化历史视频、参考图像和目标视频，支持多镜头、多主体、参考引导的长时音视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归延续是分钟级音视频生成的自然路径，但模型在干净ground-truth历史上训练、在自身生成历史上推理，累积误差导致身份漂移、过平滑和音画不同步。现有方法通过重用预测残差作为合成噪声来缓解不匹配，但残差校正的有效性关键取决于产生残差时的flow-matching噪声水平，这一关键因素此前未被深入探索。

Method: Vorch-Director提出了噪声层级感知的残差校正策略：(1) 将每个残差与产生它的噪声水平关联，训练时从匹配的噪声区间注入残差，使注入误差与去噪过程对齐；(2) 在音视频LTX-2扩散Transformer上引入任务嵌入，区分历史视频、参考图像和目标视频，实现统一条件化；(3) 结合干净条件汇聚（clean conditioning sink）和混合任务训练，支持多镜头、多主体、参考引导的长时音视频生成。

Result: 在ST-Bench上评估Vorch-Director，并提出了一个新的长时程音视频基准，包含质量漂移和长距离一致性指标。大量实验表明，该方法在稳定性和音视频保真度上均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: Vorch-Director通过噪声层级感知的残差注入策略有效缓解了自回归训练与推理之间的分布不匹配问题，在保持高效teacher-forcing训练的同时产生更真实的生成历史，显著提升了长时程音视频生成的质量漂移控制和音画一致性。

Abstract: Autoregressive continuation provides a natural path toward minute-scale audio-visual generation by repeatedly extending a short-window generator conditioned on previously generated video and audio. However, models are trained on clean ground-truth histories, while inference relies on their own generated histories, where accumulated errors cause identity drift, over-smoothing, and audio-visual desynchronization. Recent methods reduce this mismatch by reusing prediction residuals as synthetic corruption, but we observe that the effectiveness of residual correction critically depends on the flow-matching noise level at which residuals are produced. We propose Vorch-Director, a noise-level-aware residual correction strategy that associates each residual with its originating noise level and injects residuals from matched noise regimes during training. By aligning injected errors with the denoising process, Vorch-Director produces more realistic autoregressive histories while retaining efficient teacher-forcing training. Built on the audio-visual LTX-2 diffusion transformer, Vorch-Director further introduces task embeddings to distinguish historical video, reference images, and target video, enabling unified conditioning for long-horizon generation. Together with a clean conditioning sink and mixed-task training, Vorch-Director supports multi-shot, multi-subject, reference-guided audio-visual long-video generation. We evaluate Vorch-Director on ST-Bench and introduce a new long-horizon audio-visual benchmark with metrics for quality drift and long-range consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved stability and audio-visual fidelity over strong baselines.

</details>


### [50] [Evidence-Driven Dynamic Visual Selector for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05780)
*Bo Zhang,Wenxin Wang,Feng Chen,Zhihao Zhang,Zixuan Wang,Changsheng Li,Yinjie Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EviSelect，一种基于目标MLLM内部注意力证据的细粒度动态视觉选择框架，通过稀疏预填充高效估计注意力图作为先验，并利用GRPO优化随机策略实现分布感知的动态采样，在长视频理解基准上减少了约50%的视觉token并实现了3.9倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的长视频理解方法虽通过选择查询相关帧来缓解推理成本和上下文长度问题，但过度依赖外部代理评分器与刚性启发式规则，与目标MLLM的内在证据存在不对齐问题，且无法适应视频中非均匀的时空信息密度分布。

Method: EviSelect首先通过高度压缩的视觉输入和稀疏注意力高效近似目标MLLM的注意力图，作为与完整模型良好对齐的结构化先验；基于此先验推导出三个互补的注意力组件，设计轻量级选择器来定位查询相关时间戳，并自适应调整局部采样率和空间分辨率；将选择器形式化为随机策略，通过GRPO在联合精度-效率奖励下优化，利用组相对比较鼓励策略根据视频信息密度动态分配计算资源。

Result: 在三个长视频理解基准上，EviSelect相比现有方法取得了更优的性能，同时将选中的视觉token减少约50%，并实现了3.9倍的端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: 基于目标MLLM内部注意力证据的动态视觉选择策略能在保持甚至提升理解性能的同时显著降低计算成本，证明了证据驱动的自适应采样是长视频理解中一种有效的范式。

Abstract: Recent advancements in MLLM-based long-form video understanding have mitigated inference-time computational cost and limited context lengths by selecting query-relevant frames. However, existing approaches predominantly rely on external proxy scorers and rigid heuristic rules, inevitably suffering from misalignment with the target MLLM's intrinsic evidence and failing to accommodate the non-uniform spatiotemporal information density. In this paper, we propose a fine-grained dynamic visual selection framework named EviSelect, grounded in the target MLLM internal attention evidence. Our method efficiently probes visual evidence via sparse prefilling as a structured prior to guide distribution-aware dynamic sampling. Specifically, we efficiently approximate attention maps of the target MLLM using highly compressed visual inputs and sparse attention, well-aligned to the full counterpart. Conditioned on three complementary attention components derived from this prior, we design a lightweight selector that not only precisely locates query-relevant timestamps but also adaptively adjusts the local sampling rate and spatial resolution. To enable evidence-conditioned spatiotemporal sampling, we formulate the selector as a stochastic policy and optimize it via GRPO under a joint accuracy--efficiency reward. By rewarding correct predictions under lower visual cost through group-relative comparisons, our method encourages the policy to allocate computation dynamically according to the information density of each video. Across three long video understanding benchmarks, EviSelect achieves superior performance compared to existing methods while reducing selected visual tokens by about 50\% and achieving a 3.9x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [51] [Controllable Clothing: Precise Labels and Generation for Virtual Try-On with Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05834)
*Max Rehman Linder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的虚拟试穿（VITON）图像生成引导方法，利用开源AI模型为服装图像标注长度、风格等标签，通过训练适配器实现用户可控且更贴近真实穿着效果的多样化图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟试穿图像生成缺乏对服装属性的精细化控制，生成的图像可能偏离真实穿着效果，误导消费者；零售端希望生成图像尽可能真实反映服装的实际合身度。

Method: 利用新的开源AI模型对服装图像进行标注（如长度、风格等标签），将标签与服装图像配对，训练适配器（adapters），使生成模型能够接受用户对这些属性的控制，从而生成更多样化且可控的图像。

Result: 提出的方法能够生成更丰富的、可用户控制的图像集，同时保证生成图像更接近真实穿着效果，降低误导消费者的风险。

Conclusion: 该方法为零售端提供了一种确保虚拟试穿图像忠实反映真实合身度的手段，提升了图像生成的可控性与真实性。

Abstract: In this technical report, I present a new method for guiding image generation in the context of Virtual- Try-On (VITON). The proposed method leverages new open source Ai models to augment the image data with labels, such as lengths and styles. By training adapters with these labels paired with images of the garments, the model can produce a more diverse set of images that the user can control. For the end user, such as a retailer, this means that they can assure that the produced image is as true to the true fit as possible, not misleading consumers

</details>


### [52] [Accurate Localization of Road Traffic Objects on the Road Plane Using Surveillance Camera Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05840)
*Jan Gawroński,Witold Czajewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段几何感知的车辆定位管线，从单目路侧监控相机中估计车辆足迹在道路平面上的投影。首先使用YOLO26检测车辆，然后通过ResNet34回归网络预测车辆底座投影的四个角点，最终以预测四边形的几何中心作为车辆位置。在CARLA合成数据上训练并在DAIR-V2X真实数据上微调，在DAIR-V2X数据集上图像空间平均定位误差从31.77像素降至15.30像素（改善51.8%），中位误差降至4.29像素；中等距离车辆的地面平面中位误差从5.52米降至0.90米，远距离车辆从8.67米降至1.84米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常以检测器边界框中心来估计车辆位置，但在相机架设较高（俯视视角）以及车辆体积较大时，由于透视畸变和视差效应，边界框中心会产生较大的定位误差，严重影响智能交通系统、交通监控和冲突分析等应用的精度。

Method: 提出一个两阶段几何感知定位管线：(1) 使用YOLO26检测器检测车辆；(2) 使用专用的ResNet34回归网络预测车辆底座在道路平面上投影的四个角点，最终位置取预测四边形（车辆足迹）的几何中心。训练策略为在CARLA合成数据上预训练，再在DAIR-V2X真实路侧数据上微调。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验均显示相比朴素边界框中心定位有显著改进。DAIR-V2X上图像空间平均误差从31.77px降至15.30px（改善51.8%），中位误差降至4.29px；地面平面中位误差方面，中等距离车辆从5.52m降至0.90m，远距离车辆从8.67m降至1.84m。实验还发现检测边界框周围的上下文信息对几何定位至关重要，远距离车辆和受强透视畸变及视差影响的几何挑战性场景中获益最大。

Conclusion: 以车辆足迹投影（四边形）的几何中心替代边界框中心作为定位依据，能有效缓解透视畸变和视差带来的定位误差，尤其对远距离车辆和几何挑战性场景效果显著。该两阶段管线在合成数据预训练加真实数据微调的框架下具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate vehicle localization from monocular roadside surveillance cameras is important for intelligent transportation systems, traffic monitoring, and traffic conflict analysis. Standard approaches often estimate vehicle position from the center of the detector bounding box, which can produce large errors due to perspective distortion and parallax, especially for elevated cameras and large vehicles.
  This paper proposes a two-stage geometry-aware localization pipeline that estimates the projection of the vehicle footprint onto the road plane. First, vehicles are detected using a YOLO26-based detector. Second, a dedicated ResNet34 regression network predicts four corner points corresponding to the projected vehicle base. The final position is computed as the geometric center of the predicted quadrilateral.
  The method was trained on synthetic data generated in CARLA and fine-tuned on real-world roadside imagery from DAIR-V2X. Experiments on synthetic and real data showed clear improvements over naive bounding-box-center localization. On DAIR-V2X, the mean image-space localization error decreased from 31.77 px to 15.30 px, a 51.8% improvement, while the median error decreased to 4.29 px. Median ground-plane error for medium-range vehicles decreased from 5.52 m to 0.90 m, and for far-range vehicles from 8.67 m to 1.84 m.
  The results also show that contextual information surrounding the detector bounding box is important for geometric localization. The largest gains were observed for distant vehicles and geometrically challenging cases affected by strong perspective distortion and parallax.

</details>


### [53] [Overcoming Attention Drift: Homogeneity-Heterogeneity Guided Feature Aggregation for Low-Light Remote Sensing Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05843)
*Yaozi Zhong,Xingxing Yang,Shaohui Mei,Mingyang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HALO是一个双先验驱动的极端低光遥感图像增强框架，通过光照不变语义先验（区域同质性）和伪3D拓扑先验（边界异质性）的协同引导，并引入同质-异质协同注意力模块（H2CAM）实现特征聚合，在8个合成及真实遥感基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端低光退化下的遥感图像增强对地球观测和下游机器视觉至关重要，但现有方法在严重噪声和光照破坏下存在注意力漂移问题，会错误地在不同物理边界间聚合特征，导致结构模糊和颜色失真。

Method: 提出HALO框架，将增强问题建模为由基础模型先验引导的特征聚合问题。具体包含：(1) 光照不变语义先验提供区域同质性作为内容一致聚合的正向偏置；(2) 伪3D拓扑先验提供边界异质性作为防止跨边界混淆的负向惩罚；(3) 提出同质-异质协同注意力模块（H2CAM）以解决跨模态先验融合中的特征冲突。

Result: 在8个具有挑战性的合成和真实遥感基准上达到最先进性能，显著提升物理边界锐度和颜色保真度，同时最大化保留下游地球观测任务所需的判别性特征。

Conclusion: HALO通过双先验的协同引导有效解决了极端低光环境下注意力漂移导致的结构模糊和颜色失真问题，为遥感图像增强提供了更具鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Restoring high-fidelity remote sensing imagery from extreme low-light degradation is indispensable for reliable Earth observation and downstream machine vision. However, under severe noise and illumination corruption, existing methods suffer from attention drift, erroneously aggregating features across distinct physical boundaries and causing severe structural blurring and color distortion. To address this, we propose HALO, a dual-prior-driven enhancement framework that formulates enhancement as a guided feature aggregation problem driven by foundation model priors. Specifically, an illumination-invariant semantic prior provides regional homogeneity as a positive bias for content-consistent aggregation, while a pseudo-3D topological prior provides boundary heterogeneity as a negative penalty to strictly prevent cross-boundary confusion. To cooperatively incorporate these two priors, we propose a Homogeneity-Heterogeneity Cooperative Attention Module (H2CAM) to resolve feature conflicts during cross-modal prior fusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HALO achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 challenging synthetic and real-world remote sensing benchmarks, significantly improving physical boundary sharpness and color fidelity while maximizing the preservation of discriminative features for downstream Earth observation tasks.

</details>


### [54] [Curia-MAE: Multi-Modal Multi-Anatomy MAE Pre-Training for 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05844)
*Théo Danielou,Antoine Saporta,Léo Alberge,Corentin Dancette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Curia-MAE，一种多模态、多解剖部位的放射学基础模型预训练方法，通过在卷积MAE预训练中扩展稳健重建目标、特征正则化和局部-全局相似性目标，在30万张CT和MRI图像上训练，显著提升冻结编码器在3D分割任务上的性能，在病灶分割任务上超越从头训练的nnU-Net。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学基础模型的密集预测任务（如3D分割）评估不足，且冻结编码器的预训练模型性能仍不及从头训练的nnU-Net这一最先进基线。作者旨在缩小这一差距，提升冻结编码器在分割任务上的表现。

Method: 扩展卷积MAE预训练方法，引入三个目标：稳健重建目标、特征正则化器和局部-全局相似性目标，在30万张CT和MRI图像上预训练多模态、多解剖部位的Curia-MAE模型。

Result: 在8个解剖和病灶分割基准上，Curia-MAE相比强MAE基线显著提升冻结编码器性能；完全微调下保持竞争力，在标注数据稀缺的病灶任务上表现更优。

Conclusion: 单个冻结编码器可复用至多种分割任务，降低临床工作流中部署和适配模型的成本，预训练权重将公开提供。

Abstract: Radiology foundation models learn transferable representations that can be adapted to new tasks by training only small layers on top of a frozen encoder. Dense prediction tasks such as 3D segmentation are, however, underrepresented in their evaluation, and, with the encoder kept frozen, pre-trained models still fall short of nnU-Net, the state-of-the-art reference trained from scratch. To close this gap we extend convolutional MAE pre-training with a robust reconstruction objective, a feature regularizer, and a local-global similarity objective. Using this method, we propose Curia-MAE, a multi-modal, multi-anatomy MAE model pre-trained on 300,000 CT and MRI images covering a large number of anatomical sites. On eight anatomy- and lesion-focused segmentation benchmarks, Curia-MAE improves frozen-encoder performance over a strong MAE baseline, while remaining competitive under full finetuning and superior on lesion tasks, where labeled data is scarce. These results indicate that a single frozen encoder can be reused across diverse segmentation tasks, reducing the cost of adapting and deploying such models in clinical workflows. We will make our pre-trained model weights publicly available.

</details>


### [55] [DTRNet: Dual Text-Radical Decoding for Handwritten Chinese Text Recognition with Faked Character Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05848)
*Runrui Li,Lin Zhu,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DTRNet，一种双文本-部首解码框架，用于K-12场景下的行级伪造汉字检测。该框架将上下文感知的文本识别与逐字结构验证解耦，通过部首分支预测合法字形描述序列（IDS）实现基于词典的伪造字符判断，并引入IDS引导的置信度调整（IGCA）在推理阶段用结构证据精炼文本预测，兼顾行级效率与显式结构证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12教育场景中，手写汉字识别不仅需要转录学生书写，还需检测伪造字符。现有识别模型局限于预定义的正常字符集，无法显式识别伪造字符；而现有检测方法存在互补性局限：字符级方法提供可解释的结构证据但效率低，行级方法高效但依赖置信度分数，容易漏检且缺乏显式结构证据。关键挑战在于保持行级效率的同时，保留不依赖上下文推理的字符结构证据。

Method: 提出DTRNet双文本-部首解码框架，包含两个分支：文本分支执行行级转录，部首分支预测合法的字形描述序列（IDS）以进行基于词典的伪造字符判断。此外引入IDS引导的置信度调整（IGCA）机制，在推理阶段利用结构证据精炼文本预测。

Result: 实验结果表明，DTRNet能有效检测伪造字符，同时保持较强的识别性能，并提供可解释的部首级证据。代码、检查点和已处理数据集已公开发布。

Conclusion: DTRNet通过解耦文本识别与结构验证，成功在行级伪造字符检测中兼顾了效率与可解释性，为K-12手写汉字识别场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In K-12 educational scenarios, handwritten Chinese text recognition should not only transcribe student writing, but also detect faked characters. However, existing recognition models are usually confined to a predefined set of normal characters and therefore cannot explicitly identify faked characters. Existing detection methods exhibit complementary limitations: character-level methods provide interpretable structural evidence but suffer from low efficiency, whereas line-level methods are efficient but rely heavily on confidence scores, making them prone to missed detections and lacking explicit structural evidence. Thus, the key challenge is to preserve character-structural evidence independent of contextual inference while maintaining line-level efficiency. To this end, we propose DTRNet, a dual Text-Radical decoding framework for line-level faked character detection. DTRNet decouples context-aware text recognition from character-wise structural verification, where the text branch performs line-level transcription and the radical branch predicts legal Ideographic Description Sequences (IDS) for lexicon-based faked character judgment. We further introduce IDS-Guided Confidence Adjustment (IGCA) to refine text predictions using structural evidence during inference. Experimental results demonstrate that DTRNet effectively detects faked characters while maintaining strong recognition performance and providing interpretable radical-level evidence. Code, checkpoints, and the processed dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/BNU-ERC-ITEA/DTRNet.

</details>


### [56] [D-CLOT: Double Closed Loop Optimal Transport for Unsupervised Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05877)
*Elena Bueno-Benito,Mariella Dimiccoli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出D-CLOT框架，通过从精炼后的帧嵌入中重新估计动作原型，解决基于最优传输的无监督动作分割中"表示-原型不一致"这一核心问题。引入图约束模块保持局部邻域几何，并在两个实例化变体中分别使用k-means和OT重心更新原型，在五个基准上超越CLOT，并首次在Assembly101上建立无监督动作分割基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输（OT）的无监督动作分割方法中，定义OT成本的潜在动作原型仅通过伪标签损失的梯度进化，而没有从精炼后的帧几何中重新估计，导致"表示-原型不一致"问题。该问题在模糊的过渡区域以及短时或低频动作上尤为突出，成为方法性能的核心瓶颈。

Method: 在CLOT（基于估计的段嵌入精炼帧嵌入）基础上，进一步从精炼帧嵌入中重新估计动作原型。具体包含两个关键模块：(1) 图约束模块，通过保留编码器输出的局部邻域几何来正则化OT精炼后的帧和段表示；(2) 动作嵌入精炼步骤，定期将原型重新锚定到稳定的表示几何上。提出两种实例化：D-CLOT使用k-means更新原型；D-CLOT_B使用以精炼传输计划加权的OT重心更新原型，实现与当前传输几何一致的分配感知更新。

Result: 在五个基准上，两种变体在段级质量上均优于CLOT：YTI上每视频增益最高达+12.7 F1和+10.2 mIoU，FS-Eval上活动级增益最高达+8.9 F1。此外，首次在Assembly101（一个远比已有工作常用基准更精细的程序性基准）上建立了无监督动作分割基线。消融和敏感性分析表明两种精炼机制互补且鲁棒。

Conclusion: 通过从精炼表示中重新估计动作原型，并利用图约束保持局部几何一致性，D-CLOT有效解决了表示-原型不一致问题，显著提升了无监督动作分割性能，尤其在挑战性更高的细粒度程序性基准（Assembly101）上展现出潜力。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) has emerged as an effective framework for unsupervised action segmentation. Yet, in existing OT-based methods, the latent action prototypes that define the OT costs are not re-estimated from the refined frame geometry. Instead, they evolve solely through gradients from the pseudo-label loss. We identify this \emph{representation--prototype inconsistency} as a central bottleneck, particularly around ambiguous transitions and for short or infrequent actions. To address this issue, we build on the recently introduced CLOT, which refines frame embeddings based on estimated segment embeddings, and further re-estimates the action prototypes from the refined frame embeddings. Specifically, we introduce a graph-constrained module that regularizes the OT-refined frame and segment representations by preserving the local neighborhood geometry of the encoder output. An action-embedding refinement step then periodically re-anchors the prototypes to this stabilized representation geometry. We study two instantiations that share the same backbone, graph module, and objective: D-CLOT updates the prototypes using $k$-means, whereas D-CLOT$_{B}$ updates them as OT barycenters weighted by the refined transport plan, yielding an assignment-aware prototype update consistent with the current transport geometry. Across five established benchmarks, both variants improve segment-level quality over CLOT, with per-video gains of up to $+12.7$ F1 and $+10.2$ mIoU (YTI) and activity-level gains of up to $+8.9$ F1 (FS-Eval). We further establish the first unsupervised action-segmentation baseline on Assembly101, a procedural and substantially more fine-grained benchmark than those commonly used in prior work. Extensive ablations and sensitivity analyses demonstrate that the two refinement mechanisms are complementary and robust.

</details>


### [57] [MAVISEG: Manifold Propagation and Visual Prototypes for Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Segmentation in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05878)
*Rajatsubhra Chakraborty,Xujun Che,Ritabrata Chakraborty,Xi Niu,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAVISEG是一个无需训练的细化层，通过恢复扩散Transformer在生成过程中暴露的结构化信号（时间轨迹、概念视觉统计和图像成对特征几何），在不改变底层归因方法的情况下提升零样本开放词汇语义分割性能，在六个基准上均取得最优mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本到图像扩散Transformer的语义分割归因方法逐像素独立打分，仅将像素特征与固定的文本类表示进行比较（无论是输出空间相似度还是交叉注意力权重），忽略了模型自身暴露的结构化信号，包括生成轨迹的时间结构、每个概念的视觉外观统计以及图像自身的成对特征几何。这些被丢弃的信号可能包含关键的概念级信息。

Method: MAVISEG作为一个训练无关的细化层，仅消耗逐像素-概念分数场和像素特征空间。其算子能够恢复三类结构化信号：(1)生成轨迹的时间结构，(2)每个概念的视觉外观统计，(3)图像自身的成对特征几何。由于操作算子不绑定特定归因方法，MAVISEG具有捕获无关性（capture-agnostic），可叠加在任何已有的归因方法之上。

Result: 在六个基准上，MAVISEG在所有训练无关方法中取得最强总体结果，并在每个基准上实现最佳mIoU。值得注意的是，增益在初始捕获最弱时最大，且各个算子根据其细化字段中的噪声水平做出不同贡献。

Conclusion: 扩散Transformer携带的概念级信息比当前归因方法所能恢复的更多，且其中大部分信息是在从模型到掩码的转化过程中丢失的，而非模型本身就缺少这些信息。这为改进无需训练的语义分割方法提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion transformers learn about objects and scenes by learning to generate them, making them strong candidates for training-free zero-shot open-vocabulary semantic segmentation. State-of-the-art attribution methods score each pixel independently, comparing its features against a fixed text-derived class representation, whether as an output-space similarity or as a cross-attention weight. This discards structured signals the model itself exposes: the temporal structure of the generative trajectory, the visual appearance statistics of each concept, and the image's own pairwise feature geometry. We present MAVISEG, a training-free refinement layer that recovers these signals. Because its operators consume only a pixel-by-concept score field and a pixel feature space, MAVISEG is capture-agnostic rather than tied to one attribution method. Across six benchmarks it achieves the strongest overall results among training-free methods, including the best mIoU on every benchmark. Interestingly, gains are largest where the initial capture is weakest, and individual operators contribute depending on the noise in the field they refine. Our results indicate that diffusion transformers carry more concept-level information than current attribution methods recover, and that much of it is lost on the way to the mask rather than absent from the model.

</details>


### [58] [To See a World in a Living Context: Unified Indoor-Outdoor Urban World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05879)
*Xiaobin Huang,Zilong Huang,Yang Luo,Hongchao Fan,Yiping Chen,Ting Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HoloWorld是首个统一室内外场景生成的3D城市场景框架，通过持续更新的跨尺度世界上下文，实现了从城市级到建筑级的连贯生成，确保室内与外部建筑保持显式对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本驱动3D生成方法虽然能独立生成大规模室外环境和详细室内场景，但二者通常是分别合成的，缺乏构建连贯城市世界所需的对应关系。作者希望解决室内外场景生成割裂的问题。

Method: HoloWorld采用逐步表示和更新世界信息的渐进式方法，从城市级规划延伸到个体建筑。其核心机制包括：(1) 基于持续更新的跨尺度世界上下文进行条件生成；(2) 以自回归方式生成城市外部场景，保证跨街区的一致空间组织和视觉身份；(3) 将生成的外部表示锚定到3D建筑实例和足迹上，支持具有几何约束布局和继承外观特征的建筑级室内生成。

Result: HoloWorld在城市场景生成任务上超越了现有最优方法，平均AQS分数提升7.68%，并取得最高平均RDR分数。同时在统一3D城市世界中保持了强建筑级室内外对应关系及跨街区连续性。

Conclusion: HoloWorld首次在统一的3D城市世界中实现室内和室外生成的统一，验证了通过跨尺度世界上下文将不同粒度场景合成结合起来的可行性，为连贯城市级3D内容生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D generation has advanced rapidly in creating large-scale outdoor environments and detailed indoor scenes, but these domains are usually synthesized independently, lacking the correspondence required for a coherent urban world. We present HoloWorld, a unified indoor-outdoor urban world generation framework built on a continuously updated cross-scale world context. Initializing from a user description, HoloWorld progressively represents and updates the diverse world information, from city-scale planning to individual buildings, allowing generated interiors to maintain explicit correspondence with their associated exterior buildings. Conditioned on the evolving context and previously generated neighboring blocks, HoloWorld autoregressively generates urban exteriors with consistent spatial organization and visual identity across blocks. The generated exterior representations are further grounded in 3D building instances and footprints, enabling building-specific indoor generation with geometry-constrained layouts and inherited appearance characteristics. To our knowledge, HoloWorld is the first framework to unify indoor and outdoor generation within a coherent 3D urban world. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloWorld achieves superior urban exterior generation performance, improving the average AQS score over the SOTA by 7.68\% and obtaining the highest average RDR score, while maintaining strong building-level indoor-outdoor correspondence and cross-block continuity within a unified 3D urban world.

</details>


### [59] [Mapping Armenian Paris: Extracting and Geocoding Commercial Advertisements from the 20th-Century Diaspora Press](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05911)
*Chahan Vidal-Gorène,Seda Kirakosyan,Edita Matevosyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于IIIF的端到端流水线，将法国亚美尼亚数字化报刊转化为20世纪巴黎亚美尼亚商业社区交互式地图。该流水线利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）作为数据引导策略，在低资源西亚美尼亚语环境下实现广告的定位、读取、结构化解析与地理编码。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西亚美尼亚语属于低资源语言，现有离线版式识别和OCR模型不支持该语言；传统基于行级CRNN的OCR在强曲率扫描件上表现不可靠，手工标注也无法达到规模化生产结构化数据的需求。

Method: 构建基于IIIF的端到端流水线，包含以下环节：首先利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）在每页上定位并读取商业广告，将其解析为结构化记录；随后对这些记录进行地理编码并放置在交互式地图上。VLM作为数据引导策略，兼顾规模化生产与强曲率扫描场景下的可靠性。同时设计了Label Studio模板，在单次标注过程中同时捕获检测与语义字段。

Result: 产出包括：一个包含500页西亚美尼亚语报刊语料库及3,270条广告级标注；一个可复现的工作流程，可迁移至其他低资源历史语料库；以及交互式地图展示巴黎亚美尼亚商业社区的空间分布。

Conclusion: VLM驱动的数据引导策略是处理低资源历史语言（如西亚美尼亚语）的有效杠杆，为类似场景下的数字化史料挖掘提供了可借鉴的范式。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end, IIIF-based pipeline that turns the digitised Armenian press of France into an interactive map of the 20th-century Parisian Armenian commercial community. On each page, commercial advertisements are located, read, and parsed into structured records, which are then geocoded and placed on the map. Western Armenian is under-resourced and unsupported by off-the-shelf layout and OCR models, so the pipeline uses vision-language models (VLMs) as a data-bootstrapping strategy: they produce usable structured records at a scale hand annotation could not reach, and stay reliable on the strongly curved scans where conventional line-level CRNN OCR breaks down. The contribution includes a 500-page Western Armenian press corpus with 3,270 advertisement-level annotations, a Label Studio template that captures detection and semantic fields in a single annotation pass, and a reproducible workflow transposable to other under-resourced historical corpora. More broadly, the work shows that VLM-driven data bootstrapping is an effective lever for under-resourced historical languages such as (Western) Armenian.

</details>


### [60] [Floating Radiance Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05920)
*Krzysztof Byrski,Rafał Tobiasz,Grzegorz Wilczyński,Mikołaj Zieliński,Dawid Baran,Dominik Belter,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlaRe（漂浮辐射网络）是一种将显式可光线追踪几何与连续神经辐射函数相结合的神经场景表示方法，通过浮动的平面广义高斯原语携带局部辐射场描述符，支持交互式渲染、递归光线追踪、几何变形、网格提取和外观风格化等操作，在保持神经场表现力的同时提供显式可寻址结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经场景表示方法大多与单一渲染范式紧密耦合，缺乏与传统图形工作流（如光线追踪、几何编辑、网格提取等）的兼容性和集成能力，限制了其通用性。

Method: 场景由浮动的平面广义高斯原语表示，每个原语携带一个紧凑的局部辐射场潜在描述符；一个跨场景共享的轻量级解码器将该描述符、局部表面坐标和视角方向映射为颜色和透明度。这种方法结合了神经场的表现力与显式可寻址结构，支持硬件加速的交互式渲染和递归光线追踪（反射、折射、透明度、阴影），并在描述符空间中支持原语级变形、网格提取和外观风格化。

Result: 在标准重建基准上的实验表明，FlaRe在仅使用紧凑原语集合的情况下实现了具有竞争力的渲染质量，同时支持渲染、光线追踪、几何操作和外观编辑的统一场景建模。

Conclusion: FlaRe建立了一种通用场景表示，将高质量神经渲染、光线追踪、几何操控和外观编辑统一到同一模型中，为神经渲染与常规图形工作流的集成提供了可行的解决方案，并已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in neural scene representations enable photorealistic novel-view synthesis, yet most methods remain tightly coupled to a single rendering paradigm, limiting their versatility and integration with conventional graphics workflows. We introduce Floating Radiance Networks (FlaRe), a neural scene representation combining explicit ray-traceable geometry with continuous neural radiance functions. A scene is represented by floating planar generalized Gaussian primitives, each carrying a compact latent descriptor of a local radiance field. A lightweight decoder shared across the scene maps this descriptor, local surface coordinates, and viewing direction to color and opacity. This formulation preserves the expressiveness of neural fields while providing an explicitly addressable structure that can be efficiently queried and manipulated. Hardware-accelerated primitive intersections enable interactive rendering and recursive ray-tracing, including reflections, refractions, transparency, and shadows. The same representation further supports primitive-level deformation, mesh extraction, and appearance stylization directly in its learned descriptor space. Experiments across standard reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate competitive rendering quality while using a compact set of primitives. Together, these results establish FlaRe as a versatile representation that brings high-fidelity neural rendering, ray-tracing, geometric manipulation, and appearance editing into a unified scene model. Source code is available online. Source code can be found at: https://github.com/KByrski/FlaRe

</details>


### [61] [MirrorNet: Can Medical Image Anonymization Really Protect Patient Identity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05938)
*Attila Simkó*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现去标识化的医学影像（仅移除元数据）仍可通过像素内容识别患者身份。作者使用一对耦合的循环一致变分自编码器，在横断面医学图像与非医学的人脸识别图像之间学习对应关系，能够从医学扫描中恢复患者可辨认的面貌（MAE=0.163），并反向合成扫描图，表明医学影像应作为生物特征数据而非可匿名化记录来治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学影像去标识化仅移除元数据（姓名、日期等），假设这样即匿名化。但除直接含面部结构的扫描外，影像像素本身是否具有病人识别能力鲜被审视。若不证实这一点，患者隐私保护存在重大漏洞。

Method: 使用一对耦合的循环一致变分自编码器（cycle-consistent VAEs），在横截面医学影像与病人身份识别图像之间学习双向的、循环一致的对应映射。从留出测试的扫描图重建出病人可辨认的面部影像，并反过来从面部影像合成医学扫描。

Result: 从留出的扫描图成功恢复病人的可辨认长相（身份区域MAE=0.163），并能反向从非医学图像合成医学扫描图，证明医学影像像素保留了可重建的识别信息。

Conclusion: 去标识化的医学扫描本质上仍是病人的"照片"，具有身份识别能力。因此，医学影像不应被视为可匿名化的普通记录，而应作为生物特征数据进行管理和治理。代码与模型已开源共享以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Medical images are routinely de-identified---names, dates, and other metadata removed---and then shared for research, teaching, and public benchmarks under the assumption that this renders them anonymous. Such de-identification protects the metadata but not the pixels, and---apart from scans that directly contain facial structures---whether the image content itself identifies the patient has received little scrutiny. We investigate this question by learning a cycle-consistent correspondence between a cross-sectional medical image and a non-medical, patient-identifying image, using a pair of coupled, cycle-consistent variational autoencoders. From a held-out scan, the model recovers a recognisable likeness of the patient (identity-region MAE = 0.163); conversely, it synthesises a scan from such an image. These results indicate that a de-identified medical scan remains identifying---it is, in effect, a photograph of the patient---and that imaging data should be governed as biometric data rather than as anonymisable records. To support reproducibility, the code and trained models are shared at https://github.com/attilasimko/public-repository.

</details>


### [62] [Respect Your Zero-Shot Uncertainty: Conservative Calibration for Test-Time-Adapted Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05945)
*Jingyan Jiang,Yaru Sun,Xiao Chen,Jiazhen Huang,Caiting Li,Zhijian He,Yin Chen,Pingting Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出零样本锚定熵校准（ZAEC），一种无需标签的后处理校准方法，用于修复测试时适应（TTA）对视觉语言模型校准质量的破坏。ZAEC通过将零样本熵作为样本级不确定性参考，仅对熵缩减的尖锐化预测进行最小温度缩放，在保持分类精度和类别排名不变的情况下，显著降低期望校准误差（ECE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时适应（TTA）虽能提升视觉语言模型在分布偏移下的识别精度，但往往损害校准质量，使预测置信度不可靠，影响下游决策。现有无标签校准方法要么依赖提示优化，要么依赖仅提供粗粒度预测分布特征的logit范围统计。作者发现TTA存在"预测保持型尖锐化"（prediction-preserving sharpening）失败模式——即使top-1预测及正确性不变，TTA仍会增加置信度并降低熵，导致校准恶化。

Method: 作者首先系统分析了TTA导致校准退化的机制，发现熵降低与ECe增加之间存在关联。基于此，提出Zero-Shot-Anchored Entropy Calibration（ZAEC）：将零样本预测的熵作为样本特定的不确定性参考，通过最小温度缩放选择性地恢复尖锐化预测的零样本熵，同时保持其他预测不变。该方法无需标签校准数据或学习参数，且保持类别排名和分类精度。

Result: 在5种TTA方法和15个数据集上，ZAEC在ViT-B/16上取得了最低的后处理宏平均ECE，并在RN50上表现出一致的校准改进。

Conclusion: ZAEC验证了将零样本熵作为校准参考的有效性，提供了一种简单、无需标签、不损失精度的后处理校准方案，有效解决了TTA带来的校准退化问题。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) can improve the recognition accuracy of vision-language models under distribution shift, but often degrades calibration, making predictive confidence unreliable for downstream decision-making. Many existing label-free calibration approaches are either coupled to prompt optimization or rely on logit-range statistics that provide only a coarse characterization of the predictive distribution. We show that TTA can increase confidence and reduce entropy even when the top-1 prediction and its correctness remain unchanged, a failure mode we term prediction-preserving sharpening. Across diverse TTA methods and benchmarks, larger entropy reductions relative to paired zero-shot predictions are associated with greater increases in Expected Calibration Error (ECE). On entropy-reduced samples, confidence gains also tend to exceed accuracy gains. Based on these findings, we propose Zero-Shot-Anchored Entropy Calibration (ZAEC), a label-free post-hoc method that uses zero-shot entropy as a sample-specific uncertainty reference. ZAEC selectively restores the zero-shot entropy of sharpened predictions through minimal temperature scaling while leaving all other predictions unchanged. It requires no labeled calibration data or learned parameters and preserves class rankings and classification accuracy. Across five TTA methods and 15 datasets, ZAEC achieves the lowest post-hoc macro-average ECE on ViT-B/16, with consistent gains on RN50.

</details>


### [63] [Big, Bright, or Invisible: A Frozen-Feature Benchmark of 3D CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05960)
*Maulik Chevli,Johannes Brandt,Rickmer Braren,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统地评测了十种冻结的3D CT基础模型编码器，在三个胸廓CT数据集（含一个未见过的内部临床数据集）上通过k近邻、零样本提示和线性探测三种方式评估其诊断广度。研究发现不存在通用的最优编码器，模型排名随评估场景剧烈波动；细粒度图像令牌化结合视觉-语言对齐的模型通常表现最佳，但轻量级监督编码器仍然极具竞争力。更关键的发现是，决定性能的首要因素不是模型架构，而是物理瓶颈——病灶的可检测性与其组织对比度和空间范围成正比。广泛或高对比度异常（如设备、积液）能可靠检出，但小尺寸、低对比度的局灶性病变对所有编码器仍是持续挑战，这归因于全局池化嵌入的固有限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规CT解读具有全面的特性，会捕捉整个扫描体积中的偶然发现。3D CT基础模型有望通过提供解剖和病理的通用表征来辅助这一过程。为了评估这些模型的诊断广度，作者对多种CT编码器进行了系统性的基准测试，以探讨它们在不同诊断场景下的表现能力。

Method: 作者在三个胸廓CT扫描队列上对十个冻结的CT编码器进行基准测试，包括一个未见过的内部临床数据集。评测采用三种方法：k近邻（k-NN）、零样本提示和线性探测（linear probing），以全面衡量编码器的表征能力。此外，通过受控的器官内对比分析，实证考察了病灶的对比度和空间范围对可检测性的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1）不存在通用的最优编码器，排名随评估场景显著变化；2）结合细粒度图像令牌化与视觉-语言对齐的模型通常表现最佳，但轻量级监督编码器也能以显式标签替代数据规模获得竞争力；3）性能的首要决定因素是物理瓶颈——可检测性随病灶对比度和空间范围增大而提升；4）广泛或高对比度异常（设备、积液）可被可靠恢复，而小尺寸低对比度的局灶性病变对所有编码器都是持久挑战。

Conclusion: 作者认为，全局池化嵌入的内在局限性导致小尺寸、低对比度结构的精确表征难以实现，因此未来需要区域级或病灶级的预训练策略来克服这一瓶颈。该工作为CT基础模型的设计方向提供了重要指导：不应仅追求架构或规模的提升，而需关注表征粒度与病灶定位能力。

Abstract: Routine CT interpretation is inherently comprehensive, capturing incidental findings across the entire scan volume. 3D CT foundation models could assist this process by providing generalizable representations of anatomy and pathology. To evaluate their diagnostic breadth, we benchmark ten frozen CT encoders across three cohorts of thoracic CT scans, including an unseen internal clinical dataset, using $k$-nearest neighbors, zero-shot prompting, and linear probing. We find no universal state-of-the-art, with rankings fluctuating significantly depending on the evaluation context. While models combining fine-grained image tokenization with vision-language alignment generally perform best, a lightweight supervised encoder remains highly competitive, demonstrating that explicit labels can effectively substitute for scale. Crucially, rather than model architecture, we observe that the primary determinant of performance is a physical bottleneck: a finding's detectability scales with its contrast against surrounding tissue and its spatial extent. Through controlled within-organ comparisons, we empirically demonstrate that widespread or high-contrast abnormalities, such as devices and effusions, are reliably recovered. Conversely, small, low-contrast focal lesions remain a persistent challenge across all evaluated encoders. We attribute this to the inherent limitations of globally pooled embeddings, suggesting that accurately representing small, low-contrast structures will require region- or lesion-level pretraining.

</details>


### [64] [Topology-Aware Neighborhood Learning for Source-Free Cross-Scene Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05964)
*Qingmei Li,Juepeng Zheng,Jiarui Zhang,Jianxi Huang,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种拓扑感知的无源域学习框架（Topology-Aware Source-Free Learning），用于跨场景高光谱图像分类。该方法通过熵动量伪标签（EMP）精化k-means聚类分配，并结合上下文邻域拓扑（CNT）挖掘目标域特征空间的流形级几何结构，在无源数据条件下实现接近甚至超越有源域自适应方法的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统跨场景高光谱图像分类依赖域自适应方法，但这些方法需要访问源域数据。然而在实际遥感场景中，隐私规则或存储限制常常使得源域数据不可用，导致传统域自适应方法失效。因此亟需一种不依赖源数据的源-free学习框架来解决跨场景高光谱图像分类问题。

Method: 提出拓扑感知的源-free学习框架，包含两大核心模块：(1) 熵动量伪标签（EMP），通过熵感知置信度和时间预测动量来精化k-means聚类分配；(2) 上下文邻域拓扑（CNT），结合协作表示提取的全局结构信息与近邻搜索建模的局部相似性信息，实现对目标域特征空间流形级几何属性的完整编码。总目标函数包含精化伪标签上的交叉熵、基于log内积的拓扑一致性约束以及用于平衡分类的信息最大化项。

Result: 在三个典型跨场景高光谱图像数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在性能上超过了现有的最先进方法；消融研究进一步验证了每个模块的贡献，证实了拓扑感知建模在无源数据情况下实现稳健准确分类中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知建模在无源域数据条件下对高光谱图像分类至关重要，所提出的源-free学习框架能够有效地利用目标域自身的结构信息，实现稳健且准确的分类，为隐私受限或存储受限的真实遥感场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Domain adaptation has advanced cross-scene hyperspectral image classification, significantly improving discriminative capability in complex scenarios. However, privacy rules or storage limits often block access to data from the source domain. Conventional domain adaptation methods become impractical, severely restricting their utility in realistic remote sensing scenarios. To tackle this challenge, we propose a topology-aware source-free learning framework. We first introduce the entropy momentum pseudo-labeling (EMP) to refine k-means assignments by leveraging entropy-aware confidence and temporal prediction momentum. Under the guidance of the refined pseudo-labels, we further utilize the contextual neighborhood topology (CNT) to exploit the intrinsic geometric structure of the target feature space. Combining the global structural information extracted by collaborative representation with the local similarity information modeled by nearest neighbor search, the CNT accomplishes the comprehensive encoding of manifold-level geometric properties in the target domain feature space. The overall objective integrates cross-entropy on refined pseudo-labels, log inner product-based topology consistency, and an information-maximization term for balanced classification, ensuring stable adaptation in the source-free setting. Extensive experiments on three typical cross-scenarios demonstrate that the proposed method exceeds state-of-the-art performance, and ablation studies further validate the contribution of each module. The results highlight the critical role of topology-aware modeling in achieving robust and accurate classification without source data.

</details>


### [65] [Diff-VF: Training-free High-quality Long Video Generation via Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05976)
*Haoning Yang,Xinyuan Chen,Yaohui Wang,Guo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Diff-VF是一个无需训练、即插即用的模型无关框架，通过三种互补策略（混合噪声初始化、加权窗口采样、时间扩展采样）将现有短视频扩散模型扩展为长视频生成器，并在长视频增强中引入跳跃残差引导，实现了时间连贯性与运动多样性之间更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型大多在短视频上训练，外推至长视频时会退化，难以在保持长时间时间连贯性的同时保留多样运动。需要一个无需修改或微调基础模型的解决方案来生成一致、高质量且动态的长视频。

Method: Diff-VF包含四个核心组件：1) 混合噪声初始化（HNI）约束全局语义；2) 加权窗口采样（WWS）消除窗口间的不连续；3) 时间扩展采样（TES）通过时间步变化的融合建立长距离依赖；4) 跳跃残差引导（Skip Residual Guidance）通过时间步依赖的引导在保真度与真实感之间取得平衡，用于长视频增强。

Result: 在VBench-Long评估中，Diff-VF相比基础模型及FreeNoise、FreeLong、RIFLEx等最近的无训练长视频生成基线，在时间连贯性与运动多样性之间取得了更优的平衡，同时保持了具有竞争力的逐帧质量。在两个基础模型上的实验验证了其对不同时空建模策略的适用性。

Conclusion: Diff-VF通过无需训练的组合式策略有效解决了短视频扩散模型外推到长视频时的连贯性与多样性平衡问题，且具有模型无关性和即插即用性，大量消融实验验证了各组件及超参数的贡献。

Abstract: Recently, diffusion models have made great progress in video generation. However, most existing video diffusion models are trained with short videos, and degrade when extrapolated to long videos, struggling to maintain long-range temporal coherence while retaining diverse motions. To generate consistent, high-quality and dynamic long videos, we propose Diff-VF, a training-free, plug-and-play and model-agnostic framework that converts existing short-video diffusion backbones into long-video generators without modifying or fine-tuning the base model. Diff-VF couples three complementary strategies: Hybrid Noise Initialization (HNI) to constrain global semantics, Weighted Window Sampling (WWS) to remove inter-window discontinuities, and Temporal Extended Sampling (TES) to establish long-range dependencies with a timestep-varying fusion. We further extend Diff-VF to long-video enhancement via Skip Residual Guidance that balances fidelity and realism through timestep-dependent guidance. VBench-Long evaluation results show that Diff-VF achieves a more favorable balance between temporal coherence and motion diversity than base models and recent training-free long video generation baselines, including FreeNoise, FreeLong, and RIFLEx, while maintaining competitive frame-wise quality. Experiments on two base models demonstrate the applicability to video diffusion models with different spatial-temporal modeling strategies. Extensive ablations validate the contribution of each component and hyperparameters.

</details>


### [66] [Multi-Year Geospatial Reasoning using Interannually-Consistent Historical Predictions as a Free Input Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05979)
*Syed Roshaan Ali Shah,Kasper Bonte,David Bekaert,Kristof Van Tricht,Dieter Wens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将历史预测档案和外部辅助图层（如BVL掩码）引入深度学习模型内部的方案，使年度土地覆盖/作物类型分类器具备跨年推理能力。在泛欧洲约5.4M标注像素数据集上，该方法将作物类别F1提升1.6个百分点，尤其对多年生和木本作物收益显著，并纠正了模型偏向高召回的错误分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每年运行的业务化机器学习系统（如土地覆盖和作物类型分类）会在多年运行中积累两种有价值但极少被回馈给模型的信号：系统自身的历史预测档案，以及处理联盟中其他伙伴提供的辅助图层。传统做法将这些信号仅用于规则后处理或固定输入掩码，未充分利用其跨年一致性和上下文信息。

Method: 以Copernicus土地监测服务高分辨率层（HRL）Croplands作物类型产品为测试平台，将历史预测和外部辅助图层（BVL掩码）引入模型内部。具体包括：(1) 提出一种CTY嵌入编码器，将每次历史预测表示为置信度加权、按时间排序的分类token，并沿年份轴进行注意力操作；(2) 研究BVL掩码在模型输入和输出中应如何表示；(3) 在评估时仅针对18个作物类别分别报告精确率和召回率，以公平比较不同设计在处理非作物像元重标注时的表现。

Result: 在约5.4M标注像素的泛欧洲数据集上，加入预测历史使作物类别F1提升1.6个百分点，并纠正了偏向高召回的错误分布，其中最大收益出现在多年生和木本作物上（橄榄+4.6、水果+3.7、坚果+3.2个百分点）。在历史年份和目标年份中一致地表示BVL掩码，可为作物类别额外增加约2.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种低成本、可复用的通用方案，适用于任何周期性运行、输出类别图的地理空间或基础模型。将历史预测和外部辅助图层纳入模型内部而非仅作外部后处理，能显著提升分类器跨年推理的能力和类别间的错误分布平衡性。

Abstract: Machine learning, and deep networks in particular, are increasingly used to derive higher-level Earth observation (EO) products such as annual land-cover and crop-type maps. Many are generated operationally: each year a new acquisition is processed, typically with the same model, extending a multi-year archive. In the process these systems accumulate two kinds of useful signal that are almost never fed back into the model: the system's own archive of past predictions, and ancillary layers produced by other partners in a processing consortium. Both are normally used outside the network, as rule-based post-processing or a fixed input mask. Using the Copernicus Land Monitoring Service High Resolution Layer (HRL) Croplands crop-type product as a testbed, we show that bringing both signals inside the model turns a single-year, single-task pixel classifier into one that reasons across years. We introduce a Crop Type (CTY) embedding encoder that represents each past prediction as a confidence-scaled, time-ordered categorical token and attends over the year axis, and we study how the externally provided Base Vegetation Layer (BVL) mask should be represented in the model's inputs and outputs. To compare designs fairly when they relabel non-crop pixels, we evaluate on the 18 crop classes only and report precision and recall separately. On a pan-European dataset of about 5.4M labelled pixels, adding the prediction history raises crop-only F1 by 1.6 percentage points (pp) and, more importantly, corrects a recall-skewed error profile, with the largest gains on perennial and tree crops (olives +4.6, fruits +3.7, nuts +3.2 pp). Representing the BVL mask consistently in both the history and the target year adds about 2.5 pp on the crop classes. The approach is a low-cost recipe for any recurring geospatial or foundation model that emits class maps.

</details>


### [67] [Universal Concept Disruption for SAM3 Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05983)
*Hao Wang,Yuxuan Zhang,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通用概念破坏（UCD）——首个针对SAM3图像分割的跨概念通用对抗攻击方法。UCD从(图像,名词短语)对中学习单一有界扰动，联合破坏文本条件输入路径、最大化提示共享视觉特征差异、抑制存在门控概念分数，并通过面积坍缩和干净掩码Dice破坏腐蚀保留掩码的空间有效性。在多个数据集上显著降低分割性能（平均mask AP从59.43降至18.73），且扰动可迁移至SAM3.1和SAM3视频推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3将可提示分割从几何驱动的掩码预测扩展为开放词汇概念分割，采用文本条件定位模型判断概念是否存在并分割所有匹配实例。这种存在门控设计提升了概念级预测能力，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究SAM3在对抗攻击下的脆弱性。

Method: UCD方法包含四个联合攻击组件：（1）破坏文本条件输入路径；（2）最大化提示共享视觉特征的发散；（3）抑制最终存在门控概念分数；（4）通过面积坍缩和干净掩码Dice破坏来腐蚀保留掩码的空间有效性。从(图像,名词短语)对中学习单一有界图像扰动，将SAM3作为集成概念定位系统进行端到端攻击。

Result: 在SACo-Gold、LVIS、RefCOCO、PhraseCut和OpenImages五个数据集上，UCD在匹配评估协议下一致优于所有基线，将平均mask AP从59.43降至18.73，平均cgF1从50.32降至20.49。学到的扰动无需重新优化即可迁移到SAM3.1和SAM3视频推理。提示集成、轻量级头微调和时间滤波只能提供有限的恢复能力。

Conclusion: UCD揭示了SAM3概念分割系统在面对通用跨概念对抗攻击时的严重脆弱性，且现有防御手段（提示集成、微调、时间滤波）恢复效果有限，这对开放词汇概念分割模型的部署安全性提出了重要警示。

Abstract: SAM3 extends promptable segmentation from geometry-driven mask prediction to open-vocabulary concept segmentation, where a text-conditioned grounding model decides whether a concept is present and segments all matching instances. While this presence-gated design improves concept-level prediction, its adversarial robustness remains unexplored. In this paper, we introduce Universal Concept Disruption (UCD), the first universal cross-concept adversarial attack tailored to SAM3 image segmentation. UCD learns a single bounded image perturbation from (image, noun-phrase) pairs and attacks SAM3 as an integrated concept-grounding system. It jointly disrupts the text-conditioned input path, maximizes divergence in prompt-shared visual features, suppresses the final presence-gated concept scores, and corrupts the spatial validity of retained masks through area collapse and clean-mask Dice disruption. Across SACo-Gold, LVIS, RefCOCO, PhraseCut, and OpenImages datasets, UCD consistently outperforms all baselines under a matched evaluation protocol, reducing average mask AP from 59.43 to 18.73 and average cgF1 from 50.32 to 20.49. The learned perturbation also transfers to SAM3.1 and to SAM3 video inference without re-optimization, while prompt ensembling, lightweight head fine-tuning, and temporal filtering provide limited recovery.

</details>


### [68] [Wan-Animate-2: Pushing the Application Boundaries of Character Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06009)
*Guangyuan Wang,Li Hu,Dechao Meng,Zhongyi Zhang,Peng Zhang,Mingyang Huang,Ruoshi Zhang,Ke Sun,Zhe Zhang,Xingjun Wang,Gang Cheng,Bang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Wan-Animate-2，一个端到端角色动画框架，直接消费驱动视频于重新设计的Diffusion Transformer中，消除了中间运动提取器。同时提出高效变体Wan-Animate-2-Lite，通过三阶段训练范式（teacher forcing预训练、错误缓冲区机制、Self-Forcing蒸馏与分块反向传播）将推理延迟降至实时阈值，支持流式角色动画生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色动画方法存在三类局限：显式运动表示方法面临提取误差和身份漂移；隐式运动特征方法通过压缩丢失细粒度动态；上下文学习方法虽避免了中间表示但计算成本过高。此外，所有现有系统均面向离线合成，无法满足数字头像、直播等交互应用对实时性的需求。

Method: Wan-Animate-2采用重新设计的Diffusion Transformer架构，直接消费驱动视频，彻底消除中间运动提取器以实现更高的运动保真度和身份保持。引入文本驱动的视点控制，将输出相机视角与驱动视频解耦。Wan-Animate-2-Lite通过三阶段训练范式实现实时推理：teacher forcing预训练结合错误缓冲区机制，以及Self-Forcing蒸馏配合分块反向传播。

Result: 定性评估和用户研究表明Wan-Animate-2在多样角色和运动模式上实现了高保真动画效果。Wan-Animate-2-Lite将推理延迟降低至实时阈值，支持流式角色动画。作者还将公开Wan-Animate-2-Base模型权重以促进社区发展。

Conclusion: Wan-Animate-2通过端到端架构和实时推理方案，克服了现有方法的运动提取误差与离线限制，为数字虚拟人和直播等交互应用打开了新的部署场景。
