Abstract: Character image animation remains a foundational yet challenging task in computer vision. Existing approaches can be broadly categorized into three paradigms: methods based on explicit motion representations suffer from extraction errors and identity drift; methods based on implicit motion features lose fine-grained dynamics through compression; and in-context learning approaches avoid intermediate representations but incur prohibitive computational costs. Furthermore, all current systems are designed for offline synthesis, unable to meet the real-time requirements of interactive applications such as digital avatars and live-streaming hosts. To address these limitations, we present Wan-Animate-2, an end-to-end character animation framework that directly consumes the driving video within a redesigned Diffusion Transformer. Our architecture achieves superior motion fidelity and identity preservation by eliminating intermediate motion extractors entirely. We further introduce text driven viewpoint control that decouples the output camera perspective from the driving video--a capability rarely supported by prior character animation methods that rely on explicit motion representations. Beyond generation quality, we present Wan-Animate-2-Lite, an efficient variant that reduces inference latency to real-time thresholds through a three-stage training paradigm: teacher forcing pretraining with error buffer mechanism, and Self-Forcing distillation with chunk-wise backpropagation. This enables streaming character animation for interactive applications, opening new deployment scenarios that were previously infeasible. Qualitative evaluations and user studies demonstrate that Wan-Animate-2 achieves high-fidelity animation results across diverse characters and motion patterns. To foster further research and community development, we will release the Wan-Animate-2-Base model weights to the public.

</details>


### [69] [Iterate or Widen? When Test-Time Refinement Helps LiDAR Scene Completion: A Controlled Study of Evidence Geometry, Training Coverage, and Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06014)
*Shijie Hao,Weining Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过严格的对照实验，在LiDAR语义场景补全任务中系统比较了迭代式补全模型与更宽的单次预测模型，发现收益并非普适：连续角度移除场景下迭代带来显著增益（+0.911 mIoU），而独立稀疏化场景下训练数据增强效果远优于迭代（+5.975 vs +0.300）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在场景补全任务中，选择迭代式补全模型（测试时额外计算）还是更宽的单次预测器（更多参数）这一问题常被数据增强、去噪课程、容量差异和非配对评估等因素混淆。作者旨在通过严格解耦协议，厘清迭代与宽模型之间的真实性能边界。

Method: 采用严格的对照协议：比较单次预测器、参数匹配的更宽预测器、以及权重共享的多网格细化器（从同一冻结预测器初始化）。数据增强区分了连贯区域移除、独立稀疏化、距离依赖衰减和加性杂波四类退化，同时保持精确的场景-条件配对。使用5个训练种子和815帧SemanticKITTI序列08进行验证，采用移动块自助法计算95%置信区间。

Result: 在连续角度移除下，完整迭代系统比宽对照组在mIoU上提升0.911点（95% CI: [0.804, 1.040]），超过预设0.5点实际阈值。在独立75%稀疏化下，迭代仅增加0.300点（[0.166, 0.436]），而观测族数据增强增加5.975点（[5.662, 6.140]）。两种干预均无法修复加性杂波。计算成本方面，迭代系统每帧10.74ms/0.75GiB，宽对照为6.25ms/0.23GiB。

Conclusion: 迭代补全的收益是几何条件依赖的而非普适的：连贯性间隙可证明固定深度细化的合理性，而广泛稀疏化的证据更适合通过训练覆盖来解决，杂波证据则需要不同的鲁棒性机制。该结果确立了经验边界而非通用优势。

Abstract: Should a completion model spend extra test-time compute by iterating, or spend a similar parameter budget on a wider one-shot predictor? The answer is easily confounded by denoising curricula, corruption augmentation, capacity, and unpaired evaluation. We study this question in LiDAR semantic scene completion by comparing a one-shot predictor, a parameter-matched wider predictor, and a weight-tied multigrid refiner initialized from the same frozen predictor. The protocol separates coherent region removal, independent thinning, range-dependent attenuation, and additive clutter while preserving exact scene-condition pairing. Across five training seeds and 815 SemanticKITTI sequence-08 frames, the full iterative system improves mIoU over the wide control by 0.911 points under contiguous angular removal, with a 95% moving-block bootstrap interval of [0.804, 1.040] that clears a predeclared 0.5-point practical margin. Under independent 75% thinning, iteration adds only 0.300 points [0.166, 0.436], whereas observation-family augmentation adds 5.975 points [5.662, 6.140]. Neither intervention repairs additive clutter. The iterative system also costs 10.74 ms and 0.75 GiB per frame, versus 6.25 ms and 0.23 GiB for the wide control. These results establish a geometry-conditioned empirical boundary rather than a universal advantage: coherent gaps can justify fixed-depth refinement, broadly thinned evidence is addressed more effectively by training coverage, and spurious evidence requires a different robustness mechanism.

</details>


### [70] [PaCoNet: Deep Data Extraction for Parallel Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06030)
*Poonam Poonam,Hannah Kniesel,Pere-Pau Vázquez,Timo Ropinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PaCoNet，这是首个用于平行坐标图数据自动提取的深度学习方法。它不仅能提取线条坐标，还能提取单个数据样本，并引入了大规模平行坐标数据集，为平行坐标可视化分析与重设计奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从可视化中提取数据一直是计算机视觉领域的长期挑战，现有研究主要集中在柱状图、折线图和饼图等低维可视化上。然而，平行坐标作为一种广泛使用的高维数据可视化方法，在该领域几乎未被探索。由于平行坐标图在设计不佳或数据密集时容易变得杂乱难以解读，因此自动数据提取具有特别重要的研究价值。

Method: PaCoNet包含三个核心贡献：(1) 提出了首个专门针对平行坐标分析的深度学习架构，能够提取线条坐标并进一步提取单个数据样本；(2) 引入了一个大规模平行坐标数据集用于训练和测试；(3) 通过对比实验证明该方法显著优于未适配的基线方法。

Result: 实验表明PaCoNet在平行坐标数据提取任务上显著优于未适配的基线方法，实现了线条坐标和单个数据样本的自动提取，首次实现了平行坐标图的自动化分析与重设计。

Conclusion: PaCoNet填补了平行坐标图数据自动提取这一研究空白，为复杂的可视化分析奠定了基础，推动了计算机视觉与数据可视化两个领域的交叉发展。所有代码、训练模型和数据生成脚本将在论文接受后公开。

Abstract: Extracting data from visualizations has long challenged computer vision, with current research focused on bar, line, and pie charts, among other low-dimensional visualizations. However, parallel coordinates as a widely used high-dimensional data visualization approach, remain largely unexplored in this context. As parallel coordinate plots can quickly become cluttered and difficult to interpret when poorly designed or densely populated, automated data extraction from such visualizations is of particular interest. In this paper, we propose PaCoNet, the first approach for parallel coordinate data extraction. PaCoNet not only extracts line coordinates, but also enables the extraction of individual data samples for further analysis. Towards this end, we make the following contributions. We present the first deep learning approach tailored for parallel coordinate analysis, and demonstrate that it outperforms unadapted baselines by a significant margin. We further introduce a large-scale parallel coordinate dataset for training and testing. Together, these key contributions enable for the first time the automated analysis and redesign of parallel coordinate plots. PaCoNet thus lays the groundwork for complex visualization analysis, and further advances the intersection of computer vision and data visualization. All code, trained models, and data generation scripts will be made publicly available upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [71] [DARAD: Dual Adapters and Ranking-Aware Distillation for Continual Remote Sensing Image-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06059)
*Xi Chen,Xu Chen,Xiangyang Jia,Wei Wang,Xu Zhang,Zhenyuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DARAD是一种双适配器和排序感知蒸馏框架，用于遥感图像-文本检索中的持续学习场景。它通过空间融合适配器、多专家语义路由和双向排序蒸馏，同时适应新数据并保持历史跨模态检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像文本检索（RS-ITR）在持续学习场景下面临挑战：遥感数据的尺度变化和分布偏移会加剧跨模态对齐空间的失真，导致现有持续学习方法难以支持可靠的持续检索。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 视觉分支采用空间融合适配器，整合粗粒度区域线索和细粒度补丁线索以应对遥感尺度变化，同时将视觉更新锚定在预训练对齐空间中；(2) 文本分支采用多专家语义路由，将共享语义与专用残差分离，吸收新兴描述同时约束全局文本嵌入漂移；(3) 双向排序蒸馏利用冻结教师模型和历史锚点保持历史跨模态排序结构，缓解跨持续阶段的对齐空间失真。

Result: 在多阶段持续检索协议下，DARAD相比现有持续学习方法取得了更优性能，在适应新到达数据的同时保持了对历史数据的有效性。

Conclusion: DARAD通过双适配器和排序感知蒸馏机制有效解决了遥感持续检索中跨模态对齐空间失真的问题，验证了在持续遥感图像文本检索任务中的优越性。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of Earth observation technologies, remote sensing archives are rapidly expanding, making remote sensing image-text retrieval (RS-ITR) increasingly important. However, continual RS-ITR remains challenging because scale variation and distribution shifts in RS aggravate cross-modal alignment space distortion, making it difficult for existing continual learning (CL) methods to support reliable continual retrieval. To address this challenge, we propose DARAD, a dual-adapter and ranking-aware distillation framework that preserves the historical cross-modal ranking structure while learning new visual and textual concepts from evolving archives. Specifically, the visual branch introduces a spatial fusion adapter, which integrates coarse regional cues and fine-grained patch cues to accommodate RS scale variation while anchoring visual updates to the pretrained alignment space. The textual branch employs multi-expert semantic routing, which separates shared textual semantics from semantically specialized residuals to absorb newly emerging descriptions while constraining global text embedding drift. Furthermore, bidirectional ranking distillation uses a frozen teacher model and historical anchors to preserve the historical cross-modal ranking structure, thereby mitigating alignment space distortion across continual stages. Experiments under a multi-stage continual retrieval protocol show that DARAD achieves superior performance over existing CL methods, improving adaptation to newly arrived data while maintaining effectiveness on historical data.

</details>


### [72] [Learning from Failures: Retrieval-Centric CoT via Hard Negatives for Unified Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06060)
*Zelong Sun,Jun Wang,Kaicheng Yang,Tiancheng Gu,Ziyong Feng,Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniME-R1提出了一种嵌入器-顾问（embedder-adviser）框架，通过生成以检索为中心的思维链（RC-CoT）来改进统一多模态检索。该框架让顾问基于初始检索结果分析嵌入器混淆的判别性线索，若目标在top-k集合中则直接重排，否则生成RC-CoT调整检索方向并进行全库重新检索。实验在MMEB-V2及多个通用多模态检索基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LVLM的多模态检索器虽然高效可扩展，但直接编码原始多模态输入往往忽略细粒度判别性线索，导致对语义相似候选的混淆。已有的CoT推理方法仅基于查询本身生成，解释的是"查询描述了什么"，而非"检索器误解了什么"，缺乏检索反馈的指导。

Method: 提出嵌入器-顾问框架UniME-R1：顾问逐样本分析初始检索结果以识别嵌入器混淆的判别性线索；若目标在初始top-k中则直接重排，否则生成RC-CoT（检索中心思维链）修正检索方向，并通过双模式嵌入器进行全库重新检索。训练中通过挖掘硬负例模拟检索失败场景，联合优化直接检索和RC-CoT增强检索，并通过监督学习和面向检索目标的强化学习对齐顾问。

Result: 在MMEB-V2和多样化的通用多模态检索基准上，UniME-R1相较于强基线持续提升了检索性能。

Conclusion: 检索推理应基于检索反馈进行条件化而非仅依赖查询本身；通过让推理过程针对嵌入器的具体误解进行优化，可以显著提升统一多模态检索系统的效果。

Abstract: Unified multimodal retrieval aims to identify candidates that satisfy complex user intent expressed through heterogeneous inputs. Although Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM)-based retrievers are efficient and scalable, directly encoding raw multimodal inputs often misses fine-grained discriminative cues, leading to confusion among semantically similar candidates. Recent methods mitigate this limitation by generating Chain-of-Thought (CoT) rationales to enrich the query representation. However, such reasoning is typically derived from the query alone: it explains what the query describes, but not what the retriever misunderstands. We argue that effective retrieval reasoning should instead be conditioned on retrieval feedback. Based on this insight, we introduce UniME-R1, an embedder-adviser framework that learns to reason over initially retrieved candidates and generate Retrieval-Centric Chain-of-Thought (RC-CoT). The adviser analyzes candidates individually to identify the discriminative cues confused by the embedder. If the target appears in the initial top-k set, UniME-R1 directly reranks the candidates; otherwise, it generates RC-CoT to refine the retrieval direction and performs full-corpus re-retrieval with a dual-mode embedder. To train the framework, we mine hard negatives to simulate realistic retrieval failures, jointly optimize direct retrieval and RC-CoT-augmented retrieval, and align the adviser with retrieval outcomes through supervised learning and retrieval-oriented reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments on MMEB-V2 and a diverse set of general multimodal retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that UniME-R1 consistently improves retrieval performance over strong baselines.

</details>


### [73] [Bar-JEPA: Extracting Values from Bar Chart with Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06062)
*Poonam Poonam,Alexander Epple,Timo Ropinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）的柱状图数值恢复管线，通过自监督方式学习高语义质量的潜在特征，由一个简单快速的解码器输出刻度与柱子的坐标，从而恢复每根柱子的数值，并在与端到端监督基线的对比中展示了自监督微调的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柱状图虽易被人类理解，但计算化地提取底层数据并不容易。基于机器学习的方法通常需要标注的真实世界数据，而标注过程耗时且标注数据稀缺，这限制了模型的训练效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出一个针对柱状图的逐柱数值恢复管线：使用JEPA编码器通过自监督方式产生语义丰富的潜在特征，解码器模型仅需简单快速训练即可输出刻度与柱子的坐标，并利用这些坐标恢复柱子的数值。模型包含自监督微调环节，以提升特征质量。

Result: 通过与端到端监督基线模型进行比较，证明了自监督微调的有效性以及提取特征的语义质量优势。代码、数据集和检查点已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: 自监督的JEPA特征提取方法能够有效支持柱状图数值恢复任务，相比端到端监督方法具有更高的特征语义质量，且解码器训练简单高效，为数据可视化反向解析提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Bar charts are commonly used in data visualization, and while they are easily understood by humans, it is non-trivial to extract the underlying data computationally. For a machine-learning-based approach, training chart de-rendering models usually requires labeled, real-world data. Labeling data is a time consuming task, which is why annotated data is scarce. Models can learn more efficiently when provided with features of high semantic quality, which a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) is designed to learn in a self-supervised manner. We present a per-bar, numerical value recovery pipeline for bar charts, where a JEPA encoder is used to produce semantically rich latent features. The decoder model consuming these features is simple and quick to train and outputs the coordinates of ticks and bars, which can be used to recover bar values. The effectiveness of self-supervised finetuning and quality of the extracted features is evident when comparing our model to end-to-end supervised baselines. Code, datasets and checkpoints are available on \href{https://github.com/dralois/Bar-JEPA}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [74] [The Next Screenshot Knows: Gated Hindsight Distillation for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06065)
*Weiwei Li,Junzhuo Liu,Tong Chu,Hengfu Yu,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Gated Hindsight Distillation (GHD)方法，利用下一帧截图作为训练时的特权信息来辅助GUI智能体学习动作背后的推理，解决标准模仿学习丢弃后续观测导致缺乏动作合理性证据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离线训练将轨迹分解为前缀-动作对，丢弃后续观测，导致模型无法理解动作为何正确。例如启用Soft Wrap时需要点击Edit或View，但只有菜单打开后才能看到证据。没有这种证据，模仿学习让模型几乎没有机会采样到正确推理路径。

Method: GHD使用下一帧截图作为训练时的特权信息。学生模型仅从可观测的轨迹前缀进行预测，参数共享的教师模型额外观察到下一帧截图并对学生的on-policy响应重新评分。仅当学生失败且基于事后信息的教师模型能够恢复演示动作时，才应用蒸馏。

Result: 在AndroidWorld和AndroidLab两个基准上，GHD相对于GRPO在两个视觉语言模型上均提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通过条件式事后蒸馏，GHD缓解了标准模仿学习中动作合理性证据缺失的问题，有效提升了GUI智能体的任务完成能力。

Abstract: GUI agents are commonly trained offline from successful interaction trajectories. Standard training decomposes each trajectory into prefix-action pairs: the agent predicts an action from the current screen and interaction history, while the subsequent observation is discarded. This removes the rationale of why an action is correct: the evidence often appears only on the subsequent screen. For example, to enable Soft Wrap, the agent should click Edit or View, but nothing reveals this until the menu opens. Without such evidence, standard imitation gives the model little chance of ever sampling and thus learning the correct reasoning. To address this issue, we propose Gated Hindsight Distillation (GHD), which uses the next screenshot as privileged information during training. A student predicts from the observable trajectory prefix, while a parameter-sharing teacher additionally observes the next screenshot and re-scores the student's on-policy responses. We apply distillation only when the student fails and the hindsight-conditioned teacher recovers the demonstrated action. GHD improves task success over GRPO on AndroidWorld and AndroidLab across two vision-language models. The code and checkpoints will be made available.

</details>


### [75] [Domain-Grounded Candidate Selection for Agentic Image Editing: A Shadow Removal Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06075)
*Shilin Hu,Jingyi Xu,Dimitris Samaras,Hieu Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究商用视觉语言模型是否降低了经典物理先验在低级视觉任务中的作用，以阴影去除为案例，发现商用生成模型虽能产生干净的阴影去除效果，但存在幻觉和物理解读错误等失败模式；作者提出基于物理引导的智能体候选筛选流程，在ShadowRemovalRefine基准上CDD达0.0075，较最强先前方法降低至少47%，表明物理先验不是被替代而是用于约束和引导物理欠约束的生成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用视觉语言模型具备了广泛到可与专用系统匹敌的视觉先验，自然引发一个问题：它们是否减少了对经典物理信息低级视觉方法的需求？阴影去除是一个由场景几何、光照、材质和遮挡物共同塑造的问题，成对数据难以大规模收集，是检验这一问题的理想场景。

Method: 提出一个智能体式候选筛选流水线：生成器基于物理引导的提示（将阴影视为光照遮挡效应而非材质或物体结构）生成引导探针，评估器筛选主要失败，必要时重试，采样多个候选，过滤后选择在阴影去除与场景保真之间取得平衡的最终结果。整个过程以阴影形成物理为根基，对生成器和评估器都注入物理先验。

Result: 在ShadowRemovalRefine基准上，所提出的物理导向流水线取得CDD为0.0075，相比最强先前方法至少降低47%的CDD。

Conclusion: 商用视觉语言模型并不会取代经典的低级视觉先验；相反，物理先验对于约束和引导物理上欠约束的生成过程仍然至关重要。

Abstract: Commercial vision-language models are reshaping computer vision, with visual priors broad enough to rival task-specific systems. This raises a natural question: do they reduce the need for classic, physics-informed low-level vision? We study this through shadow removal, a problem shaped by scene geometry, illumination, materials, and occluders, where paired shadow and shadow-free data are hard to collect at scale. We find that a commercial generative editor, used directly, can produce clean shadow-free edits that preserve surface texture and local appearance. However, this comes with a new failure mode: the same editor can regenerate scene content, hallucinate objects, or misread a shadow as material or geometry, producing plausible but physically wrong edits. We address this with an agentic candidate-selection pipeline: the editor generates a guided probe, an evaluator screens for major failures, retries when needed, samples multiple candidates, filters them, and selects a final result balancing shadow removal against scene preservation. Grounding this process in shadow-formation physics makes it more reliable: prompting the generator and evaluator to treat shadows as illumination effects caused by light occlusion, not material or object structure, measurably improves quality and consistency. On the ShadowRemovalRefine benchmark, our physics-oriented pipeline achieves a CDD of 0.0075, reducing CDD by at least 47% over the strongest prior method. These results suggest that commercial vision-language models do not replace classic low-level vision priors; instead, such priors remain useful for constraining and steering physically underconstrained generation.

</details>


### [76] [Dense-Cast: A lightweight ensemble of deep learning architectures for precipitation nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06082)
*Gourav Jyoti Kalita,Hidam Kumarjit Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级深度学习模型用于半小时尺度的降水临近期预报（nowcasting），该模型结合了DenseNet架构、残差连接和Transformer编码器，以印度东北部地区为研究对象，使用GPM IMERG降水数据（30分钟分辨率），以过去5个时间步预测未来2个半小时的降水，取得了MAE 0.235mm、RMSE 0.735mm和KGE 0.816的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降水短期预报对灾害管理和防灾准备至关重要，但降水的变异性与非线性特性使其预报困难；同时，在时空数据中捕捉时间依赖性也是降水临近期预报的挑战。现有方法在模型参数和预报精度之间存在权衡，需要一种轻量且有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种轻量级深度学习模型，融合了DenseNet架构、残差连接和Transformer编码器。模型输入为前5个半小时时间步的降水数据，输出预测未来2个半小时的降水。研究区域为印度东北部（季风季节6-9月降水量最大），使用GPM IMERG 30分钟分辨率降水数据集进行训练和测试。

Result: 模型在30分钟预报间隔下取得最佳MAE为0.235毫米，RMSE为0.735毫米，KGE评分为0.816。

Conclusion: 通过结合DenseNet、残差连接和Transformer编码器，该轻量级模型在减少模型参数的同时实现了有效的降水临近期预报，为降水临近期预报提供了一种高效且有前景的深度学习方案。

Abstract: Proper short-term forecasting of precipitation is crucial in disaster management and preparedness. Nonetheless, the variability and nonlinearity of precipitation make short-term forecasting challenging for meteorologists. Moreover, capturing temporal dependencies in spatiotemporal data is a challenge in precipitation nowcasting. In this article, we introduce a lightweight deep learning model for half-hourly precipitation nowcasting. This model has been designed by incorporating the DenseNet architecture, residual connections, and transformer encoders for effective precipitation nowcasting with reduced model parameters. The North-Eastern region of India has been selected as the area of interest for our study. The region receives the highest precipitation during the months of June-September due to the monsoon season. The proposed model takes the previous five time-steps of half-hourly precipitation as inputs and predicts the precipitation in the next two half-hours. The GPM IMERG precipitation dataset with a 30-minute cadence has been used in this study for training and testing the model. The proposed architecture achieves best MAE of 0.235 millimetres, RMSE of 0.735 millimetres, and KGE score of 0.816 at an interval of 30 minutes.

</details>


### [77] [Confidence matters: Leveraging Multi-view Geometric Priors for GS-based Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06117)
*Hongyu Zhou,Zorah Lähner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了将几何先验（预测的法线图和深度图）集成到3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）框架中以改善重建质量，发现基于多视图的预测（如VGGT）优于单视图方法，且置信度图能显著提升先验的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽然能实现实时渲染和稀疏表示，但其依赖传统的运动恢复结构（SfM）初始化和光度优化，导致对高镜面物体等复杂场景的几何重建效果不佳。作者希望通过集成几何先验来改进这一局限性。

Method: 将预测的法线图和深度图以几何先验的形式集成到3DGS框架中。作者对比了单视图预测与多视图预测（如视觉几何接地变换器VGGT）的效果，并利用多视图模型产生的置信度图对每个预测进行加权，以提高先验信息的利用效率。

Result: 在标准基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建质量上取得了一致的改进，在包含高镜面物体的复杂场景中获得了显著的性能提升。多视图预测结合置信度加权的方案明显优于单视图预测。

Conclusion: 将几何先验（特别是多视图预测的深度和法线）引入3DGS框架可以有效改善几何重建质量，置信度图是提升先验效果的关键因素，尤其对于高镜面物体等具有挑战性的场景。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a widely-used tool for novel view synthesis, offering real-time rendering in a sparse representation. However, the method's reliance on structure-from-motion initialization and photometric optimization can lead to suboptimal geometric reconstruction, particularly for objects with high specularity. In this work, we investigate the integration of geometric priors, in the form of predicted normal and depth maps, into the 3DGS framework to improve the reconstruction quality. We analyze the effect of incorporating these priors into GS-based methods and our evaluation reveals that multi-view predictions, as they are done by the recent visual geometry grounded transformer (VGGT), outperform single-view alternatives. A major factor is the existence of a confidence map for the estimations, which comes as a by-product of multi-view models and which can significantly improve the effectiveness of priors by weighting each prediction appropriately. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks show consistent improvement in reconstruction quality and significant gains in complex scenes including specular objects.

</details>


### [78] [Sample-Adaptive Latent Rewards for Uncertainty-Guided Diffusion Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06125)
*Rui Li,Yuanzhi Liang,Ke Hao,Ziqiao Weng,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,XueLong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SURE是一个统一的潜在空间框架，用于图像和视频扩散模型的对齐优化。它通过SURE-LRM学习奖励分布（预测高斯均值作为奖励分数、方差作为不确定性估计），并通过SURE-REFL利用不确定性引导的密集后训练反馈，全程在潜在空间中进行，无需像素级解码。实验表明其在偏好预测和后训练优化上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在奖励模型仅输出标量分数，无法估计预测不确定性，导致生成器无法判断反馈的可靠性，容易使优化走错方向并引发奖励作弊（reward hacking）。因此需要一种能同时提供奖励预测和不确定性估计的统一框架。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) SURE-LRM：样本自适应潜在奖励模型，为每个带噪潜在变量预测高斯奖励分布，均值表示奖励分数，方差表示预测不确定性（无需人工标注）；(2) SURE-REFL：不确定性引导的奖励反馈学习，在去噪轨迹的选定转换处查询冻结的SURE-LRM，将分离的方差转化为可靠性权重，每个加权奖励仅通过其局部转换反向传播。整个过程保持在潜在空间，无需像素解码或完整去噪图。

Result: SURE-LRM在偏好预测上超越强基线；SURE-REFL在多种指标上达到SOTA性能，进一步提升了优化稳定性；在VBench上取得了最高的质量、语义和总体分数。

Conclusion: 通过学习奖励分布而非单一标量分数，并利用不确定性作为反馈可靠性权重来指导密集后训练，SURE成功解决了潜在奖励模型缺乏不确定性估计的问题，在图像和视频扩散模型对齐任务上均取得了优越性能。

Abstract: Latent reward models can supervise visual diffusion models without decoding intermediate states into pixel space. This makes alignment with human preferences more efficient. However, existing latent reward models output only scalar scores. They do not estimate the uncertainty of each prediction. The generator therefore cannot determine which feedback is reliable. This can drive optimization in the wrong direction and lead to reward hacking. We propose \textsc{SURE}, a unified latent-space framework for image and video diffusion models. It learns reward distributions and directly uses their reliability to guide dense post-training. First, we propose sample-adaptive latent reward model (\textsc{SURE-LRM}). It predicts a Gaussian utility for each noisy latent. Its mean predicts the reward score. Its variance reflect the uncertainty of prediction without human annotation. The learned distribution then guides post-training through uncertainty-guided reward feedback learning (\textsc{SURE-REFL}). This method provides uncertainty-guided dense feedback along the denoising trajectory. At selected transitions, \textsc{SURE-REFL} queries the frozen \textsc{SURE-LRM}. It converts detached variance into reliability weights for samples at the same transition. Each weighted reward is backpropagated only through its local transition. The entire process remains in latent space and requires neither pixel-space decoding nor the full denoising graph. Experiments show that \textsc{SURE-LRM} improves preference prediction over strong baselines. \textsc{SURE-REFL} achieves the sota performance among various metrics and further improves optimization stability. It also achieves the highest VBench quality, semantic, and total scores among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [79] [Patient Pose Assessment Using a CT-Based Framework for Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06126)
*Manuel Laufer,Dominik Mairhöfer,Malte Sieren,Hauke Gerdes,Fabio Leal dos Reis,Arpad Bischof,Thomas Käster,Erhardt Barth,Jörg Barkhausen,Thomas Martinetz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从CT扫描中合成生成深度图像与X光片配对数据的框架，用于训练AI模型自动评估X光拍摄前的患者体位。在上踝关节数据集上的实验表明，预训练后体位评估准确率可提升最多11个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X光片的诊断质量高度依赖于患者拍摄时的体位，但体位摆放困难且缺乏标准化。虽然已有AI方法可通过深度图像自动评估体位，但由于监管障碍，现实中难以获取配对深度图像与X光片数据进行训练，因此需要合成数据方案。

Method: 提出一个从CT扫描数据中合成生成深度图像与X光片配对数据集的框架，生成包含3077对上踝关节图像对的合成数据集，并在此数据集上预训练体位评估模型。

Result: 在生成的3077对合成上踝关节数据集上进行预训练后，真实上踝关节体位评估的准确率提升了最多11个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于CT的合成数据生成框架可以有效缓解真实配对数据难以获取的问题，通过预训练显著提升真实场景中基于深度图像的患者体位自动评估性能。

Abstract: An adequate diagnostic quality of radiographs is essential for reliable diagnoses and treatment planning. The patient's pose during radiography is one of the most important factors determining the diagnostic quality. Since patient positioning is difficult and not standardized, an automated AI-based approach using depth images to automatically assess the patient's pose before the radiograph has been taken would be helpful. Due to regulatory hurdles, however, it is difficult in practice to acquire the required depth images and corresponding radiographs. In this paper, we present a framework that can generate such training data synthetically from Computed Tomography scans. We further show that by pretraining on our generated synthetic dataset consisting of 3077 image pairs of upper ankle joints, the pose assessment of real upper ankle joints can be improved by up to 11 percentage points.

</details>


### [80] [Learning visual representations for compositional analysis of artworks and photographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06142)
*Fatemeh Behrad,Tinne Tuytelaars,Johan Wagemans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种图像构图分析范式：基于感知分组的人类启发方法（使用目标中心模型和图表注意力网络）与基于大规模构图数据集微调的预训练基础模型。在构图评分/分类、构图图像检索和视觉显著性检测等任务上，冻结编码器时人类启发方法性能相当且可解释；数据充足时大型自监督模型表现更优，但牺牲了可解释性和跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构图是艺术作品中传达意义、情感和美学质量的核心，但在视觉理解中仍是最不形式化的维度之一。先前工作指出学习有意义的构图表示存在差距，归因于语义偏差，并暗示人类启发的思路可能是关键。

Method: 采用两种并行范式进行对比：(1) 人类启发方法基于感知分组理论，使用目标中心模型进行区域级分解，并借助图表注意力网络建模元素间的空间关系；(2) 基于近期大规模构图数据集微调的基础模型范式。

Result: 冻结编码器条件下，人类启发方法性能具有竞争力且保持可解释性；当数据充足支持微调时，大型自监督模型显著超越前者，但代价是丧失可解释性并削弱跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 两种范式各有优劣：人类启发方法在零样本/低数据场景下可解释且稳健，基础模型在数据充足时精度更高但缺乏可解释性，二者之间存在性能与可解释性/泛化之间的根本权衡。

Abstract: Composition, the deliberate arrangement of visual elements, is central to how meaning, emotion, and aesthetic quality are conveyed in artwork, yet it remains among the least formalized dimensions of visual understanding. Prior work highlights a persistent gap in learning meaningful compositional representations, attributing it to semantic bias and suggesting that human-inspired approaches may be key. We compare two parallel paradigms for composition analysis: a human-inspired method grounded in perceptual grouping, and fine-tuned foundation models enabled by recent large-scale compositional datasets. The human-inspired approach uses object-centric models for region-level decomposition and a graph attention network to capture spatial relationships between elements. Both paradigms are evaluated on composition score/category prediction, compositional image retrieval, and visual saliency detection. With frozen encoders, the human-inspired method achieves competitive performance while remaining interpretable. When sufficient data enables fine-tuning, large self-supervised models outperform significantly, but at the cost of interpretability and cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [81] [BendTwin: Robust Dense-to-Sparse Physical Reconstruction with Bending-Aware Differentiable Spring-Mass Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06164)
*Yixiong Jing,Qi Wang,Lin Chen,Junwei Jiang,Guangming Wang,Haibing Wu,Olaf Wysocki,Wanli Ma,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BendTwin是一种弯曲感知的可微分弹簧-质量框架，用于从视频重建可变形物体并进行未来预测。它在传统轴向弹簧基础上引入弯曲刚度和阻尼约束（作用于局部表面三元组），通过惩罚静止角偏差来正则化高阶变形，从而提升机械稳定性并实现更保真的数字孪生重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于弹簧-质量的物理驱动重建方法通常仅依赖轴向弹簧，这种简化不足以描述底层结构力学，当物理图被粗化时会出现机械欠约束问题，导致局部变形不稳定，限制了重建的物理保真度和预测能力。

Method: BendTwin在可微分弹簧-质量框架中引入弯曲感知机制：对局部表面三元组添加弯曲刚度和阻尼项，通过惩罚偏离静止角（rest angle）的偏差来正则化高阶形变。该方法在保持弹簧-质量系统简洁性的同时增强机械稳定性，并支持从稀疏视角RGB-D视频中重建。

Result: 实验表明BendTwin在视频重建和未来预测任务上持续优于仅含轴向弹簧的PhysTwin基线。消融研究证明弯曲约束在不同下采样比例下均能维持系统稳定性，并对原始PhysTwin公式产生一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: BendTwin为从稀疏视角RGB-D视频构建机械保真数字孪生提供了一种有效方案，通过弯曲约束弥补轴向弹簧模型的力学不足，兼顾了系统简洁性与物理稳定性。

Abstract: Reconstructing objects with mechanical properties from video observations enables physically consistent dynamic prediction, benefiting robotics planning and interaction. Existing spring--mass based physical driven reconstruction approaches offer efficient and differentiable physical reconstruction, but they typically rely on axial springs alone. Such formulations oversimplify the underlying structural mechanics and can become mechanically under-constrained when the physical graph is coarsened, limiting their ability to preserve stable local deformation. We present BendTwin, a bending-aware differentiable spring--mass framework for video-based reconstruction and future prediction of deformable objects. BendTwin introduces bending stiffness and damping over local surface triplets, penalizing deviations from rest angles and regularizing higher-order deformation. These bending constraints improve mechanical stability while preserving the simplicity of spring--mass system. Experiments show that BendTwin consistently outperforms the axial-only PhysTwin baseline. Ablation studies further demonstrate that the bending constraints maintain system stability across different downsampling ratios and consistently improve upon the original PhysTwin formulation. Overall, BendTwin provides an effective approach for constructing mechanically faithful digital twins from sparse-view RGB-D videos.

</details>


### [82] [Support Operation Factorization: Compositional Readout of Frozen Vision Encoders under Controlled Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06174)
*Zhongyao Wang,Wanli Ouyang,Taoyong Cui,Pheng Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种组合分析冻结视觉编码器的新协议SO-OPF（单细胞留出+可注入对齐），将细胞能量分解为支持显著性和竞争性操作后验，以区分"载体能否组合持有绑定"和"网格能否从扁平标签恢复"两个问题。在DINOv3和SigLIP2上验证了该方法，在Shapes3D和COCO上取得良好结果，同时暴露了MuJoCo基底的渲染器特定失败边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准因子探针分别独立地对各轴进行评分，可能奖励多个操作复用同一预测槽位（称为操作洗钱/operation laundering），无法同时确定冻结视觉编码器中"什么改变了"以及"在哪里改变"，即组合分析的两个基本问题被聚合分数混淆。

Method: 提出注入对齐的留一细胞留出协议（leave-one-cell-out），在支持×操作网格上进行；引入SO-OPF读头，将细胞能量分解为支持显著性（support salience）与竞争性操作后验（competitive operation posterior）。这分离了两个问题：已知网格时载体是否组合持有绑定，以及网格能否从扁平细胞标签中恢复。在DINOv3和SigLIP2冻结特征上评估，并在Shapes3D-Extended、全局图像不相交COCO以及重建的MuJoCo基底上测试。

Result: 在DINOv3上：已知阶乘分配在Shapes3D-Extended达到0.874注入准确率，在COCO达到0.799；从扁平标签学习分配分别达到0.769和0.762。Shapes3D上匹配轴感知监督下，因子载体将学习分配准确率从0.653提升至0.841（对比稠密载体），并消除了洗钱差距。SigLIP2在COCO上复现分离结果。MuJoCo基底上学习分配准确率DINOv3为0.569、SigLIP2为0.484，存在显著槽位坍缩。

Conclusion: 因子化读头和注入式评估能在两个基底上恢复持有绑定，同时暴露而非隐藏渲染器特定的失败边界；但并未确立从扁平标签进行通用恢复的能力。

Abstract: Compositional analysis of frozen vision encoders should determine both what changed and where it changed. Standard factor probes score these axes separately, however, and can reward multiple operations that reuse the same predicted slot. We call this failure operation laundering. We introduce an injectively aligned leave-one-cell-out protocol over support x operation grids and SO-OPF, a readout that factors cell energy into support salience and a competitive operation posterior. This formulation separates two questions that aggregate scores conflate: whether the carrier composes held-out bindings when the grid is known, and whether that grid can be recovered from flat cell labels. With frozen DINOv3 features, known factorial assignment reaches 0.874 injective accuracy on Shapes3D-Extended and 0.799 on globally image-disjoint COCO; learning the assignment from flat labels reaches 0.769 and 0.762, respectively. Under matched-axis-aware supervision on Shapes3D, the factored carrier improves learned-assignment accuracy from 0.653 to 0.841 over a dense carrier and eliminates its laundering gap. SigLIP2 replicates the COCO separation. A rebuilt MuJoCo substrate exposes a boundary: learned-assignment accuracy is 0.569 with DINOv3 and 0.484 with SigLIP2, with substantial slot collapse. Thus factored readout and injective evaluation recover held-out bindings on two substrates while exposing, rather than hiding, a renderer-specific failure boundary; they do not establish universal recovery from flat labels.

</details>


### [83] [EvReflection: Event-Driven Micro-Dynamics for Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06184)
*Jiaxiao Wang,Dachun Kai,Huyue Zhu,Quanquan Hu,Zhenyang Xu,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvReflection，一种利用事件相机捕捉微动态信息来解耦反射与透射层运动，从而突破传统单帧反射去除方法中固有的层间歧义问题的新型网络。作者设计了Micro-Dynamics Decoupler从事件流中提取层特定运动先验，并配合Parallax-Attention Rectifier对RGB图像进行干净的去反射处理，同时构建了首个真实世界数据集EVR^2和视差感知仿真管线以解决数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反射去除方法主要依赖单帧静态图像先验，由于反射层与透射层之间存在固有的模糊性，难以获得干净的分离结果，往往残留严重伪影。作者希望利用事件相机能够捕捉微秒级动态变化的优势，通过观测两层之间的差分运动来打破这种歧义，从而提升反射去除的性能。

Method: 1) 提出EvReflection网络架构，由两个核心模块组成：Micro-Dynamics Decoupler用于从事件流中解耦出反射层和透射层各自的运动特征作为先验；Parallax-Attention Rectifier利用这些动态先验引导注意力机制，从RGB图像中干净地去除反射伪影。2) 开发了视差感知仿真管线用于生成训练数据，并构建了EVR^2数据集，这是该任务首个真实世界数据集。

Result: EvReflection在合成和真实世界基准上均达到最先进性能，在PSNR指标上分别超越最优竞争方法超过1.6 dB（合成）和1.2 dB（真实），代码、数据集和预训练模型已开源。

Conclusion: 事件信号提供了一种有效的途径来突破单帧反射去除中的层间歧义难题，通过挖掘微动态差分运动信息，EvReflection实现了显著的性能提升，并为该领域提供了首个真实世界基准数据集和完整开源资源，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in reflection removal, current methods primarily exploit static image priors from a single frame and still suffer from severe residual artifacts due to the inherent ambiguity between the reflection and transmission layers. In this paper, we propose leveraging event signals to break this ambiguity. By employing event cameras to capture micro-dynamics, we reveal the differential motion between these two layers. We thereby present a novel event-driven reflection removal network, EvReflection, that utilizes these dynamic cues for layer separation. Specifically, we design a Micro-Dynamics Decoupler to disentangle layer-specific motions from event streams as priors, which then guide a Parallax-Attention Rectifier to cleanly remove artifacts from the RGB image. Furthermore, to address data scarcity, we develop a parallax-aware simulation pipeline and construct the EVR$^2$ benchmark dataset, the first real-world dataset for this task. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvReflection achieves state-of-the-art performance on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, surpassing the best competing method by more than 1.6 dB and 1.2 dB in PSNR, respectively. The code, dataset, and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/JiaxiaoWang/EvReflection.

</details>


### [84] [HOPE: Hand-Object Pressure Estimation from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06192)
*Subin Jeon,Byungjun Kim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOPE是一个从单目视频中预测手部网格顶点法向压力和接触的框架，将压力估计建模为手部中心的视频预测问题，通过将不同来源的触觉标签统一到手部顶点空间，并引入顶点锚定的视频Transformer和接触门控压力头，实现对裸手及非受限场景下接触和压力的联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的压力估计方法大多局限于平面表面和单帧图像输入，难以应用于多样的物体的动态手-物交互场景。作者希望打破这些限制，实现端到端的、与物体形状和传感器布局无关的手部压力估计。

Method: HOPE框架包含两个关键组件：(1) 将触觉手套压力、平面传感器压力和基于距离的手-物接触注释统一映射到共享的手部顶点空间，利用裸手接触数据来正则化缺乏度量标签的压力学习；(2) 引入顶点锚定的视频Transformer，将每个顶点作为持久token，跨时间聚合视觉特征和手部姿态，并使用接触门控压力头确保无接触时压力为零。

Result: 在OpenTouch、PressureVisionDB和手-物接触基准上的实验验证了HOPE在物体压力、表面压力和接触监督的HOI设置中的有效性。尽管度量压力监督主要来自戴手套视频，HOPE仍能泛化到裸手第一人称视角和非受限场景视频，产生超越仅接触或平面压力基线的联合接触和压力预测。

Conclusion: HOPE成功将物理压力估计从受限的平面/单图场景推广到动态的手-物交互视频场景，通过统一的手部顶点输出空间和顶点锚定的视频Transformer，实现了对裸手交互中接触和压力的联合预测，验证了该范式的泛化能力。

Abstract: Estimating physical pressure from vision is essential for understanding contact-rich hand-object interaction. However, prior vision-based pressure estimation methods are largely limited to planar surfaces and single image input, making them difficult to apply to dynamic hand-object interaction with diverse objects. We instead formulate pressure estimation as a hand-centric video prediction problem with monocular video as input. This formulation predicts temporally evolving per-vertex normal pressure and contact directly on the hand mesh, yielding a unified output space independent of object shape and sensor layout. Building on this formulation, we propose \textbf{HOPE}, a framework with two key components. First, we lift tactile-glove pressure, planar-sensor pressure, and distance-based hand-object contact annotations into a shared hand vertex space, allowing bare-hand contact data to regularize pressure learning where metric labels are unavailable. Second, we introduce a vertex-anchored video transformer that treats each vertex as a persistent token, aggregates visual features and hand pose over time, and uses a contact-gated pressure head to enforce that pressure vanishes without contact. Experiments on OpenTouch, PressureVisionDB, and hand-object contact benchmarks validate HOPE across object-pressure, surface-pressure, and contact-supervised HOI settings. Despite using metric pressure supervision primarily from gloved-hand videos, HOPE generalizes to bare-hand egocentric and in-the-wild videos, producing joint contact and pressure predictions beyond the scope of contact-only or planar-pressure baselines.

</details>


### [85] [CFGPNet: Cross-Attention-Based Fused Gradient Programmed Network Framework for Multispectral Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06205)
*Nima Hatami,Karim Faez,Saeed Sharifian,Hamidreza Amindavar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CFGPNet，一种基于交叉注意力的融合梯度编程网络框架，用于RGB-T多光谱目标检测。通过改进的GELAN骨干、CrossCEA跨模态交互模块、ASAF注意力选择聚合融合网络和可编程梯度辅助分支，在五个公开多光谱基准上取得了优异的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-T目标检测方法仍存在跨模态交互不足、模态分布差异导致的融合不稳定，以及基于重型注意力架构的高计算成本三大问题，亟待提出高效且有效的解决方案。

Method: 1) 使用带RepViT风格重参数化块的改进GELAN骨干增强特征表示同时保持计算效率；2) 引入CrossCEA（交叉计算高效注意力）模块增强跨模态特征交互并减少可见光和热红外分支之间的冗余信息传递；3) 设计ASAF（注意力选择与聚合融合）网络，结合密集特征聚合和选择性注意力强调生成紧凑且可判别性的融合表示；4) 在每个CFGPNet变体中集成可编程梯度辅助分支以改善梯度传递和优化质量。

Result: 在五个公开数据集（FLIR、M3FD、LLVIP、VEDAI、MFAD）上取得一致且优异的性能：FLIR达到80.7% mAP50/45.0% mAP50:95，M3FD达到89.9%/63.4%，LLVIP达到97.8%/68.9%，VEDAI达到83.3%/56.9%，MFAD达到83.4%/61.8%，并在三种模型尺度下实现了良好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: CFGPNet是一个有效且实用的多光谱目标检测解决方案，在跨不同场景、目标尺度和模态平衡的条件下均展现了稳定性能，并提供了有意义的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: RGB--T object detection exploits the complementary strengths of visible and infrared imagery, supporting robust perception in low-light, adverse-weather, and complex multi-scale environments. However, existing methods still suffer from insufficient cross-modal interaction, unstable fusion from modality distribution gaps, and the high computational cost of heavy attention-based architectures. To address these issues, CFGPNet is proposed, a Cross-Attention-Based Fused Gradient Programmed Network framework for multispectral object detection. CFGPNet uses an improved GELAN backbone with RepViT-style re-parameterized blocks to strengthen feature representation while preserving computational efficiency. A Cross Computation Efficient Attention (CrossCEA) module is introduced to enhance cross-modal feature interaction and reduce redundant information transfer between visible and thermal branches. To generate compact and discriminative fused representations, an Attention Selection and Aggregation Fusion (ASAF) network combines dense feature aggregation with selective attention-based emphasis. Moreover, a programmable-gradient auxiliary branch is integrated into each CFGPNet variant to improve gradient delivery and optimization quality. Experiments on five public multispectral benchmarks, FLIR, M3FD, LLVIP, VEDAI, and MFAD, demonstrate that CFGPNet achieves strong and consistent performance across diverse scenes, object scales, and modality balances. In particular, the framework attains 80.7% mAP50 / 45.0% mAP50:95 on FLIR, 89.9% / 63.4% on M3FD, and 97.8% / 68.9% on LLVIP. It also reaches 83.3% / 56.9% on VEDAI and 83.4% / 61.8% on MFAD. These results show that CFGPNet is an effective, practical solution offering useful accuracy--efficiency trade-offs across three model scales. The code, data, and fine-tuned models are available at https://github.com/NimaHatami99/CFGPNet.

</details>


### [86] [EmoWorld: A Decoupled Affective Field for Controllable Emotional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06231)
*Bingyuan Wang,Baistan Zhyldyzbekov,Kunyu Feng,Zeyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmoWorld是一个在冻结的流匹配视频扩散Transformer中解耦全局氛围、情感语义线索和时间进程三个因素的视频情感编辑框架，通过视觉氛围引导(VAS)、语义情感引导(SAS)和时间情感引导(TAS)三种策略分别控制不同维度的情感表达，在Wan2.2上显著提升了目标情感对齐度并减少了时间波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器将全局氛围、情感语义线索和时间进程纠缠在单一文本条件中，无法独立控制这些因素，导致情感表达难以精确调控。

Method: 该框架包含：一次性准备阶段，从保持几何中性的全景图和情感编辑全景图中提取层特定情感方向和可复用线索库；推理阶段采用三种引导策略：VAS将氛围方向注入隐藏状态，SAS分离可独立缩放的提示残差以控制语义线索，TAS在去噪步和时间轴上插值端点残差场。

Result: 在Wan2.2上，VAS将目标情感对齐提升19%并减少48%的时间波动代理指标；SAS提升目标情感对齐37%并增加36%的可检测情感线索；TAS在最强基线上提升转换单调性15%。框架在27种情感类别、文生视频和图生视频设置下评估，跨多个Video-DiT骨干网络可移植，支持相机条件组合且无需更新生成器参数。

Conclusion: EmoWorld成功实现了视频生成中情感因素的解耦控制，无需修改生成器参数即具备跨骨干网络的通用性和组合能力，为视频情感可控生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Emotion shapes how viewers interpret a scene, yet existing video generators entangle global atmosphere, affect-bearing semantic cues, and temporal progression within a single text condition. We present EmoWorld, a framework that decouples these factors within a frozen flow-matching video diffusion transformer (Video DiT). A one-time preparation stage extracts layer-specific affect directions and a reusable cue library from geometry-preserving neutral and emotion-edited panoramas. At inference, Visual Atmosphere Steering (VAS) injects atmosphere directions into hidden states, Semantic Affective Steering (SAS) isolates a separately scalable prompt residual for semantic cues, and Temporal Affective Steering (TAS) interpolates endpoint residual fields across denoising and video time. On Wan2.2, VAS improves target-emotion alignment by 19% while reducing a temporal-fluctuation proxy by 48%; SAS improves target-emotion alignment by 37% and increases detected affect-bearing cues by 36%; and TAS improves transition monotonicity by 15% over the strongest baseline. EmoWorld is evaluated across 27 emotion categories in text-to-video and image-to-video settings, demonstrates portability across multiple Video-DiT backbones, and supports camera-conditioned composition without updating generator parameters.

</details>


### [87] [Depth-Guided Video Object Counting in Crowded Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06236)
*Yuanjing Xu,Xinyan Liu,Weidong Chen,Zixuan Zou,Linhao Zhang,Zhuangzhe Meng,Antoni B. Chan,Weigang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出深度引导检测器（DG-Det）结合统一去重框架，利用深度线索和多尺度RGB-D交叉注意力解决拥挤遮挡场景下的视频目标计数问题，并发布新的RGB-D视频目标计数数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标计数方法仅依赖RGB信息，在拥挤和遮挡条件下判别能力受限，难以鲁棒地根据文本或视觉提示计数目标类别的所有实例。

Method: 提出Depth-Guided Detector（DG-Det），通过深度线索与多尺度RGB-D交叉注意力融合深度信息，并引入显式遮挡预测增强空间理解；同时提出统一去重框架以消除跨帧冗余计数，并发布包含深度信息和多类别目标的RGB-D视频目标计数数据集。

Result: 与现有基线相比，MAE降低62.01%，RMSE也获得一致改进，代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 深度信息与RGB特征的融合能显著提升拥挤遮挡场景下视频目标计数的鲁棒性和准确性，所提出的方法和数据集为后续研究提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Our primary objective is to advance video object counting in crowded scenes, aiming to robustly count all instances of a target category based on given text or visual prompts. Existing methods rely on RGB information, limiting their discriminative ability in crowded and occluded conditions. To address this, we propose a Depth-Guided Detector (DG-Det) along with a general post-processing pipeline. By integrating depth cues with multi-scale RGB-D cross-attention and explicit occlusion prediction, our method enhances spatial understanding and achieves robust detection in crowded and occluded scenes. Furthermore, we introduce a unified de-duplication framework to eliminate cross-frame redundant counting. To facilitate future research, we also release a new RGB-D Video Object Counting dataset featuring depth information and multiple object categories persequence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a 62.01\% reduction in MAE compared to existing baselines, and also produces consistent improvements in RMSE. We provide the source code at https://github.com/streamer-AP/DG-Net and the dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aerospace123/RGBD-VideoCount.

</details>


### [88] [PRISM: Distribution-Gated Flow Matching for Controllable Unpaired Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06240)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一个无GAN的流匹配（flow-matching）框架，通过可学习的逐特征门控（per-feature gate）替代传统的全局噪声/引导值控制，实现非配对图像到图像翻译中"保留什么"与"改变什么"的解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的非配对图像翻译方法使用单一的全局噪声或引导值来控制图像保留程度，无法将需要保留的内容与需要改变的外观分离开来，导致翻译时难以在目标真实性与结构保持之间取得平衡。

Method: PRISM采用无GAN的流匹配框架，其核心是一个可学习的逐特征门控。该门控的空间先验基于每个源特征到目标特征分布的标准距离推导，远离目标分布的特征被释放（改变），而目标一致的特征则被保留。该门控同时控制两个环节：(1) 初始化——将真实源潜在表示与任务匹配的腐败（corruption）混合，腐败方式分为内容锚定（AdaIN，用于保结构翻译）和部分锚定（用于结构改变翻译）；(2) ODE积分过程中的传输时序。门控在推理时可被文本或检测器的局部覆盖而无需重新训练。

Result: 在五个自然与生物医学基准（AFHQ猫→狗、CelebA-HQ外观翻译、昼夜重光照、虚拟染色、乳腺冰冻→永久石蜡病理）上，在共享相同划分协议的评估下，PRISM在四个基准上取得最佳Inception FID和KID，在第五个上取得有竞争力的结果；在组织病理学任务上取得最接近理想的细胞核计数比，表明其在目标真实性与结构保持之间取得了有利的平衡。

Conclusion: PRISM通过逐特征门控将"保留"与"改变"进行空间解耦，同时控制初始化与传输时序，在不需要对抗训练的情况下，在图像质量与结构保持之间取得了优于现有方法的平衡，并支持推理时的局部可覆盖性。

Abstract: Unpaired image-to-image translation must decide, per image, what to change and what to preserve without paired supervision. Many diffusion-based unpaired translators control preservation through a single global noise or guidance value applied across the image, which cannot separate content to keep from appearance to change. We present PRISM, a GAN-free flow-matching framework that replaces this global control with a learned per-feature gate. The gate's spatial prior is derived from each source feature's standardized distance to the target feature distribution, so features far from the target are freed while target-consistent features are preserved. The same gate controls both the initialization, which mixes the real source latent with a task-matched corruption, and the transport timing during Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration. The corruption is matched to the task, content-anchored (AdaIN) for structure-preserving translation and partially anchored for structure-changing translation, and the gate can be overridden locally at inference from text or a detector without retraining, preserving important structures of the original image while still generating realistic results. We evaluate PRISM on five natural and biomedical benchmarks (AFHQ cat->dog, CelebA-HQ appearance translation, day->night relighting, virtual staining, and breast frozen->permanent histopathology). Among the evaluated methods under a shared same-split protocol, PRISM attains the best Inception FID and KID on four benchmarks and a competitive result on the fifth, and on histopathology yields the nuclei-count ratio closest to the ideal, supporting a favorable balance between target realism and structural preservation.

</details>


### [89] [Engram-E2VID: Reference-Based Event-to-Video Reconstruction via Generative Activation of Appearance Engrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05728)
*Feiyu Ji,Xiang Li,Hao Ma,Tianxiang Huang,Qingxin Lu,Mengqi Ji,Lei Han,Xiaokang Yang,Xiaoyun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Engram-E2VID，一种基于结构引导的参考图+事件流到目标帧重建框架，通过将参考帧编码为token空间的外观记忆（appearance engrams），并将事件流转化为目标时刻的运动结构脚手架，在一步扩散骨干中逐步激活相关外观记忆以重建目标帧，在三个基准上比最强同输入基线PSNR最高提升3.29 dB、LPIPS降低0.08。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件流虽然能提供细粒度的时间线索，但只编码稀疏且异步的对数强度变化而非绝对外观信息，因此仅凭事件流精确重建目标帧极具挑战。核心难点在于如何在复杂运动和长时程间隔下，将事件推导出的目标时刻结构与参考帧中的相关外观信息进行有效关联，而直接依赖像素级对应关系的方法在复杂场景中难以奏效。

Method: Engram-E2VID采用结构引导框架：(1) 将参考帧编码为token空间的外观记忆（appearance engrams）；(2) 将事件流与参考上下文转换为目标时刻的运动结构脚手架（motion-structure scaffold），捕获运动边界和事件引起的结构变化；(3) 在一步扩散骨干中，脚手架推导的结构token跨层逐步交互并激活相关外观记忆；(4) 通过token空间关联使目标结构获取参考外观而无需像素级对应，扩散先验补充不确定或新出现的区域。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，Engram-E2VID相对于最强同输入基线在PSNR上最高提升3.29 dB，LPIPS最高降低0.08，并且随着重建间隔增加，性能下降速度更慢，展现出更好的长时程重建鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Engram-E2VID通过将事件结构与参考外观在token空间进行生成式关联，有效解决了事件到视频重建中复杂运动和长时程间隔下的外观-结构对应难题，在无需像素级匹配的前提下实现了更优且更稳健的重建性能。

Abstract: Reference-based event-to-video reconstruction aims to recover target RGB frames from a reference frame and the event stream captured over the reference-to-target interval. Although events provide fine-grained temporal cues, they encode sparse and asynchronous log-intensity changes rather than absolute appearance, making faithful reconstruction intrinsically challenging. The central challenge lies in associating event-derived target-time structures with relevant appearance information from the reference frame, especially under complex motion and long temporal intervals. In this work, we propose Engram-E2VID, a structure-guided framework that reconstructs target frames through the generative activation of appearance engrams. Specifically, the reference frame is encoded into token-space appearance engrams, while the event stream and reference context are transformed into a target-time motion-structure scaffold that captures motion boundaries and event-induced structural changes. Within a one-step diffusion backbone, scaffold-derived structural tokens progressively interact with and activate relevant appearance engrams across layers. This token-space association allows target structures to access reference appearance without relying on direct pixel-wise correspondence, while the diffusion prior complements uncertain or newly revealed regions. Across three benchmarks, Engram-E2VID improves PSNR by up to 3.29 dB and reduces LPIPS by up to 0.08 over the strongest same-input baseline, while degrading more slowly as the reconstruction interval increases.

</details>


### [90] [Toward Deployable Bangla Sign Language Recognition with Expert-Validated Data and a Lightweight Attention-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06252)
*Saad Ahmed,Md Khalid Syfullaha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSBdSL38数据集（10,874张专家验证的孟加拉手语图像，覆盖全部38个手语手势）和一个轻量级注意力卷积网络（298,470参数），实现96.37%准确率，比ImageNet预训练模型少8.5-68倍参数，在商用手机上以0.48MB、单张3.98ms运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉聋哑人群主要依赖孟加拉手语（BdSL）交流，但现有自动识别系统依赖受控环境数据集且缺乏专家验证，使用笨重的预训练骨干网络不适合移动端部署。目标是将基准准确率转化为可部署的可访问性应用。

Method: 构建RSBdSL38数据集（来自孟加拉三所特殊学校真实手语者，专家验证的38种手势、覆盖51个孟加拉字母）。提出轻量级注意力卷积网络，包含分组瓶颈残差块、通道和空间注意力、多尺度深度可分离手部特征块、双池化和Swish激活，从零训练。

Result: 从零训练达到96.37%准确率（五个种子95.72%±0.54%），与最佳ImageNet预训练架构差距仅1.08个百分点，但参数量少8.5-68倍、MACs少1.3-21.7倍。在六个公开BdSL基准上重训达到92.95-98.33%，合并语料97.04%，BdSL-38零样本76.25%。移除任何架构阶段损失7.61-89.30分，训练配方最多损失3.17分。保留6/36个手语者的划分达85.18%。量化至0.48MB后，在商用手机上以15.5MB占用、3.98ms/张运行。

Conclusion: RSBdSL38数据集与从零训练的轻量模型共同将基准准确率转化为可部署的移动端可访问性方案，成本仅为预训练骨干的一小部分；数据集、代码和模型均已开源。

Abstract: Deaf and hard-of-hearing people in Bangladesh communicate mainly through Bangla Sign Language (BdSL). Automatic BdSL recognition on personal devices could widen access to education and services. Existing systems use controlled-setting datasets without expert verification and heavyweight pretrained backbones unsuited to on-device use. We introduce RSBdSL38, 10,874 expert-validated images spanning all 38 BdSL hand signs, representing the 51 letters of the Bangla alphabet, recorded from real signers at three special-needs schools across Bangladesh. We propose a lightweight attention based convolutional network of 298,470 parameters, built from grouped bottleneck residual blocks, channel and spatial attention, a multi-scale depthwise hand-feature block, dual pooling, and Swish activations. Trained from scratch, it attains 96.37% accuracy (95.72% +- 0.54% over five seeds), within 1.08 percentage points of the best of nine ImageNet-pretrained efficient architectures under an identical protocol, using 8.5 to 68x fewer parameters and 1.3 to 21.7x fewer MACs. Retrained, it reaches 92.95 to 98.33% on six public BdSL benchmarks, 97.04% on a merged corpus, and 76.25% zero-shot on BdSL-38. Removing any architectural stage costs 7.61 to 89.30 points, against at most 3.17 for the training recipe. Grad-CAM with deletion-insertion and weight-randomization checks confirms that predictions follow the signing hand. A signer-independent split holding out 6 of 36 signers yields 85.18%. Quantized to 0.48 MB, it runs at 3.98 ms per image within a 15.5 MB footprint on a commodity smartphone. Together, RSBdSL38 and our from-scratch model turn benchmark accuracy into deployable accessibility at a fraction of pretrained-backbone cost; dataset, code, and models are released.

</details>


### [91] [HoloCount: A Holistic Visual Counting Benchmark for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06420)
*Jinhong Deng,Limeng Qiao,Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HoloCount，一个基于三级层次分类法的、全面且可诊断的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）视觉计数基准，涵盖语义计数、分析计数和鲁棒性测试三个层面，通过对20多种SOTA MLLM的评估揭示了模型在从感知向复杂推理和对抗场景过渡时性能显著下降的临界差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在定性场景理解上取得了显著成功，但定量精度仍存在瓶颈，主要表现为数值幻觉。已有的计数基准多聚焦于简化场景中的基础感知任务，无法捕捉逻辑约束或对抗条件下出现的复杂失效模式，亟需一个更全面的诊断性基准。

Method: 构建HoloCount基准，采用三级层次分类法：(1) 语义计数——原子级和属性枚举；(2) 分析计数——通过空间和集合推理进行逻辑组合；(3) 鲁棒性测试——针对高密度场景、语言偏见等不利条件和grounded counter-priors探测模型完整性。对20多种SOTA MLLMs进行全面评估。

Result: 揭示了一个关键性能差距：即使顶级模型在任务从感知层面向复杂分析推理和对抗场景过渡时也出现显著性能退化，为当前MLLM计数能力提供了系统性图景。

Conclusion: 该研究为开发更可靠、更接地气的多模态系统提供了路线图，HoloCount数据集已公开。

Abstract: Visual counting is a fundamental pillar of multimodal intelligence, requiring a seamless integration of fine-grained grounding and spatial reasoning. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in qualitative scene understanding, their quantitative precision remains a significant bottleneck, often characterized by persistent numerical hallucinations. Existing counting benchmarks primarily focus on basic perception in simplified contexts, failing to capture the complex failure modes that emerge under logical constraints or adversarial conditions. To address these limitations, we introduce HoloCount, a holistic and diagnostically rich benchmark structured around a three-level hierarchical taxonomy. HoloCount evaluates MLLMs across: (1) Semantic Counting, focusing on atomic and property-based enumeration; (2) Analytical Counting, assessing logical composition through spatial and set-based reasoning; and (3) Robustness Testing, probing model integrity against adverse scenarios and grounded counter-priors, such as high-density scenes and linguistic biases. Through an exhaustive evaluation of over 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs, we reveal a critical performance gap: even top-tier models degrade significantly as tasks transition from perception to complex analytical reasoning and adverse scenarios. Our findings provide a systematic landscape of current MLLM counting capabilities and offer a roadmap for developing more grounded and reliable multimodal systems. The dataset is available at https://mm-mvr.github.io/HoloCount/.

</details>


### [92] [MASS: Multiplayer World Models with Authoritative Shared State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06257)
*Ziqi Cai,Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Zixian Gao,Yunheng Liu,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS（Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State），一种将世界动力学与视角渲染解耦的多玩家世界模型。它通过学习的Logic Engine从联合动作推进全局权威类型化状态，并作为唯一的循环记忆与同步参考；再由学习的Rendering Engine按需生成任意相机的独立一致视图。在多人Snake基准上相较多视角基线实现了更高的状态精度和更低的跨视角不一致性，可推进1024个并发玩家进行10000步循环预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在多玩家环境中将世界状态与视角相关的视觉潜在表示纠缠在一起，导致冗余计算、视角不一致和扩展性差。

Method: 受多人游戏架构启发，MAS将世界动力学与视角渲染解耦：1）学习的Logic Engine基于全局权威类型化状态从联合动作推进世界，无需任何手写转移函数，并作为唯一循环记忆和同步参考；2）学习的Rendering Engine从共享状态按需为任意请求的相机生成独立且一致的视图。

Result: 在多人Snake基准上，MAS相较最先进的多视角基线取得了更高的状态精度和更低的跨视角不一致性。模型能够以1024个并发玩家推进预测世界达10000个循环步骤。

Conclusion: 显式的权威状态建模为可扩展且一致的多智能体世界模拟提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Current video world models struggle in multiplayer environments because they entangle world state with view-dependent visual latents, leading to redundant compute, view inconsistencies, and poor scalability. We propose MAS (Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State) to resolve this limitation. Inspired by multiplayer game architectures, MAS disentangles world dynamics and view rendering. A learned Logic Engine advances a global, authoritative typed state from joint actions without any hand-written transition function, acting as the sole recurrent memory and synchronization reference. From this shared state, a learned Rendering Engine generates independent and consistent views for any requested camera on demand. This explicit disentangling allows MAS to achieve superior state accuracy and lower cross-view inconsistency compared to state-of-the-art multi-view baselines on a matched multiplayer Snake benchmark. It advances predicted worlds with 1,024 concurrent players for 10,000 recurrent steps. Our results show that explicit, authoritative state modeling provides a practical foundation for scalable and consistent multi-agent world simulation.

</details>


### [93] [VSMP-IMU: Video-Grounded Semantic Motion Programs for Sensor-Aware Synthetic IMU Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05782)
*Lala Shakti Swarup Ray,Vitor Fortes Rey,Mengxi Liu,Paul Lukowicz,Bo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VSMP-IMU，一种基于视频和结构化语义运动程序（SMP）的可控合成IMU数据生成框架，用于增强可穿戴人体活动识别（HAR）模型性能。该框架将活动定义语义与标签保持变异分离，从视频中提取并增强SMP，合成运动后转换为虚拟IMU信号并接地到目标穿戴域。在五个公开IMU-HAR数据集上的留一评估中，VSMP-IMU平均Macro-F1达78.33%，比纯真实训练提升9.77%，比最强先验合成基线提升4.04%；在低资源、长尾不平衡等场景下均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴HAR常受限于标注传感器数据稀缺（尤其低资源、类别不平衡和跨主体泛化场景）。合成IMU生成可缓解此问题，但现有方法存在权衡：视频驱动方法视觉可信但易受姿态估计误差影响，文本驱动方法可控但缺乏动作真实执行方式的对齐。需要一个同时兼顾视觉真实性和可控性的合成数据生成方案。

Method: VSMP-IMU是一个视频接地框架，核心是基于结构化语义运动程序（SMP）将活动定义语义与标签保持变异分离。流程为：（1）从输入视频提取并增强SMP；（2）基于SMP合成运动；（3）将运动转换为虚拟IMU信号；（4）将生成的信号接地到目标可穿戴设备域。

Result: 在五个公开IMU-HAR数据集上进行留一评估：平均Macro-F1达78.33%，比纯真实训练提升9.77%，比最强先验合成基线提升4.04%。低资源场景下比纯真实训练提升18.54%，比最强先验合成基线平均提升超6%。长尾不平衡评估中，尾类Macro-F1比纯真实训练提升19.86%，比SOTA提升4.76%。

Conclusion: 结构化视频接地语义为可控、穿戴相关的合成传感器数据生成提供了实用基础，能有效缓解标注数据稀缺问题，并在多种设定下显著提升HAR模型性能。

Abstract: Wearable human activity recognition (HAR) is often limited by the scarcity of labeled sensor data, especially in low-resource, class-imbalanced, and subject-generalization settings. Synthetic IMU generation can reduce this dependency and enhance HAR machine learning model's performance, but existing approaches face a trade-off without addressing all factors: video-driven methods are visually grounded but sensitive to pose-estimation errors, while text-driven methods are controllable but often weakly grounded in how activities are actually performed. We present VSMP-IMU, a video-grounded framework for controllable synthetic IMU generation based on a structured Semantic Motion Program (SMP), which separates activity-defining semantics from label-preserving variation. Given an input video, VSMP-IMU extracts and augments an SMP, uses it to synthesize motion, converts the motion into virtual IMU signals, and grounds the resulting signals to the target wearable domain. We evaluate VSMP-IMU against state-of-the-art synthetic data generation methods on five public IMU-HAR datasets under leave-one-person-out evaluation. VSMP-IMU achieves an average Macro-F1 of 78.33%, improving over real-only training by 9.77% and over the strongest prior synthetic baseline by 4.04%. In low-resource settings with reduced training data-samples, it improves over real-only training by 18.54% and over the strongest prior synthetic baselines by more than 6% on average. Under long-tail evaluation in imbalanced datasets, it improves tail-class Macro-F1 by 19.86% over Real-only training and by 4.76% over SOTA. These results show that structured video-grounded semantics provide a practical foundation for controllable, wearable-relevant synthetic sensor data generation.

</details>


### [94] [OTLesMix: Wasserstein Barycenter and Optimal Transport Map for Synthetic Lesion Generation with Diverse Shapes and Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06264)
*Robin Trombetta,Carole Lartizien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OTLesMix的新型图像合成方法，利用Wasserstein重心和最优传输计划生成多样且真实的脑部病变样本用于数据增强。在三个脑病变分割任务上，该方法相比不使用合成数据的模型Dice分数提升2.9至6.6个百分点，并优于现有基于混合的先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于混合策略的数据增强方法虽然能生成新样本，但主要局限在于生成的病变形状和位置变异性有限，无法充分多样化训练数据，从而限制了模型性能的提升。

Method: 提出OTLesMix方法，利用Wasserstein重心和最优传输计划来生成真实且多样的合成样本，通过更有效的混合策略克服传统mix-based方法在病变形状与位置多样性上的不足。

Result: 在三个脑病变分割任务上，OTLesMix相比不使用合成数据的基线模型Dice分数提升2.9至6.6个百分点，并超越现有的基于混合的先进方法。

Conclusion: OTLesMix作为一种基于Wasserstein重心和最优传输的图像合成方法，能够有效提升医学影像分割性能，验证了其在数据增强方面的优越性。

Abstract: The development of deep learning over the past decade has revolutionized medical imaging segmentation, allowing the extraction of precise descriptors from large volumes to characterize pathologies. Data augmentation is a technique widely regarded as a way to improve model training. It includes simple transformations like spatial operations or intensity modifications, but also more advanced synthesis techniques. Their goal is to generate new realistic samples from an existing dataset to diversify the images used during training. Among them, several propose different mixing strategies to combine real samples. However, one of their major shortcomings is to yield limited variability in terms of generated lesion shapes and locations. In this work, we introduce a novel image synthesis method, called OTLesMix, that leverages Wasserstein barycenter and optimal transport plan to generate realistic and diverse samples. We evaluated our method on three brain lesion segmentation tasks, on which it improves the Dice score compared to a model trained without synthetic data by 2.9 to 6.6 points, and outperforms state-of-the-art mix-based methods.

</details>


### [95] [KVAE: Family of Tokenizers for Multimodal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05798)
*Andrey Shutkin,Denis Parkhomenko,Ivan Kirillov,Kirill Chernyshev,Kirill Malakhov,Ilia Vasiliev,Ilia Trushkin,Valeriya Kobenko,David Chikovani,Alexander Ivanov,Azat Saginbaev,Egor Silvestrov,Ivan Mikheev,Konstantin Zakharov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了KVAE系列Tokenizer（包括KVAE-Audio、KVAE-3D和KVAE-2D），分别针对音频、视频和图像设计，用于后续的文本条件生成任务。实验表明这些Tokenizer在重建质量和生成效果上匹配或超越了前沿开源Tokenizer（如Wan-2.2、HunyuanVideo-1.5、FLUX.2等），并公开了训练细节和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在扩散模型（LDM）依赖Tokenizer将输入信号映射到压缩表示空间，这使Tokenizer成为生成流程中的关键组成部分，直接影响训练速度、生成质量和下游应用。然而当前缺少一个系统设计、跨模态（音频、图像、视频）且面向文本条件生成的统一Tokenizer方案。

Method: 提出了KVAE系列Tokenizer：KVAE-Audio（连续全频段48kHz音频tokenizer，50Hz latent率、64通道）；KVAE-3D（两个因果视频tokenizer，分别实现4x16x16和4x8x8压缩）；KVAE-2D（图像模型，8倍压缩因子、32通道）。这些Tokenizer均针对后续文本条件生成任务进行设计，并提供了训练细节、模型选择方法和设计选择的消融实验。

Result: 在重建指标（PSNR、LPIPS、PESQ等）和客观生成指标（Frechet Distance、CLIP score、CLAP score等）以及主观侧对比评估上，KVAE系列匹配或超越了前沿开源Tokenizer（包括Wan-2.2、HunyuanVideo-1.5、FLUX.2、MovieGen、StableAudio和MMAudio）。

Conclusion: KVAE系列Tokenizer证明了可以通过统一的架构设计和精心调优来构建跨模态的高质量Tokenizer，并已开源代码和详细的训练经验，为该领域的研究和开发提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Latent diffusion modeling (LDM), a prominent paradigm, utilizes tokenizers to map input signal to compressed representation. This dependency positions tokenizer as an integral part of generation process itself, since it affects learning speed, quality of synthesized samples and lay foundation for later applications. This report presents series of KVAE tokenizers for audio, image and video, all designed for subsequent text-conditioned generation: KVAE-Audio, a continuous full-band 48 kHz tokenizer with a 50 Hz latent of 64 channels; KVAE-3D -- two causal video tokenizers for 4x16x16 and 4x8x8 compression; KVAE-2D, an image model, compressing input by factor of 8 with 32 channels. We demonstrate that reconstruction (PSNR, LPIPS, PESQ, etc.) and generation results on objective (Frechet Distance, CLIP score, CLAP score, etc.) and subjective (side-by-side evaluation) metrics matches or surpasses frontier opensource tokenizers, such as VAEs from Wan-2.2, HunyuanVideo-1.5, FLUX.2, MovieGen, StableAudio and MMAudio. Considering difficulty of development, we share with community training details, model selection method and ablation on design choices. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/kandinskylab/kvae and https://github.com/kandinskylab/kvae-audio.

</details>


### [96] [TLNM: Externally Validated Tooth Detection, Numbering and Segmentation from Smartphone Photographs Using Mask R-CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06275)
*Arash Nedaei,Henna Tiensuu,Elina Väyrynen,Saujanya Karki,Jaakko Suutala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能手机照片的牙齿定位与编号模型，采用定制化Mask R-CNN管道，通过掩膜灰世界白平衡算法和anatomical约束检测层来处理患者自生成数据的变异性。模型在内部测试集上达到实例掩膜AP@50为0.818，外部数据集上达到0.901，展示了消费级手机影像支持自动牙齿级解剖映射的可行性，为资源受限环境下的远程筛查和远程牙科提供了可扩展的低成本基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数十亿人存在口腔健康问题，但专业牙科护理的成本和可及性限制了预防性口腔保健的开展。现有研究依赖临床级X光片或口腔内窥镜图像，普通公众无法获得这些设备进行自我筛查，因此需要开发基于智能手机照片的自动牙齿定位与编号方法。

Method: 开发了一个定制化的Mask R-CNN管道，使用1,272张标注的智能手机图像进行训练。针对患者自生成健康数据的变异性，管道包含两个基于领域知识的机制：(1)掩膜灰世界白平衡算法，用于减轻人工色彩偏移；(2)解剖学约束检测层，用于强制结构有效性并抑制假阳性。评估包括四个阶段：内部留出测试、独立外部测试、描述性消融研究以及基于相同内部测试集的折叠训练稳定性分析。

Result: 内部测试集上实例掩膜AP@50为0.818，类感知PQ为0.780，操作F1为0.884。十次运行的训练稳定性分析中AP@50标准差仅为0.009。尽管外部数据集在人群、传感器和采集协议上存在差异，模型仍达到AP@50为0.901、类感知PQ为0.832、操作F1为0.928。推理管道以开源、容器化API形式公开提供。

Conclusion: 消费级智能手机图像可以支持自动化的牙齿级解剖映射，为资源受限环境下提供可扩展、潜在低成本的远程筛查和远程牙科基础。

Abstract: Oral health issues affect billions globally, but the cost and limited access to professional dental care hinder preventive oral healthcare. Research relies on clinical-grade radiographs or intraoral camera images, unavailable for public self-screening. This study introduces a tooth localisation and numbering model for smartphone photographs. We developed a customised Mask Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Mask R-CNN) pipeline trained on 1,272 annotated smartphone images. To address variability in patient-generated health data, the pipeline incorporates two domain-informed mechanisms: a masked gray-world white-balancing algorithm to mitigate artificial colour casts and an anatomically constrained detection layer to enforce structural validity and suppress false positives. Evaluation comprised four stages: internal held-out testing, independent external testing, a descriptive ablation study, and fold-based training stability analysis using the same internal test set. On the internal test set, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.818, class-aware PQ of 0.780, and operational F1 of 0.884. Training stability showed limited between-model variation: across ten runs, instance-mask AP@50 had a standard deviation of 0.009. On the external dataset, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.901, class-aware PQ of 0.832, and operational F1 of 0.928 despite differences in population, sensors, and acquisition protocols. The inference pipeline is available as an open-source, containerised API. These results demonstrate that consumer-grade smartphone imagery can support automated tooth-level anatomical mapping, offering a scalable, potentially low-cost foundation for remote screening and tele-dentistry in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [97] [UQ-Loc: Uncertainty-Aware LiDAR Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06307)
*Jacek Komorowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UQ-Loc是一种基于LiDAR的场景坐标回归方法，在LightLoc架构上扩展了一个各向异性高斯协方差头，预测每个体素的3x3正定协方差矩阵，以实现不确定性感知的6-DoF定位。训练使用带有kNN空间平滑正则化的负对数似然损失，推理采用改进的SC2-PCR求解器（不确定性加权种子评分和马氏距离内点检验），并以期望校准误差（ECE）作为不确定性质量评估指标。实验表明该方法在提升定位精度的同时产生了良好校准的协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR场景坐标回归方法仅产生确定性预测，忽略了可提高鲁棒性和下游决策能力的偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）。

Method: 1) 在LightLoc架构上增加各向异性高斯协方差头，预测每个体素的3x3正定协方差矩阵；2) 采用负对数似然（NLL）损失并加入kNN空间平滑正则化项；3) 推理阶段使用改进的SC2-PCR求解器，包含不确定性加权的种子打分和马氏距离内点检验。

Result: UQ-Loc在6-DoF定位精度上实现了一致的提升，同时产出了校准良好的协方差预测。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模偶然不确定性并将不确定性信息融入求解过程，UQ-Loc在提升定位精度的同时提供了可信的不确定性估计，为下游决策提供了更可靠的依据。

Abstract: LiDAR-based Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) maps point clouds directly to 3D scene coordinates, enabling precise 6-DoF localisation without explicit map retrieval. However, existing methods produce deterministic predictions, discarding aleatoric uncertainty that could improve robustness and downstream decision-making. We present UQ-Loc, which extends the LightLoc architecture with an anisotropic Gaussian covariance head that predicts a full 3x3 positive-definite covariance matrix per voxel. Training uses a Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) loss augmented with a kNN-based spatial smoothness regulariser, while inference employs a modified SC2-PCR solver with uncertainty-weighted seed scoring and a Mahalanobis-distance inlier test. We adopt Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as a principled metric for evaluating the quality of the predicted uncertainty. Experiments demonstrate that UQ-Loc achieves consistent improvement in 6-DoF localization accuracy while producing well-calibrated covariances.

</details>


### [98] [Does FLAIR super-resolution erase or hallucinate small white-matter lesions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06311)
*Zahra Khodakarami,Yue Li,Pulkit Khandelwal,John Detre,Sandhitsu Das,Christopher Brown,David Wolk,Paul Yushkevich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了超分辨率重建在WMH分割前的应用效果，发现SR的主要影响是擦除小真实病灶（而非产生幻觉病灶），且随切片厚度增加而加剧；ECLARE在恢复小病灶信号方面表现最佳，而基于INR的方法不比三次插值更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLAIR扫描在临床中通常以厚层切片采集，面内分辨率差，超分辨率被广泛用于恢复各向同性体积，但尚不清楚在WMH分割前应用SR是否会保留病灶内容——模型可能擦除小的真实病灶或产生不存在的幻觉病灶，这一问题需要系统评估。

Method: 使用ADNI队列中29名个体的1mm各向同性高分辨率FLAIR扫描（由专家手动分割WMH作为金标准），将其退化为模拟的3mm和5mm面外采集。分别使用多对比隐式神经表示（INR）、单对比自监督模型（ECLARE）和三次插值进行上采样至HR网格。在四种WMH分割方法中选用对HR小病灶最敏感的MARS-WMH，采用检测灵敏度、擦除率（HR检测的病灶在重建后丢失的比例）和幻觉率（预测成分在手动和HR分割中均不存在）作为评价指标进行逐病灶分析。

Result: SR的主要效应是擦除小的真实病灶而非产生幻觉，且擦除程度随切片厚度增加而加剧；所有重建方法相对原始厚切片均改善了病灶检测。ECLARE在两种厚度下对恢复小病灶信号表现最佳，而INR方法并不优于三次插值。

Conclusion: 超分辨率重建在WMH分割前应用时应谨慎选择方法，ECLARE在小病灶保存方面优于更复杂的INR方法，提示需要针对病灶完整性的专门评估来指导SR方法的选择。

Abstract: White matter hyperintensities (WMH), bright regions on Fluid-attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) scans are associated with cerebrovascular pathology and neurodegeneration. FLAIR is usually acquired with thick slices in clinical settings, giving it poor through-plane resolution. Super-resolution (SR) is a widely used method for recovering an isotropic volume from an anisotropic scan. Yet whether applying it prior to WMH segmentation preserves lesion content remains unknown: a model may erase small real lesions or hallucinate absent ones. We used 1-mm isotropic high-resolution (HR) FLAIR scans from 29 individuals in the ADNI cohort, each manually segmented for WMH by an expert. Then, we degraded each to simulated 3 and 5 mm through-plane acquisitions. Multi-contrast implicit neural representation (INR), a single-contrast self-supervised model (ECLARE), and cubic interpolation were used to upsample them onto the HR grid. WMH segmentation from a simulated thick slice and the original HR FLAIR set the floor and ceiling, respectively, for the per-lesion analysis. Of four WMH segmentation methods (WMH-SynthSeg, segcsvd, MARS-WMH, TrUE-Net), we ran the analysis under the most sensitive one to small lesions on HR (MARS-WMH) with the evaluation metrics of detection sensitivity, erasure rate (HR-detected lesions lost after reconstruction), and hallucination rate (predicted components absent from both the manual and HR segmentation). The dominant effect of SR was erasure of small real lesions, not hallucination, and it increased with slice thickness, though every reconstruction still improved lesion detection over the raw thick slice. ECLARE recovered small lesion signal best at both thicknesses, while the INR was no better than cubic interpolation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Universal Pathologies, Conditional Consequences: A Triple-Robustness Analysis of RAG for Multi-Hop Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05153)
*Meftun Akarsu,Burak Ozdemir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"三重稳健性"分析框架（嵌入器 × 语料 × 判断器），在统一检索架构下对GraphRAG与向量RAG进行系统对比，发现过度引用是架构级普遍现象但忠实性后果因语料而异，并指出单判决模型的忠实性评估不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量报告显示GraphRAG在引用精度上不如向量RAG，但这种现象的成因和适用边界一直受语料限制，缺乏系统性、跨条件可控的对比分析。

Method: 固定检索架构不变，变化三个正交维度：嵌入器（e5-small vs text-embedding-3-small）、语料（DO-178C类型化边需求 vs Wikipedia段落链/MuSiQue）、判断器（GPT-5.4 × GPT-4.1配对）。共执行4,440次主矩阵运行、600次跨语料运行和1,200次配对忠实性判断，并使用基于稠密嵌入的学习路由器进行跳数分类。

Result: (C2a)过度引用在三种设置下均为架构级普遍现象，GraphRAG输出11-15个ID/答案，引用精度仅0.12-0.23，检索召回0.68-0.87；(C2b)忠实性后果因语料而异——DO-178C上跨跳忠实性从74%崩溃至40%，而Wikipedia链上从42%升至58%；(C1)条件胜出者随语料变化但对嵌入器稳健；(C3)单判决LLM忠实性评估脆弱（GPT-5.4自kappa仅0.137，41%项目改判）；(C4)学习路由器在稠密嵌入上宏观F1达0.86。

Conclusion: 三重稳健性分析是衡量RAG架构声明的可信度最低标准；GraphRAG的过度引用是普适的，但其对忠实性的影响高度依赖语料特性，评估忠实性时必须采用多判断器设置。

Abstract: GraphRAG underperforms vector RAG on citation precision in many reports, but where and why have remained corpus-bound. We present a triple-robustness analysis that holds the retrieval architecture fixed and varies three orthogonal axes embedder (local e5-small -> Azure text-embedding-3-small), corpus (DO-178C typed-edge requirements -> Wikipedia paragraph chains via MuSiQue), and judge (paired GPT-5.4 x GPT-4.1) across 4,440 main-matrix runs, 600 cross-corpus runs, and 1,200 paired faithfulness judgments. (C2a) Over-citation is architecturally universal: GraphRAG emits 11-15 IDs per answer at citation precision 0.12-0.23 and retrieval recall 0.68-0.87 across all three settings. (C2b) Its faithfulness consequence is corpus-conditional: in typed-edge DO-178C, GraphRAG faithfulness collapses 74%->40% across hops; on Wikipedia chains the same pipeline rises 42%->58% because over-cited paragraphs remain topically supporting. (C1) Stratum-conditional winners are corpus-conditional but embedder-robust: vanilla wins 2-hop on DO-178C, GraphRAG wins 2-hop on MuSiQue, identical under either embedder. (C3) Single-judge LLM faithfulness is fragile to retrieval state: same-judge self-kappa across embedders is 0.137 for GPT-5.4 (verdict change on 41% of items). A learned router on dense embeddings alone reaches macro-F1 0.86 on hop classification (C4). We argue triple-robustness is the minimum bar for trustworthy RAG architecture claims.

</details>


### [100] [RIG-RoPE: Relation- and Instance-Gated Rotary Positional Encoding with Duration-Aware Temporal Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05154)
*Donggen Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RIG-RoPE，一种关系与实例门控的旋转位置编码机制，旨在解决交错多模态上下文（如文本、图像、视频混合输入）中静态多维位置分配（如M-RoPE）的两个缺陷：跨模态/跨实例的空间干扰，以及等步长时间坐标与信息密度不匹配的问题。RIG-RoPE通过模态指示器、视觉实例标识符和时长感知坐标来门控H/W旋转并改进时间旋转，无需学习参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有M-RoPE等静态多维位置编码在交错多模态场景中存在两个关键问题：(1) 对空间位移无明确几何定义的token对（如跨模态或跨实例）仍施加高度/宽度旋转，导致空间语义干扰；(2) 时间坐标被简单视为等步长计数器，文本token、图像块和视频片段的信息密度不同却被赋予相近的时间相位推进，无法准确反映真实的时间/信息消耗关系。

Method: RIG-RoPE对每个token附加三个元数据：模态指示器、视觉实例标识符、标量信息时长坐标。具体机制包括：(1) 仅在查询-键对来自同一视觉实例时才应用H/W旋转，否则将未知空间位移做边缘化处理而非置零；(2) 时间旋转采用插值累积块时长——文本消耗单位时长，图像使用维度感知的对数空间尺度，视频在对数空间扩展基础上应用对数时间扩展；(3) 提供理论论证：避免跨实例空间旋转的规范不变性论证、静态ID在共享H/W子空间下的不可能性结果、以及反对等步长多模态时长的一致性论证。该方法可在tiled attention核内实现，每个token仅增加常量元数据开销。

Result: 本文是一份初步报告，建立了公式化和验证路径，并未声称经验性能优势。主要贡献在于：（1）识别M-RoPE静态多维位置编码在交错多模态场景的两个结构化缺陷；（2）提出RIG-RoPE作为无学习参数的修复方案；（3）提供三个理论支撑性论证（规范不变性、不可能性、时长一致性）；（4）说明在tiled attention核中实现只需每token常量附加元数据，计算开销可控。

Conclusion: RIG-RoPE通过关系门控（relation-gating）和实例门控（instance-gating）机制解决跨模态/跨实例的空间干扰，并通过时长感知的插值时间坐标解决信息密度与时间步长不匹配的问题。该工作为多模态位置编码提供了新的理论视角，但尚处于初步阶段，需要后续实验验证其在真实多模态任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Rotary positional encoding (RoPE) is a core component of modern language models and has been extended to multimodal LLMs through multidimensional variants such as multimodal RoPE (M-RoPE), which split positional channels into temporal, height, and width subspaces. This report identifies two limitations of static multidimensional position assignment in interleaved multimodal contexts. First, height/width rotations may be applied to token pairs whose spatial displacement is not a well-defined geometric object, producing cross-modal and inter-instance spatial interference. Second, temporal coordinates are often treated as equal-step counters, so a text token, an image block, and a video segment can advance the temporal phase by comparable amounts despite different information density.
  We propose RIG-RoPE, a relation- and instance-gated RoPE mechanism with duration-aware temporal coordinates. RIG-RoPE augments each token with a modality indicator, a visual instance identifier, and a scalar information-duration coordinate. It enables H/W rotations only for query-key pairs from the same visual instance; otherwise the unknown spatial displacement is marginalized rather than set to zero. Temporal rotations use interpolated cumulative block durations: text tokens consume unit duration, images use a dimension-aware logarithmic spatial scale, and videos further apply a logarithmic temporal extension over effective frames. We provide a gauge-invariance argument for avoiding ordinary cross-instance spatial rotation, an impossibility result for static IDs under shared H/W subspaces, and a duration-consistency argument against equal-step multimodal time. RIG-RoPE adds no learned parameters and can be implemented inside tiled attention kernels with constant additional metadata per token. This preliminary report establishes the formulation and validation path without claiming empirical superiority.

</details>


### [101] [Beyond Sentiment: Comparing Traditional NLP and LLM-Based Multi-Dimensional Analysis for Political News Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05155)
*Maryam Fooladi,Federico Bottino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于RoBERTa的传统情感分析与基于LLM的多维框架分析在50篇政治新闻文章语料上的表现，发现RoBERTa将70%的文章归类为中性（"中性塌缩"现象），而LLM方法能捕捉政治偏见方向与强度、煽情性、情感诉求和政治框架等多维信息，更符合社会科学与人文学科（SSH）的研究需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统情感分析模型虽然能有效进行极性分类，但在政治话语的修辞、意识形态和框架维度上提供的信息有限，而这些维度正是社会科学与人文学科研究的核心关注点。

Method: 对来自17家国际媒体的50篇政治新闻文章，分别应用RoBERTa-based情感分析和LLM-based多维框架分析平台，进行对比研究。

Result: RoBERTa将70%的文章归类为中性（中性塌缩），其中23%的中性分类文章负面概率得分超过0.30；相比之下，LLM方法能捕捉政治偏见、煽情性、情感诉求和框架等多维分析输出。

Conclusion: 对于政治媒体分析，传统情感分析单独使用不够充分，基于LLM的多维分析框架为SSH研究提供了更具认识论适配性的计算视角。

Abstract: Traditional sentiment analysis (SA) models, while effective for polarity classification, provide limited insight into the rhetorical, ideological, and framing dimensions of political discourse -- dimensions that are central to research in the social sciences and humanities (SSH).
