We introduce Synthetic Query Probing, generating queries from documents to create controlled query-chunk pairs, enabling large-scale, reference-free analysis of cross-model similarity behavior. We evaluate the approach on multiple embedding configurations and learn score conversion functions using linear, isotonic, and quantile mappings. Experiments on SciFact and a proprietary corpus show that while models largely agree on rankings, their absolute scores exhibit systematic distortions. Learned mappings partially align these spaces and improve threshold portability, with isotonic regression performing best. Our results highlight the need for cross-model calibration and position Synthetic Query Probing as a scalable framework for analyzing embedding comparability.

</details>


### [139] [MACRO: Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05872)
*Paweł Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Akylgali Aitaza,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MACRO框架，将Transformer层的动态路由建模为上下文相关的马尔可夫策略，在不修改底层参数的前提下，通过训练数据反馈学习任务特定路由，并使用top-k Viterbi算法高效解码，在多个LLM和基准上平均提升5.0%准确率，比现有最优动态路由方法Dr. LLM提升7.2%，同时路由搜索时间降低9.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM按顺序执行各层，但动态层路由（如重复、跳过、移动层）可以提升性能。现有路由方法存在三个主要问题：需要更新模型权重、每个测试实例需要昂贵的搜索循环、或推理时需要真实标签。因此需要一种不修改参数、高效且无需标签的路由方法。

Method: MACRO将层路由建模为上下文相关的马尔可夫策略，条件包括层索引、计算预算阶段、方向位移和算子上下文，支持跳过、重复和残差隐状态加法操作。路由分布通过训练数据的反馈进行更新，并使用top-k Viterbi算法解码出高概率候选程序。

Result: 在多个开放权重LLM及推理和知识基准上，MACRO相比未路由基线平均提升+5.0%准确率，小模型受益最大；相比最优动态路由方法Dr. LLM提升+7.2%，路由搜索时间从14.8小时降至1.6小时，降低9.4倍。

Conclusion: MACRO证明了无需修改模型参数即可通过马尔可夫策略学习高效的任务特定层路由，兼顾性能提升和搜索效率，为LLM动态路由提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard Large Language Models (LLMs) execute layers sequentially. Dynamic layer routing, i.e. search for a different execution path through layers involving layer repetitions, skips and other moves, can improve performance. Existing routing approaches often require updating model weights, running expensive search loops per test instance, or demand ground-truth labels during inference. In this work, we propose Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers (MACRO), a framework that learns task-specific routes over LLM architectures without modifying underlying parameters. MACRO models layer routing as a context-dependent Markov policy conditioned on layer indices, computation budget phases, directional displacements, and operator context, supporting skip, repeat, and residual hidden-state addition operations. The Markov route distribution is updated via feedback on training data and decoded using a top-k Viterbi algorithm to isolate high-probability candidate programs. We evaluate MACRO across diverse reasoning and knowledge benchmarks on multiple open-weight LLMs. MACRO achieves a +5.0% average accuracy improvement over the unrouted baselines, with largest gains on small models. We outperform the best dynamic routing approach Dr. LLM by +7.2%, while reducing route-search time 9.4x (from 14.8 to 1.6 hours). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/MACRO.

</details>


### [140] [Causal Episodic Memory for Feedback-Driven Agent Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05906)
*Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Tam Minh Chu,Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Thuyen Vinh Ha Bui,Tho Quan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT，一种免训练的智能体框架，通过在线双极性记忆库（存储Oracle验证的成功修正和失败的探索方向）来改进Text-to-SQL修复任务。实验表明，与无状态迭代修复相比，MERIT在Spider上将执行准确率从66.34%提升至69.79%，在BIRD上从47.35%提升至48.44%，但统计分析显示Spider上的提升具有更强证据，而BIRD上的提升证据较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体在修复SQL生成失败时经常丢弃成功的修正经验，导致后续对话轮次需要重新发现类似解决方案。作者希望在不进行参数更新的前提下，探究最终的修复结果（而非中途的推理轨迹）能否作为跨查询（cross-query）经验来提升后续的Text-to-SQL修复表现。

Method: MERIT包含三个关键组件：(1)在线双极性记忆库，存储Oracle验证过的成功修正（正向记忆）和观察到的失败方向（负向记忆），只允许从早期已完成episode中检索记忆，避免测试泄漏；(2)确定性分类器对失败类型进行粗粒度划分，作为检索的条件上下文；(3)混合词法-稠密检索器（hybrid lexical-dense retriever）在冻结模型生成每个修订版本前从记忆中检索相关经验。整个流程无需任何参数更新。

Result: 使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、相同初始预测和修复预算的条件下，MERIT在Spider上从66.34%提升至69.79%，在BIRD上从47.35%提升至48.44%。成对分析显示Spider上的提升有Clear证据，BIRD上的提升证据较弱。MERIT与无类型条件的动态检索在统计上无法可靠区分。对比Reflexion风格记忆在BIRD上达到51.24%但推理成本显著更高。消融实验表明负向记忆贡献适中，类型条件和词法-稠密排序的价值因数据集而异，schema局部经验提供最一致的收益。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了因果性跨查询记忆在修复任务中的适用边界：在语法/模式错误占主导的数据集（如Spider）上，带类型条件的双极性记忆有显著帮助；但在更复杂、更多样化的数据集（如BIRD）上，更广泛的无类型动态记忆表示仍然更优。这为设计高效的Text-to-SQL修复记忆机制提供了参考。

Abstract: LLM agents that repair failures often discard successful corrections, forcing later episodes to rediscover similar solutions. We study whether finalized repair outcomes can improve subsequent Text-to-SQL episodes without parameter updates. We introduce MERIT, a training-free agent that maintains an online dual-polarity memory of oracle-verified corrections and observed unsuccessful directions. Under oracle-assisted benchmark feedback, only memories from earlier finalized episodes are eligible for retrieval. A deterministic classifier assigns a coarse failure type, which conditions a hybrid lexical-dense retriever before the frozen model generates each revision. Using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with identical initial predictions and repair budgets, MERIT improves execution accuracy over stateless iterative repair from \(66.34\%\) to \(69.79\%\) on Spider and from \(47.35\%\) to \(48.44\%\) on BIRD. Paired analyses provide clear evidence for the Spider gain but weaker evidence on BIRD. MERIT is not reliably separated from untyped dynamic retrieval on either benchmark, while Reflexion-style memory reaches \(51.24\%\) on BIRD at substantially higher inference cost. Ablations show that negative memory contributes modestly, the value of type conditioning and lexical--dense ranking is dataset dependent, and schema-local experience provides the most consistent benefit. These results clarify when causal cross-query memory improves repair and when broader memory representations remain preferable.

</details>


### [141] [Clinical Communication Processing with Models Trained on LLM-Generated Synthetic Data: A Structured Survey and Novel Application Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05993)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大型语言模型将临床数据源（如病历、诊断标签、症状列表、护理计划）转化为合成临床沟通文本的方法，用于为缺乏标注真实数据的临床NLP系统进行引导式训练，并通过13个新颖案例验证了该方法在不同沟通形式和语言上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量临床价值蕴含在医患沟通、医护交接等非结构化语言中，而非结构化表格数据。然而真实临床对话涉及隐私、碎片化和高标注成本，导致高质量的标注语料严重缺乏，制约了医疗NLP对真实沟通信息的理解。

Method: 作者提出结构化叙事综述方法：按源表示（source representation）、沟通形式与参与者、生成方法和下游任务四个维度组织文献，并辅以13个原创案例研究，利用LLM将临床数据源转化为书面或转录形式的合成沟通文本，用于构建低资源临床NLP系统。

Result: 案例覆盖EMS院前报告、野战电台伤员记录、护士交接班、患者门户分诊和低资源出院沟通等场景，结果表明合成沟通能有效引导系统构建；微调编码器模型优于评估的零样本基线，且刻意引入退化的沟通对鲁棒性有增益。

Conclusion: 合成临床沟通正成为实用的研究资源，但大多数研究仅在合成保留集上评估，真实迁移证据有限。将其确立为可复用的临床基础设施仍需真实数据迁移、安全性和外部验证。

Abstract: Much clinical value is conveyed not through structured records but through communication: exchanges in which patients describe symptoms, clinicians reason and give instructions, ambulances hand over to emergency departments, and nurses pass on a shift. Such language differs from tabular data because meaning depends on speaker role, intent, causality, uncertainty, omission, and channel noise. Healthcare natural language processing must therefore interpret information as conveyed rather than coded. This requires well-annotated corpora, which are scarce because authentic exchanges are private, fragmented, and costly to annotate. Large language models offer a way forward by transforming clinical sources, such as records, diagnostic labels, symptom lists, or care plans, into written and transcribed communication for downstream models. We present a structured narrative survey organized by source representation, communication form and participants, generation method, and downstream task, complemented by thirteen novel case studies. These build clinical NLP systems for communication channels and languages without labeled real-world data, including EMS pre-arrival reports, field-radio casualty documentation, nurse handoffs, patient-portal triage, and low-resource discharge communication. They show that synthetic communication can bootstrap such systems. Findings include the competitiveness of fine-tuned encoder models over evaluated zero-shot baselines and the value of deliberately degraded communication for robustness. The main limitation is that most studies evaluate on held-out synthetic communication, while train-on-synthetic, test-on-authentic evidence remains limited. We conclude that syn-thetic clinical communication is becoming a practical research resource; establishing it as reusable clinical infrastructure will require authentic-data transfer, safety and external validation.

</details>


### [142] [EpiBench: Can LLMs Understand Epitopes for Antibody Drug Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06022)
*Zirui Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Qinghan Wang,Yuzhi Xu,Gang Du,Tingjun Hou,Odin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EpiBench，一个基于序列的闭卷基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在抗体表位推理方面的能力，包含1,609个样本和五个相互关联的任务，揭示了现有LLMs在表位预测上仅捕获部分信号但仍存在抗体序列定位、长上下文残基定位和生物学推理方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表位决定抗体结合位点并影响治疗性质，但现有资源缺乏对LLMs表位推理能力的系统性评估。已有的表位资源聚焦于孤立预测任务或依赖结构设置，通用蛋白基准不覆盖表位中心的决策，因此需要一个面向LLM的表位推理基准。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准，包含1,609个基于结构接触、功能B细胞实验和深度突变扫描逃逸数据筛选的样本，覆盖五个任务：靶向区域发现、抗体条件表位识别、表位分箱、功能表位评估和抗体逃逸评估，并通过受控采样减少捷径伪影。对九个通用LLM进行评估，结合任务基线、抗原长度分层、显式推理对比和失败模式分析。

Result: 九个通用LLM在表位推理任务上捕获了部分相关信号，但在抗体特异性序列接地、长上下文残基定位和生物学接地推理方面仍存在明显局限。

Conclusion: EpiBench可作为测量和提升序列感知生物医学LLMs的诊断测试平台，为可靠地实现LLM辅助抗体发现铺平道路。

Abstract: Epitopes determine where antibodies bind antigens and shape downstream therapeutic properties such as functional blockade and escape resistance, making epitope understanding central to antibody drug discovery. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong biomedical reasoning ability, it remains unclear whether they can infer epitope information directly from antigen and antibody sequences. Existing epitope resources typically focus on isolated prediction tasks or rely on specialized structural settings, while general protein benchmarks do not evaluate epitope-centered decisions across the antibody development workflow. To address this gap, we introduce EpiBench, a closed-book, sequence-based, and automatically scorable benchmark for evaluating epitope reasoning in LLMs. EpiBench contains 1,609 curated samples grounded in structural antibody--antigen contacts, curated functional B-cell assays, and deep mutational scanning escape measurements. It covers five connected tasks: targetable region discovery, antibody-conditioned epitope identification, epitope binning, functional epitope assessment, and antibody escape assessment, with controlled sampling to reduce shortcut-based evaluation artifacts. We evaluate nine general-purpose LLMs and analyze their behavior through task-specific baselines, antigen length stratification, explicit-reasoning comparison, and failure-mode inspection. The results show that current LLMs capture partial epitope-related signals but remain limited in antibody-specific sequence grounding, long-context residue localization, and biologically grounded reasoning. Therefore, EpiBench provides a diagnostic testbed for measuring and improving sequence-aware biomedical LLMs toward reliable LLM-assisted antibody discovery.

</details>


### [143] [FormBharo: Designing and Evaluating a Voice Agent for Conversational Form Filling in Rural India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06027)
*Aman Dalmia,Sanskriti Midha,Jigar Doshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FormBharo，一个用于印度低收入人群语音入档的语音代理系统，它将LLM与确定性规则验证和流程控制相结合，在严格的延迟和成本预算下通过电话完成结构化表单填写。该代理已在ARMMAN的母婴健康项目中进行试点，作者还发布了包含3,760个多轮对话测试的FormVoiceAgentBench基准，发现端到端评估与组件级性能存在显著差异，并采用Pareto加权和方法选择最优部署配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在印度，几乎所有社会福利都始于表格填写，但最需要这些福利的人群往往不识字。现有的面对面手动登记方式依赖一线卫生工作者逐人办理，效率低下且浪费有限的资源。因此需要一种通过语音对话来帮助文盲或半文盲人群填写表格的自动化方案。

Method: 构建FormBharo语音代理，通过电话通话实现结构化表单填写。核心方法是将LLM与确定性、基于规则的验证和流程控制相结合：规则层负责验证LLM的提取结果并控制对话流程，在严格的延迟和成本约束下运行。同时构建了FormVoiceAgentBench基准，使用人工录制的印地语音频和960个模拟调用中的3,760个多轮对话测试来评估转录、提取、回复生成等组件及端到端表单完成效果。最后采用Pareto加权和标量化方法在准确率、成本和延迟之间进行多目标权衡，选择可部署的配置。

Result: 当LLM接收到带错误的口语转录而非参考转录时，表单完成率最多下降约41个百分点。基于规则的流程控制能够恢复许多回合级提取错误，使较小且更便宜的模型在表单完成上与前沿模型匹配甚至超越。组件性能不能预测端到端性能：GPT-5.5在参考转录上的回合级提取准确率最高（99.8%），但在表单完成上排名较低。由于错误在流水线中既会传播也会相互抵消，最佳的模型选择只能通过端到端评估得出。没有任何单一模型能同时在准确率、成本和延迟上最优，因此采用Pareto加权和方法选择可部署配置。

Conclusion: 端到端评估对于语音代理系统至关重要，组件级指标可能产生误导；规则制的流程控制能显著弥补LLM在错误转录下的不足；在真实部署中需要考虑准确率、成本和延迟的多目标权衡，而非仅追求单一指标最优。

Abstract: In India, almost every social benefit starts with a form, yet the people who need these benefits most are often unable to read or write. Reaching them requires a spoken conversation. Today that work falls to frontline health workers who enroll beneficiaries one at a time, a poor use of stretched capacity. We built FormBharo ("fill the form" in Hindi), a voice agent that fills a structured form over a phone call under tight latency and cost budgets by pairing Large Language Models (LLMs) with deterministic, rule-based validation and flow control. It is being piloted with ARMMAN, an NGO running large-scale maternal and child mobile-health programs in India, to enroll low-income, Hindi-speaking mothers in antenatal and postnatal care. To our knowledge, it is the first voice agent piloted to fill an enrollment form for this population. We openly release FormVoiceAgentBench, a benchmark pairing human-recorded Hindi audio with 3,760 multi-turn conversation tests across 960 simulated calls, to evaluate our agent's components (transcription, extraction, reply generation) and end-to-end form completion under real acoustic variations. Form completion drops by up to ~41 points when LLMs receive error-prone real-speech transcripts instead of reference ones. The rule-based controls recover many turn-level extraction errors, helping smaller, cheaper models match or surpass frontier models on form completion. Component performance does not predict end-to-end performance: GPT-5.5 leads turn-level extraction accuracy on reference transcripts (99.8%) but ranks lower on form completion. Since errors both propagate and cancel across the pipeline, the optimal model choice of models emerges only through end-to-end evaluation. Finally, no single model is best across accuracy, cost, and latency at once, so we use a Pareto-based weighted-sum scalarization to select a deployable configuration balancing the three.

</details>


### [144] [Training-Free Token-Level Steering for LLM Personalized Co-Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06069)
*Wenhao Mao,Chengbin Hou,Weixiao Wang,Jialiang Zhu,Min Liu,Yibin Hao,Hairong Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SteerWrite是一个无需训练的个性化协同写作框架，通过无梯度方法将基础大语言模型适配到专业领域，在小数据集上表现优异，达到最新技术水平并显著减少人工编辑工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在个性化方面有潜力但缺乏专业领域知识；传统微调方法计算成本高且难以应对快速数据更新，检索增强生成无法提供细粒度token级引导，且聊天式界面未充分挖掘协同写作范式。

Method: 提出SteerWrite，一种训练-free的框架，无需梯度更新即可将基础模型适配到专业领域，并针对小数据集进行了专门设计，实现token级别的细粒度引导。

Result: 在多样化数据集、指标和模型上达到最新技术水平，显著减少人工编辑工作量。

Conclusion: SteerWrite证明无需梯度更新的无训练框架能够有效实现个性化协同写作，兼顾领域适配能力与计算效率，是传统微调和RAG方案的有效替代。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for personalization, they often lack specialized domain knowledge. Conventional solutions like fine-tuning struggle with high computational costs and rapid data updates, while Retrieval-Augmented Generation fails to provide fine-grained, token-level steering. Furthermore, chat-based interfaces remain dominant, whereas productive co-writing paradigms have not yet been well exploited beyond the coding domain. To this end, we introduce SteerWrite, a training-free framework designed for personalized co-writing. Our method effectively adapts the base model to specialized domains without gradient updates, with specific designs tailored to small datasets. Experiments demonstrate that SteerWrite achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse datasets, metrics, and models, significantly reducing human editing effort.

</details>


### [145] [Beyond Sequence Order: Syntax-Informed Positional Embeddings for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06111)
*Haris Riaz,Hyungji Kim,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语法信息位置嵌入（SiPE），在预训练阶段从依存句法解析中学习轻量级语法先验，并将其注入绝对、相对、旋转三种主流位置嵌入族中（适用于编码器和解码器），在不改动自注意力机制和其余架构的前提下提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer位置嵌入主要用于编码token距离和顺序，但基本不感知句法结构。尽管已有诸多语法注入方法，但往往以退化困惑度（perplexity）为代价，且多数在推理阶段需要边缘化多个句法树或丢弃语法信息，造成监督与推理成本的矛盾。

Method: SiPE在预训练阶段从依存句法解析中学习轻量级语法先验，并注入三种主流位置嵌入家族（绝对、相对、旋转）。研究发现注入位置依赖于架构：对于使用相对PE的自回归解码器，将语法先验与注意力分数中的相对位置项乘法耦合效果最佳；对于编码器，则直接将先验加到输入嵌入中与原生位置机制组合效果最好。

Result: SiPE在SyntaxGym基准上提升高达10.3%，同时将困惑度降低9.0%（对比无语法监督的基线模型）；在GLUE基准上提升高达8.2%。此外，SiPE在推理时仅需依赖单个句法树，而非像已有方法那样边缘化多个解析结果，建立了语法监督与推理成本之间的新的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 语法信息可以通过轻量级位置嵌入先验有效地注入Transformer模型，且注入方式应根据架构（编码器vs解码器）和PE类型进行定制。SiPE在不损害甚至改善困惑度的情况下提升句法泛化与真实语言理解能力，并以极低的推理开销实现语法监督，优于现有语法注入方法。

Abstract: Positional embeddings (PE) in Transformers encode token distance and order but are largely agnostic to \textit{syntactic structure}. We introduce \textbf{S}yntax-\textbf{i}nformed \textbf{P}ositional \textbf{E}mbeddings (\textbf{SiPE}), which learns a lightweight syntactic prior from dependency parses during pretraining and injects it across all three dominant PE families (absolute, relative, rotary), for both encoders and decoders, leaving self-attention and the rest of the architecture untouched. We isolate \emph{where} and \emph{how} the prior should enter the model, and find it depends on the architecture: for autoregressive decoders that use relative PE, the prior is strongest when coupled multiplicatively with the relative-position term of the attention score, outperforming injection into the input embeddings, into self-attention, or into the positional and attention terms jointly---while for encoders it is best added directly to the input embeddings, composing with each encoder's native positional mechanism. We find that models pre-trained with SiPE improve on the SyntaxGym benchmark by up to $10.3\%$ while simultaneously reducing perplexity by $9.0\%$ over a base model with no syntactic supervision---a metric nearly every existing syntax-injection method instead degrades. Crucially, these gains extend beyond syntactic generalization: SiPE also improves real-world language understanding, raising scores on the GLUE benchmark by up to $8.2\%$ over a model trained without it. Unlike existing syntactic language models that marginalize over many parses at inference or discard syntax at runtime, SiPE conditions on a single parse, establishing a new Pareto frontier between syntactic supervision and inference cost.

</details>


### [146] [Decolonizing Linguistic Policies in Automated Speech Recognition: A Framework for Cross-Culturally Competent Speech AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06141)
*Jay L. Cunningham,Mark Atta Mensah,Richard Martinez,Joao Vieira da Silva Neto,Efi Dawodu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从语言学政策与社会公平视角审视自动语音识别（ASR）及其语音接口对公共服务、医疗和教育等领域的影响，提出低资源、原住民和非标准语言变体的持续性识别失败不仅是技术错误，更是隐性语言政策，并引入3M伤害分类法、七层情境化模型以及参与式审计框架来推动文化胜任的ASR设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对ASR系统在低资源、原住民和非标准语言变体上持续失效的问题，指出该问题不仅是技术层面缺陷，更反映了殖民语言等级制度的隐性延续，亟需从语言政策、种族语言意识形态和去殖民计算等理论视角进行系统性批判与重构。

Method: 本文采用跨学科理论分析方法，整合语言资本、种族语言意识形态、语言政策研究及去殖民计算框架；引入三H（3M）伤害分类法（误识别、错位、不信任）和七层情境化模型来系统刻画ASR语言多样性问题；提出参与式框架与最低审计协议，将受影响社区定位为共同设计者、评估者和治理伙伴。

Result: 构建了3M伤害分类法与七层情境化模型，阐明了数据、指标和模型先验如何决定哪些声音可被机器识别，并提出了面向文化胜任ASR的参与式框架与最低审计协议。

Conclusion: ASR系统对边缘化语言群体的失败应被视为隐性语言政策而非孤立技术缺陷，需通过将受影响社区纳入共同设计、评估与治理环节，推动更具文化胜任力与公平性的语音技术发展。

Abstract: This paper focuses on automatic speech recognition (ASR) and ASR-mediated voice interfaces that shape access to public services, healthcare, and education. We argue that persistent failures for low-resource, Indigenous, and non-standard language varieties are not only technical errors, but also implicit linguistic policies that reproduce colonial language hierarchies. Drawing on linguistic capital, raciolinguistic ideology, language policy research, and decolonial computing, we show how data, metrics, and model priors determine whose voices become machine-legible. We introduce the Three Harms (3M) taxonomy---Misrecognition, Misalignment, and Mistrust---and a seven-layer situatedness model for linguistic diversity in ASR and ASR-mediated voice interfaces. We then propose a participatory framework and minimum audit protocol for culturally competent ASR, positioning affected communities as co-designers, evaluators, and governance partners.

</details>


### [147] [Routing Is Least Learnable Where It Is Most Valuable: Bounds on Representation Routing for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06171)
*Jiaming Wei,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama,Maria Perez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统测量了Web代理在不同任务中观察模式的性能，发现六种观察模式（文本、像素或两者结合）具有互补性，每次任务选择不同的模式可能带来收益。然而，通过oracle选择最佳模式的收益被运行噪声夸大（12-14%的结果变化），实际只有成本约束有效（在8/8单元中降低成本9.5-30.6%而不损失成功率）。测试的五种路由策略均无法稳定超越固定选择单一良好模式，根本障碍在于路由监督信号受限于代理自身成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web代理通常为所有任务固定选择一种观察模式（文本、像素或两者），但不同任务可能需要不同的观察模式。作者希望量化跨任务动态选择观察模式（routing）能带来多少收益，并探究其可行性。

Method: 在VisualWebArena和WebArena的8个站点-模型组合（cells）上测量六种观察模式；通过oracle分析理想路由上限；区分运行噪声与真实收益；测试五种路由策略（模式选择、强模式成本决策、零成本规则、置信度级联、池化成本分层）；分析路由监督信号与代理成功率的关系。

Result: 六种观察模式具有互补性（每种模式能解决其他模式不能解决的任务）；oracle收益被噪声夸大（重跑同一模式改变12-14%的结果）；成本约束在8/8单元中节省成本9.5-30.6%且不损失成功率；五种路由策略均不能稳健超越固定选择单一良好模式（一个稀疏单元出现脆弱例外）；路由监督供应与代理成功率高度相关（相关系数0.95）。

Conclusion: 当前代理能力下，任务的观察模式路由无法稳健超越固定策略。核心障碍是路由监督信号由代理自身生成，代理越弱则路由标签越少，而这恰恰是路由最有价值的场景。该限制属于当前代理而非路由本身——更强的代理会带来更多监督标签和更大的路由机会，可能颠覆这一结论。

Abstract: Web agents observe a browser through text, pixels, or both, and the choice is usually fixed once for all tasks. We measure six observation modes across eight site-model combinations (cells) on VisualWebArena and WebArena and ask what choosing per task would buy. The modes are complementary: each solves tasks the others miss, they fail in structurally different ways, and the best choice reverses between task sets. The obvious prize, an oracle that picks a winning mode for every task, looks large but is inflated by run-to-run noise: rerunning the same mode on the same tasks changes 12-14% of outcomes, so a second run of a mode already in hand gains about as much as adding a new one. What survives is a cost bound: sending only the tasks no mode solves to the cheapest mode cuts cost by 9.5-30.6% in 8 of 8 cells at unchanged success. We then test five routing policies (picking the mode, deciding when to spend on the strong mode, a zero-cost rule read off the task text, a confidence cascade, and pooled cost tiers), and none robustly beats simply fixing one well-chosen mode; the one exception is a fragile result in our sparsest cell. The central obstruction is that routing supervision is produced at the agent's success rate: the weaker the agent, the fewer labels a router gets, exactly where routing would be most valuable. This limit belongs to today's agents rather than to routing itself. Label supply and routing opportunity rise together (correlation 0.95 across cells), so a stronger agent can overturn the result, and we report the rerun noise bands and the full measurement protocol.

</details>


### [148] [NeSy-RAG: Neuro-Symbolic RAG for Explainable Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06292)
*Jonas Gann,Michael Gertz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NeSy-RAG，一种模块化神经符号RAG框架，将检索到的文本块综合为可归因的Prolog模块，通过谓词检索与组合生成Prolog查询，执行后获得确定性答案和透明推理链，并引入符号化知识缺口检测来识别缺失的用户事实，在ShARC基准上以61.1%准确率显著超越相同模型的RAG基线（42.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的推理过程不透明，中间推理步骤难以验证且无法可靠归因到具体证据；同时用户特定上下文的缺失很少被系统性检测，常导致不完整或错误的输出。

Method: NeSy-RAG包含三个核心组件：(1) 从检索到的文本块合成可归因的Prolog模块，每块生成编码布尔声明的语义谓词（可依赖用户事实）；(2) 使用联合自然语言-代码嵌入进行谓词检索与组合，形成Prolog查询；(3) 符号化知识缺口检测机制，识别影响查询结果的缺失用户事实并自动触发后续交互。执行Prolog查询产生确定性答案及链接到来源的透明执行轨迹。

Result: 在ShARC基准上，无需领域特定训练，NeSy-RAG达到61.1%准确率，优于相同模型的RAG基线（42.8%准确率）。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能有效提升RAG系统的推理可解释性和可归因性，且通过识别缺失的用户上下文实现系统性知识缺口检测，在性能上显著优于传统RAG基线。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves question answering by grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge such as text corpora. However, its reasoning process remains largely opaque: intermediate reasoning steps are difficult to verify and cannot be reliably attributed to specific evidence. Moreover, missing user-specific context is rarely detected systematically, often leading to incomplete or incorrect output.
  We propose NeSy-RAG, a modular neuro-symbolic RAG framework that synthesizes attributable Prolog modules from retrieved text chunks. For each chunk, the system generates semantically meaningful predicates that encode Boolean claims, which may depend on user facts. Using joint natural language-code embeddings, predicates are retrieved and composed into Prolog queries. To address incomplete user context, we introduce a symbolic knowledge-gap detection mechanism that identifies missing user facts whose truth values affect the query outcome and automatically triggers follow-up interactions.
  Executing the resulting Prolog queries yields deterministic answers together with transparent execution traces that link each reasoning step to its originating source. On the ShARC benchmark, without domain-specific training, NeSy-RAG achieves 61.1% accuracy, outperforming a same-model RAG baseline that achieves 42.8% accuracy.

</details>


### [149] [Benchmarking and Enhancing LLMs for Rule-Intensive Review of National Standard Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06312)
*Tao Wang,Qihao Yang,Rongjiao Liang,Lianghong Lin,Haitao Wang,Xinyu Cao,Tianyong Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GB/T-Bench，首个针对国家标准文档结构化审查的基准测试集，包含分级审查分类体系（25种可诊断错误类型）和可控反例生成机制，构建了4,880条审查任务记录（含7,306个可追溯错误实例）。同时提出GB/T-Reviewer多智能体框架，实验显示最强LLM仅达到0.3280 CMCS（专家为0.6640），而GB/T-Reviewer将该指标提升至0.5094，充分验证了结构化技能协作用于规则密集型文档审查的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在复杂专业任务中日益普及，但其在规则密集型文档审查方面的能力尚未得到充分评估。现有基准聚焦于领域知识和问答，忽视了专业文档的内在质量审查。国家标准文档（如GB/T标准）具有篇幅长、结构性强、规则明确等特点，是理想的测试场景，但传统审查依赖人类专家，成本高且难以规模化。因此需要构建一个专门针对国家标准文档结构化审查的基准来弥合这一空白。

Method: 1) 构建GB/T审查分类体系（GB/T Review Taxonomy），涵盖文档结构、范围一致性、规范性语体、术语一致性和规范性引用五个维度，定义25种可诊断错误类型；2) 设计可控反例生成机制，结合确定性规则和受限LLM重写，从488份文档生成7,306个可追溯的审查错误实例；3) 开发面向诊断的评估协议，要求错误位置、审查维度和错误类型三重精确匹配，并引入文档级覆盖率指标CMCS；4) 提出GB/T-Reviewer多智能体框架，将审查知识转化为专门技能，协调全局检查、定向诊断、规则扫描和结果验证四个环节。

Result: 对14个主流LLM的实验表明，人类专家与LLM之间存在显著差距：最强模型仅达到0.3280的CMCS（复合匹配综合评分），而人类专家达到0.6640。引入GB/T-Reviewer框架后，最佳模型的CMCS提升至0.5094，证明了结构化技能协同对规则密集型文档审查的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为标准化及其他高风险文档领域的可信AI铺平了道路。GB/T-Bench和GB/T-Reviewer共同展示了规则密集型文档审查中结构化技能协作的重要价值，揭示了当前LLM在此类任务上与人力的差距，并为未来的改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly support complex professional tasks, yet their capabilities in rule-intensive document review remain insufficiently evaluated. National standard documents, such as China GB/T standards, offer a representative testbed: they are lengthy, highly structured, and governed by explicit rules for scope, terminology, normative wording, and cross-section consistency. Existing benchmarks focus on domain knowledge and question answering, largely overlooking intrinsic quality review for professional documents. Such reviews rely heavily on human experts, making them costly and difficult to scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce GB/T-Bench, the first benchmark for the structured review of national standard documents. Its GB/T Review Taxonomy is a hierarchical schema covering document structure, scope alignment, normative modality, terminology consistency, and normative references, with 25 diagnosable error types. A controllable counterexample generation mechanism combines deterministic rules and constrained LLM rewriting to process 488 documents into 7,306 traceable review error instances for evaluation. We also develop a diagnosis-oriented evaluation protocol requiring exact matches on error location, review dimension, and error type, plus document-level coverage metrics. We further propose GB/T-Reviewer, a multi-agent framework that converts review knowledge into specialized skills and coordinates global inspection, targeted diagnosis, rule scanning, and result verification. Experiments with 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a substantial human-LLM gap: the strongest model achieves only 0.3280 CMCS versus 0.6640 for experts. GB/T-Reviewer raises the best CMCS to 0.5094, showing the value of structured skill coordination for rule-intensive document review. This work paves the way for trustworthy AI in standardization and other high-stakes document domains.

</details>


### [150] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Benchmarks for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06329)
*Noam Koren,Roy Bar-Haim,Abigail Goldsteen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无参考框架，利用LLM裁判评估任务型对话代理基准的质量，从一致性、复杂性和策略覆盖率三个维度进行度量，并提供可操作的弱点诊断。实验表明该框架与人工注释一致，能区分不同质量的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务型对话代理使用精选或自动生成的基准进行评测，但基准本身的质量很少被评估。低质量基准可能包含不一致的任务、过于简单的场景或有限的策略覆盖，导致评测结果不可靠。

Method: 提出一个参考无关框架，使用LLM作为裁判来评估基准的一致性（consistency）、复杂性（complexity）和策略覆盖率（policy coverage），同时提供可操作的弱点诊断。通过人工注释验证、不同能力LLM生成的基准对比，以及受控质量退化扰动实验来验证框架有效性。

Result: 在多个领域和不同裁判模型下，所提出的指标能一致地区分基准质量水平。框架与独立人工注释达成一致，并能适用于人工精选的基准。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估合成和人工精选的对话代理基准提供了一种实用方法，有助于确保评测工具本身的质量和可靠性。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents are evaluated using curated or automatically generated benchmarks, yet benchmark quality is rarely assessed. Poor benchmarks may contain inconsistent tasks, simplistic scenarios, or limited policy coverage, leading to unreliable evaluations. We introduce a reference-free framework that uses LLM judges to assess benchmark consistency, complexity, and policy coverage, while providing actionable diagnostics of weaknesses. We validate the framework by demonstrating agreement with independent human annotations and by evaluating benchmarks generated by LLMs of varying capabilities, as well as benchmarks subjected to controlled quality-degrading perturbations. Across domains and judge models, the proposed metrics consistently distinguish between benchmark quality levels. We further demonstrate the framework's applicability to manually curated benchmarks. Our framework offers a practical approach for evaluating synthetic and manually curated conversational-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [151] [RP-OPSD: Reasoning-Pivot-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multilingual Reasoning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06347)
*Xinye Wang,Junxiao Liu,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RP-OPSD（推理枢轴引导的在线策略自蒸馏），通过识别目标语言推理中的"推理枢轴"（推动或改变推理方向的关键决策），利用有无英语参考答案时教师视图的分布差异作为代理信号，对这些关键token进行特权蒸馏和参考锚定，在17种语言的数学推理基准上超越了现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言推理迁移对于将LLM的推理能力扩展到低资源语言至关重要。虽然OPSD及其变体为学生生成的rollout提供了稠密的token级监督，但其目标函数没有显式优先考虑对跨语言推理迁移最关键的信息。作者认为目标语言推理包含表面文本生成和推理枢轴生成两部分，而推理枢轴决定了后续推理方向，因此需要将特权蒸馏集中在这些关键决策点上。

Method: RP-OPSD的核心方法是：1）将目标语言推理视为表面文本与推理枢轴的组合；2）使用配对的教师视图（有英语参考答案vs无英语参考答案）之间的分布差异作为推理枢轴的运行时代理信号；3）基于该信号引导特权蒸馏（privileged distillation）和参考锚定（reference anchoring），从而对推理关键token进行更密集的监督。

Result: 在覆盖17种语言、多个难度级别的数学推理基准上，RP-OPSD优于强多语言推理基线和OPSD变体。进一步分析表明，RP-OPSD将特权蒸馏集中于推理控制token和问题条件化的状态更新token，同时降低了主要用于表面实现的token的监督权重。

Conclusion: RP-OPSD验证了推理枢轴在多语言推理迁移中的核心作用，通过显式建模推理关键决策点并集中蒸馏资源，有效提升了跨语言数学推理性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning transfer is crucial for extending reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) beyond high-resource languages. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) and its variants have emerged as a promising paradigm, providing dense token-level supervision on student-generated rollouts, yet their objectives do not explicitly prioritize reasoning signals most critical to cross-lingual transfer. We characterize that target-language reasoning comprises the generation of both surface text and reasoning pivots, which are decisions that advance or redirect the reasoning process and shape subsequent inference. This motivates concentrating privileged distillation around such pivots. We therefore propose RP-OPSD, Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, using the distributional shift between matched teacher views with and without an English reference solution as an operational proxy to guide privileged distillation and reference anchoring. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks covering 17 languages and multiple difficulty levels show that our method outperforms strong multilingual reasoning baselines and OPSD variants. Further analysis reveals that RP-OPSD concentrates privileged distillation on reasoning-control and problem-condistioned state-update tokens, while downweighting it for tokens that mainly support surface realization. Our code is available at https://github.com/NJUNLP/RP-OPSD.

</details>


### [152] [The Bitter Lesson of Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06370)
*Ishan Patel,Sahil Sen,Elias Lumer,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在BFCL v4基准上对14个语言模型系统比较了程序化工具调用（PTC）与原生JSON工具调用，发现PTC在11/14模型中达到或超过JSON基线，在GPT-5.6系列上提升10.6%，且在并行扇出和上下文退化条件下表现更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用将LLM转化为能超越训练数据行动的智能体，而对代码能力模型而言，程序化工具调用通过脚本替代僵硬的JSON调用，实现自然的链式调用和并行化。然而，目前缺乏在既定基准上、跨当前及先前模型代际、在真实任务条件下对"工具即代码"范式的系统评估。

Method: 在BFCL v4基准上，对14个语言模型进行程序化工具调用（PTC）与原生JSON工具调用的经验比较。PTC范式将工具暴露为类型化的Python存根，模型通过代码调用，执行与结果处理在单个智能体回合内完成。

Result: PTC在14个模型中的11个上达到或超过原生JSON工具调用；在GPT-5.6系列中较JSON基线提升10.6%；在并行扇出条件下14个模型中13个达到或超过基线；在上下文退化条件下保持稳定，而基线平均退化2.3%。

Conclusion: 程序化工具调用是JSON工具调用的可行且稳健的替代方案，其性能随模型发布代际能力而追踪，表明该范式具有广泛适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Tool use transforms LLMs into agents that act beyond their training data, and for code-capable models, programmatic tool calling extends this further by replacing rigid JSON calls with scripts that chain and parallelize naturally. However, a systematic evaluation of tools as code on an established benchmark across current and prior model generations under real-world task conditions has not been conducted. In this work, we empirically compare programmatic tool calling (PTC) to native JSON tool calling across 14 language models on BFCL v4. In the programmatic tool calling paradigm, tools are exposed as typed Python stubs that the model invokes through code, with execution and results handled in a single agent turn. Programmatic tool calling matches or exceeds native JSON tool calling in 11 of 14 models on BFCL v4, with the GPT-5.6 family achieving a 10.6% improvement over the JSON tool calling baseline. Further, it matches or outperforms baseline in 13 of 14 models under parallel fan-out, and holds stable under context rot conditions where baseline degrades 2.3% on average. Our results demonstrate that programmatic tool calling is a viable and robust alternative to JSON tool calling, with performance tracking model capability across release generations.

</details>


### [153] [Learning When to Trust via Selective Context Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06377)
*Xian Sun,Wei Chow,Yingshuo Wang,Junhao Liu,Wei Gao,Qing Wu,Lingdong Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将语言模型对上下文信号的应对问题重新定义为"选择性信任"（selective trust），并引入MIST基准（在干净、误导、正确上下文、无关上下文四种匹配条件下评估）和SC2W指标（衡量误导信号将正确回答翻转的错误率）。研究发现在全面基准测试中，模型普遍容易被误导信号诱导出错。作者提出SCOPE方法，通过挖掘干净正确/误导错误的样本对，在四种条件下平衡配对的偏好数据上优化DPO目标，显著降低SC2W同时保留上下文可信时的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越依赖外部信号作答，单个误导性信号即可使正确回答出错。现有"抵抗误导信号"的训练方法存在根本缺陷：模型忽略所有上下文看似鲁棒，但在上下文值得信任时却毫无用处。因此需要将问题重新表述为"选择性信任"——既要抵抗误导，又要信任正确的上下文。

Method: 1) 提出MIST基准：人工标注，将每个推理条目在四种匹配条件下呈现（干净、误导、正确上下文、无关上下文）；2) 提出SC2W配对指标：统计误导信号将干净条件下的正确答案翻转为错误的频率；3) 提出SCOPE方法：挖掘干净正确/误导错误的失败样本对，在四种条件平衡配对的偏好数据上优化标准DPO目标，而非仅对误导样本训练。

Result: 在涵盖多种主流开源模型的综合基准研究中，SC2W方法大幅降低了模型的SC2W指标，同时保持了在干净、正确或无关上下文条件下的准确性不下降，证明模型普遍存在被误导信号诱导出错的问题。

Conclusion: 模型应当以"选择性信任"能力为评判标准，而非单纯的抗误导能力。仅训练模型抵抗误导信号是不够的，必须在保持对可信上下文信任的同时抵抗误导信号。

Abstract: Language models increasingly condition their answers on external signals, and a single misleading one can turn a correct answer wrong. The obvious remedy, training models to resist such signals, hides a failure mode: a model that ignores all context looks robust yet is useless when the context is worth trusting. We recast the problem as selective trust and introduce MIST, a human-annotated benchmark that renders each reasoning item under four matched conditions (clean, misleading, correct-context, and irrelevant-context), together with SC2W, a paired metric counting how often a misleading signal flips a clean-correct answer to wrong. Across a comprehensive benchmark study, we observe that such a susceptibility is universal. We then propose SCOPE, which mines clean-correct/misleading-wrong failures and optimizes a standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) objective over matched preference pairs balanced equally across all four conditions, rather than over misleading items alone. Our approach substantially reduces SC2W on popular open-sourced models while preserving accuracy when the added context is clean, correct, or irrelevant. With this work, we argue that models should be judged on selective trust, not on resistance alone.

</details>


### [154] [Simulator-Grounded Large Language Models for Industrial Causal Reasoning: Tool-Use, Structured Injection, and Plant-Portable Retrieval for Wastewater Treatment Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05151)
*Gary Simethy,Daniel Ortiz Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种将冻结的Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct模型与可解释污水处理模拟器（CCSS-IX）结合的方式，用于工业因果问答：实时模拟器Oracle（99.5%）、结构化参数注入（79%）和解耦检索-推理DRR检索器（75.8%），三者均远超最强RAG基线（48%）。DRR仅110M参数、每厂训练约17秒，可跨厂迁移（88%），且是唯一能处理干预后反事实问题的方案（+16.3pp over方法2）。在ARC/OpenBookQA上同样复现+3pp优势，表明机制非领域特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 污水处理厂操作员需要基于本厂变量相互作用和效应传播速度的因果答案，而非通用预训练文本。现有检索增强方案在因果问答上表现不足（基线48%），需要一种既能保持模拟器可解释性、又能以低成本落地部署的因果问答方案。

Method: 在冻结的Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct模型上，将CCSS-IX模拟器通过三种方式接地：(1)实时模拟器Oracle直接调用；(2)结构化参数注入；(3)解耦检索-推理（DRR）检索器——110M参数、每厂训练~17秒。DRR进一步通过独立检索机制从模拟器提取数值参数传输给模型推理。通过临床式配对95% CI和跨领域（ARC/OpenBookQA）复制实验验证泛化性。

Result: 因果基准（198问）上：Oracle 99.5%、参数注入79%、DRR 75.8%，显著高于RAG基线48%。反事实基准（60问）上仅有DRR能处理干预后问题，较方法2高16.3pp（95% CI [+7.1,+26.4]），在时间尺度和运行工况类别上达100%。跨植物迁移仍保持88%，而静态参数表不可迁移。在ARC/OpenBookQA上DRR达79%，优于无约束Llama-3.1-8B的76%和全注入的74%。

Conclusion: 首次在单一模拟器框架下系统比较了实时工具使用、静态参数注入和学习的数值参数检索三种接地方式。DRR作为轻量级可迁移的检索-推理方案在因果和反事实问答上均表现优异且具备跨领域泛化能力，为工业因果问答部署提供了实用阶梯（deployment ladder）。

Abstract: Wastewater operators need answers grounded in how their plant's variables interact and how fast effects propagate, not in generic pretraining text, when asking causal questions such as "why is N2O rising?" or "what happens if I cut aeration by 20%?". We compare three concrete ways to ground a frozen Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct model in an architecturally interpretable wastewater simulator (CCSS-IX): a live simulator oracle (Method 1), structured parameter injection (Method 2), and a Decoupled Recall-Reasoning (DRR) retriever (Method 3). On a 198-question causal benchmark the three reach 99.5%, 79%, and 75.8%, forming a deployment ladder above the strongest retrieval-augmented baseline at 48%. The DRR retriever has 110M parameters and trains per plant in ~17 seconds; after cross-plant transfer to a biologically distinct plant it still reaches 88%, while Method 2's static table cannot transfer. On a 60-question counterfactual benchmark only Method 3 handles queries about what happens after an intervention: +16.3 pp over Method 2, paired 95% CI [+7.1, +26.4] pp, with 100% on the timescale and operating-regime categories. On the AI2 Reasoning Challenge (ARC) with an OpenBookQA fact corpus, the same selective-retrieval mechanism reaches 79% versus unconstrained Llama-3.1-8B 76% and full-injection 74%, a +3 pp out-of-domain replication that argues against a result specific to wastewater treatment. We provide the first single-simulator comparison of live tool-use, static parameter injection, and learned numerical-parameter retrieval for industrial causal question answering.

</details>


### [155] [Mean-Field Dynamics of Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05152)
*Hao Ai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，将大语言模型的链式推理建模为线索图上的引导发现过程，并通过均值场近似推导出关于线索发现比例的一维常微分方程。实验上使用学生模型对教师模型输出的归一化surprisal来识别线索标记，并对多条推理链取平均以获得统计规律。实验表明这些统计规律在同一数据集内可复现，且能被理论方程拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来带链式推理的大语言模型被广泛应用，但对其行为的理论解释仍然缺乏。理解这些行为背后的统计规律和理论机制，有助于加深对模型的理解并指导模型优化。现有方法要么简化模型架构，要么借用物理系统的类比，本文希望在不做这些简化的情况下进行理论分析。

Method: 将LLM推理形式化为线索图上的引导发现过程；利用均值场近似推导出线索发现比例的常微分方程；实验上通过学生LLM对教师LLM输出的归一化surprisal识别线索标记；对多条推理链取平均获得统计规律，并用理论方程进行拟合验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，所得到的统计规律在同一数据集内具有可复现性，并且可以由所提出的理论方程进行拟合，验证了理论框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM链式推理提供了一种不依赖架构简化的理论解释框架，通过线索图与均值场近似建立了可验证的统计模型，未来可能对优化推理策略和训练过程有指导意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with chain-of-thought reasoning have been widely applied in recent years, and theoretical explanations of their behavior may help deepen our understanding and guide model optimization. In this study, we introduce a framework that seeks statistical regularities and theoretical interpretations in LLM reasoning without simplifying the model architecture or making analogies to existing physical systems. We formulate LLM reasoning as a guided discovery process on a clue graph, and derive a one-dimensional ordinary differential equation for the fraction of discovered clues using the mean-field approximation. Experimentally, clue tokens are identified using the normalized surprisal of a student LLM on the outputs of a teacher LLM, and statistical regularities are obtained by averaging over many reasoning chains of thought. Our experiments show that the resulting statistical regularities are reproducible within the same dataset and can be fitted by the solving the proposed theoretical equation.

</details>


### [156] [Answer First, Reason Later: Commitment Order in Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05687)
*Jewon Yeom,Jaewon Sok,Seonghyeon Park,Jeongjae Park,Hwiyeong Lee,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现掩码扩散语言模型（dLLM）在推理任务上因自由提交token的顺序而失败。通过对LLaDA-8B在GSM8K上的解码过程进行日志分析，发现无约束解码会在推理区域仍有一半掩码时过早提交最终答案，并导致高达90%的问题坍缩为仅答案输出。作者通过2x2提示-解码器设计证明链式思维仅在有序提交时有效（交互效应+34.8个百分点），并提出"前沿门控提交"（frontier-gated commitment）单旋钮干预，将精度从0.528恢复至0.852，同时保留最高4倍并行解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型的核心卖点是token可以任意顺序提交，但作者怀疑这种自由在推理任务上反而是失败根源。已有窗口式采样器仅从效率角度被提出，从未被视作解决推理病理的工具，本文旨在揭示并修复这一未被认识的推理缺陷。

Method: 1) 对LLaDA-8B在GSM8K上的解码过程逐token记录提交时间，分析无约束解码的失败模式。2) 构建2x2提示-解码器设计（有无思维链 × 有序/无序提交）来分离交互效应，并将其分解为坍缩通道和顺序通道。3) 在Dream-7B和MATH-500上复现结果。4) 提出"前沿门控提交"（frontier-gated commitment）干预，即在解码前沿窗口内限制提交位置，并沿窗口大小与每步token数的前沿进行调优。

Result: 无约束解码在轨迹的15-24%处就提交最终答案，此时一半推理区域仍被掩码；画布增大时最多90%的问题坍缩为仅答案输出。EOS压力在各解码器间几乎相同，失败根源是可达性而非终止信念。链式思维与有序提交存在显著交互（+34.8个百分点，95% CI [26.8, 42.8]）。前沿门控提交将准确率从0.528恢复到0.852，保留最高4倍并行解码；最佳窗口从完全精炼时的w=1翻转为8 tokens/step时的无约束。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散模型的"任意顺序提交"自由在推理任务上是一个病理而非优势。EOS压力本身不是问题，问题在于采样器无法在远距离位置依据终止信念行动。现有窗口式采样器尽管最初为效率而设计，实际已成为修复推理坍缩的最小必要措施。这重新定义了窗口式采样器的价值与用途。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) can commit tokens in any order -- a freedom marketed as their core advantage over autoregressive decoding. We show that on reasoning tasks this freedom is instead the axis of failure. Logging every commitment during decoding of LLaDA-8B on GSM8K, we find that unconstrained (pure) decoding commits the final answer at 15-24% of the trajectory while half the reasoning region is still masked, and collapses to answer-only outputs on up to 90% of problems as the canvas grows. The cause is not the model's termination beliefs -- EOS "pressure" is nearly identical across decoders -- but reachability: whether the sampler may act on those beliefs at distant positions. A 2x2 prompt-decoder design shows that chain-of-thought helps only under ordered commitment (interaction +34.8 percentage points, 95% CI [26.8, 42.8]; without reasoning text the decoders are indistinguishable), an interaction we decompose into a collapse channel and an order channel and replicate on Dream-7B and MATH-500. A single-knob intervention -- frontier-gated commitment -- causally recovers the full gap (0.528 to 0.852) while preserving up to 4x parallel decoding, along a measured frontier whose optimal window flips from w=1 at full refinement to unconstrained at 8 tokens/step. Our results reframe existing window-style samplers, previously motivated by efficiency, as the minimal fix for a reasoning pathology they were never designed to address.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting，一种将现有企业应用封装为自主AI代理并组织成分层嵌套结构的多智能体协作框架，实现自然语言交互和跨应用流程编排，解决传统企业应用集成（ESB、API网关、RPA）的高耦合、高运维成本和智能化不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业依赖多个异构业务系统和信息应用，导致数据孤岛和流程碎片化严重。现有企业应用集成方案（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升、智能能力有限等固有缺陷，难以有效编排和利用已有应用投资。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting多智能体协作框架：1）将既有企业应用封装为自主AI代理（Application-as-Agent范式）；2）智能体组织成分层嵌套的监护拓扑结构而非扁平互联，镜像企业生态的组成复杂性；3）从每个遗留应用中提取数字代理以支持自然语言交互和自主操作；4）通过中央编排器协调多代理进行任务分解和动态调度；5）提供统一对话界面实现跨应用查询和流程编排（Conversation-as-Integration理念）。

Result: 论文提出了"应用即代理"集成范式和"对话即集成"交互哲学两大核心贡献，并在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用等场景中探索了该方法的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting为传统企业应用集成提供了新的智能化范式，通过分层嵌套的多智能体架构降低了系统耦合度并提升集成智能化水平，在异构系统协调和大数据应用方面展现良好泛化前景，为企业数字化转型提供了新的集成路径。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [158] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ignition Index (I)——一个将Global Workspace Theory (GWT)中"全或无点火"预测操作化到transformer语言模型上的标量指标。通过四参数sigmoid拟合各层线性探针准确率与输入信号强度的关系，提取陡峭度参数β-hat来量化相变陡峭程度。在11个模型、5种架构家族中验证了9.6倍的选择性（p<0.001），并发现前馈transformer的聚合β-hat比SSM高89%，循环架构沿迭代轴表现出工作空间样转变，训练过程中检测到PELT相变等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Global Workspace Theory提出认知系统应以"全或无"的相变方式对信息进行全局广播，但此前缺乏在语言模型上量化这一动态预测的验证指标。作者旨在建立首个将GWT的动态预测与机制可解释性相连接的定量桥梁，填补缩放定律文献中尚未表征的架构级判别能力空白。

Method: （1）定义Ignition Index为四参数sigmoid拟合的陡峭度参数β-hat，以各层线性探针准确率随输入信号强度的变化为数据来源；（2）使用洗牌标签作为负对照，通过Mann-Whitney U检验验证指标选择性；（3）在11个模型（涵盖5种架构家族：前馈transformer、SSM/Mamba、循环架构如Huginn、Pythia等）上对比β-hat分布；（4）采用PELT算法检测训练过程中可能存在的相变点；（5）验证与模型规模、信号强度相关的假设。

Result: （1）前馈transformer的聚合β-hat比SSM高89%（p<1e-13，Cohen's d=0.52），Mamba呈近线性曲线，缺乏全局广播；（2）Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的ignition是深度轴的2.12倍； （3）Pythia-410M在训练第256步检测到PELT相变（+67%），先于induction-head形成；（4）ignition与模型规模和信号强度的假设未获支持；（5）洗牌对照显示9.6倍选择性（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: Ignition Index是首个经验证的能量化GWT点火预测与语言模型机制可解释性之间的桥梁，具有9.6倍的测量选择性和此前未被表征的架构级判别力。研究揭示了前馈transformer与SSM在信息整合机制上的本质差异，循环架构沿循环维度的点火效应为GWT提供了计算层面的新证据，但transformer架构可能在点火机制上已趋于饱和。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [159] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Woodpecker Distillation，一种弱到强的训练框架，通过对比式局部干预从弱模型中提取纠错信号并蒸馏到强模型中，从而修复大语言模型在推理任务中的局部推理错误，显著提升数学推理基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务上常失败，但失败并非源于全局能力不足，而是中间步骤中的局部推理错误（localized reasoning bugs）。作者发现这些错误可通过弱模型生成的短补丁修复，但直接微调于弱补丁无法有效内化该纠错信号，需要探索更有效的知识转移方式。

Method: 提出 Woodpecker Distillation 框架：在同一个强模型推理前缀处，分别获取成功与不成功的弱模型补丁，构建对比式干预；利用这些干预引发的未来 token 预测分布构建纠错性教师分布；将该信号蒸馏到强模型中，使其学习如何从局部错误中重定向到正确解。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation 能持续提升强模型性能，并优于直接模仿（direct imitation）等基线方法。

Conclusion: 推理能力提升的有效信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于干预如何重塑模型未来的推理分布。通过对比式局部干预和蒸馏，可以有效地将弱模型的纠错能力转移到强模型，实现弱到强训练。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [160] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测模型中，能否用符合临床指南的分类编码替代连续预测变量而不牺牲预测性能。基于多中心欧洲注册数据库的三个治疗队列，比较标准梯度提升模型与完全分类化模型，结果显示两个队列中性能无统计学差异，一个队列有显著下降，但全局特征重要性排序保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中预测准确率高，但因模型解释与临床医生推理方式不一致而限制临床采纳。临床医生用户研究呼吁使用符合临床指南的截断值，因此研究者探索是否能用临床信息化的分类编码替代连续预测变量。

Method: 基于多中心欧洲注册数据库，将患者按三种治疗方案分层为三个队列。对比标准梯度提升模型与完全分类化梯度提升模型，后者采用基于治疗特异性阈值、符合卒中指南的分类编码。

Result: 在三个治疗队列中，完全分类化模型在其中两个队列与连续变量模型的预测准确性在统计学上无显著差异，但在一个队列中预测准确性显著下降。全局特征重要性排名在所有治疗组中保持一致。

Conclusion: 基于指南的分类化编码保留了预后因素的核心层级结构，是卒中结局模型可行且具有临床可取性的设计选择，可提升模型解释性与临床对齐程度而不显著损失性能。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [161] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE 是一个面向 LLM 智能体技能复用的多迹线溯源审计框架，通过提取表达（Expression）、实现（Implementation）和运行（Operational）三条溯源码，构建技能运行图（SOG），在审计时以确定性方式比较缓存的迹线并校准，在 SKILLTRACE-BENCH 基准上达到 AUROC 0.938、F1 0.898，并在 36,446 个技能的野外审计中超越了仓库级基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 智能体生态系统中可复用技能（混合模态的元数据、自然语言指令、代码、工具、引用和运行工作流）日益商品化，技能复用的审计问题已不同于传统代码克隆检测。现有检测器仅针对单模态源代码或整体包相似度，无法捕获仅保留技能某一部分的复用证据，因为这些证据分散在作者文本、实现片段和运行结构中。

Method: SKILLTRACE 提取三条来源迹线：Expression（表达）、Implementation（实现）和 Operational（运行）。运行迹线被表示为技能运行图（SOG），捕捉激活、过程和资源流结构。仅在一次 ingestion 阶段由 LLM 辅助运行迹线提取；审计时对缓存的迹线进行确定性比较，将每条迹线与同名严格负样本校准，并报告支撑复用决策的具体迹线。

Result: 在 SKILLTRACE-BENCH 上，包含 820 个变换后的复用正样本（对应 100 个市场锚点）和 751 个负对照，SKILLTRACE 达到 AUROC 0.938 和 F1 0.898。在包含 36,446 个技能的野外审计中，迹线归因证据能够产生可操作的复用审查队列，优于仓库级基线。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE 通过多迹线溯源审计框架有效解决了 LLM 技能市场中部分复用证据难以检测的问题，其迹线归因机制不仅能高精度地识别复用，还能为审计人员提供哪条证据链支撑判定决策的透明信息，提升可操作性与可信度。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [162] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AECR（抽象事件因果规则），一种关系级别的因果抽象范式，将具体的因果事件对转化为泛化的抽象因果逻辑，并构建多智能体CACI系统结合相似性约束聚类从噪声数据中提取可信的AECR知识库。在此基础上，提出AR-GCAE编码器将AECR注入因果图事件预测任务，实验证明该方法显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，尤其在低频长尾和未见事件样本上提升最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例级别的事件因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重的泛化缺陷，难以满足事件中心智能分析系统对风险预警、决策支持和叙事理解等任务的需求，因此需要一种能泛化到稀有及未见事件场景的因果知识表示方式。

Method: 1) 提出AECR关系级因果抽象范式，将具体因果对转化为保留内在因果关系的抽象逻辑规则；2) 设计多智能体CACI（具体到抽象因果归纳）系统，结合相似性约束聚类从噪声原始因果数据中蒸馏出可信的AECR；3) 构建两个完整的AECR知识库；4) 提出AR-GCAE（抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器），通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入CGEP基准任务。

Result: 定量实验表明，应用AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，为事件预测带来一致的性能提升，其中在稀有和未见事件样本上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 抽象级别的事件因果规则是弥补实例级别因果对泛化不足的有效途径，能够显著提升事件因果推理在低频率与未见场景下的表现，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [163] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个15.3M参数的人类国际象棋AI，通过将对弈建模为时间感知的序列过程（而非孤立地看待每个局面）来预测人类行棋。它结合了最近20步的历史编码器和时间控制模块，在6.1B个局面（来自1.17亿场Lichess快棋对局）上训练，仅用30天和一块T4 GPU，就达到了55.23%的top-1和90.95%的top-5行棋预测准确率，超越了之前的最优人类国际象棋模型Maia 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类行棋预测模型（如Maia 2）主要将每个局面作为独立输入来处理，忽略了国际象棋对弈中的人类行为本质上是时间相关的序列过程——包括开局偏好、局内行为倾向的变化以及时间压力对决策的影响。作者旨在验证"将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动"是否比"仅基于局面的方法"能产生更符合人类行为的行棋预测。

Method: Otter采用两个条件信号：(1) 行棋历史编码器——基于最近20步的历史来条件化预测，捕捉开局偏好、位置漂移和局内行为倾向；(2) 时间控制模块——根据时钟压力调制预测。模型仅有15.3M参数，在6.1B个棋局面（来自117M场Lichess快速对局）上训练，使用单块T4 GPU耗时30天完成。

Result: Otter在top-1行棋预测准确率达到55.23%，top-5达到90.95%，超越了此前最优人类国际象棋模型Maia 2，且使用了更少的参数和更少的训练数据。在11个Elo分档（<1100到>=2000）中，准确率在1900-1999分档达到峰值57.38%。

Conclusion: 将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动比仅基于局面的方法能产生更符合人类行为的行棋预测，且所需模型更小。作者公开了代码、训练好的模型及完整训练日志。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [164] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SearchAuditBench基准和SearchAuditor框架，用于评估和提升LLM审计器在深度搜索智能体长轨迹中定位、归因和修复错误的能力，端到端通过率达到32.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体通过长时间网络交互解决复杂问题，但该过程复杂且脆弱：小的推理错误可能在长而嘈杂的轨迹中传播为流畅但错误的答案。诊断这类失败需要人工检查极长的执行轨迹，超出人类能力范围，亟需自动化审计工具来减少人工负担。

Method: 构建SearchAuditBench基准，包含1,243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息、65.1K tokens），来自8个开源模型在5个深度搜索基准上的运行结果，每条轨迹都经过专家标注了关键错误步骤、搜索特定的根本原因、参考修复方案及评分标准。同时提出SearchAuditor，一个多视角审计框架，通过基于证据的裁决来定位、归因和修复搜索智能体的失败。

Result: 最强的基线方法即使使用GPT-5.5等前沿模型驱动，端到端通过率仅为26.6%；而SearchAuditor在不同前沿模型上均一致优于所有基线，端到端通过率达到32.3%，且利用其修复方案恢复失败运行能使智能体更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: SearchAuditBench和SearchAuditor为深度搜索智能体失败审计提供了系统化的评估工具和有效框架，能够有效减少人工审计负担，并通过修复方案提升智能体的错误恢复能力。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [165] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PD-GS通过引入时间对齐的音素令牌（来自ASR和强制对齐）并设计语言融合模块，以可学习门控自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，显著改善3DGS说话人头部渲染中的唇部发音精度，减少"漏嘴"伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实现快速逼真的说话头部渲染，但唇部发音不准确：口部运动过度平滑，且常违反如双唇闭合等硬性发音约束，产生"漏嘴"伪影。根本原因在于从连续声学嵌入中通过回归目标推断短暂离散的发音事件，导致预测偏向平均口型；现代自监督语音编码器虽提供丰富韵律和音素线索，但缺乏显式、帧对齐的语言学目标来可靠区分闭合级事件。

Method: PD-GS包含两个核心组件：(1) 通过ASR和强制对齐流程获取时间对齐的音素令牌，为3DGS说话人提供显式语言学目标；(2) 语言融合模块（LFM），利用可学习门控自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在保持平滑音频驱动动态的同时，在发音关键片段上强化音素引导。模型仅用单目视频通过图像重建和唇部地标监督训练。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在唇部几何指标上优于所有对比基线（LMD 2.66），并在挑战性音素序列中定性减少了闭合违规，生成更具语言学保真度的神经化身。

Conclusion: 通过注入帧对齐的离散音素信号并以门控方式与连续音频融合，PD-GS有效解决了3DGS说话人渲染中唇部发音不精确的问题，为神经化身生成提供了更语言保真的方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [166] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SMRC-SD方法，通过状态匹配路由和上下文化自蒸馏来解决特权蒸馏中的状态-参考不匹配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升多轮智能体的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权在线策略蒸馏（privileged on-policy distillation）在多轮智能体训练中提供密集监督，但由于学生智能体在交互环境中采取的行动与参考轨迹不同，实际到达的执行状态可能与参考轨迹覆盖的状态不匹配，导致无差别应用特权蒸馏会造成状态-参考不匹配问题，削弱参考指导的可靠性。

Method: SMRC-SD包含两个核心机制：(1) 状态匹配路由：在每个回合验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中的支撑状态匹配，仅在匹配状态下应用蒸馏，过滤掉缺乏本地兼容指导的回合；(2) 上下文化自蒸馏：对每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹中构建状态条件的教师上下文，使监督信号真正锚定在学生实际到达的状态上。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件成功全路径蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B，将ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。消融实验证实状态匹配路由和状态兼容教师上下文构建均对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: 通过显式判定特权轨迹何时、以何种方式指导学生，SMRC-SD有效解决了状态-参考不匹配问题，为交互式多轮智能体的特权蒸馏提供了更可靠的范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [167] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文训练了从135M到14B参数的14个模型（跨越四个架构家族）于Psych-101数据集上——该数据集包含160个实验中1070万次试次级人类选择。研究发现：在分布内，规模效应几乎消失，0.6B-1B参数即可匹配70B基线；但在分布外，规模呈现更陡峭的扩展梯度，大型模型在泛化到新任务结构时优势明显。通过逐步剥离提示通道和置换试次顺序的诊断，发现掩盖刺激和反馈内容会摧毁75.7%的学习信息并使模型低于随机水平，且模型在独立试次任务上具有置换不变性，但在试次顺序由先前反应决定的任务上敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型微调后可作为通用认知代理，但存在两个悬而未决的问题：(1) 该范式所需的模型规模到底多大；(2) 模型是否真正处理任务结构或仅是利用统计捷径（如选择历史）进行推测。作者旨在系统量化规模和架构对认知建模能力的影响，并验证模型是否真正学习任务结构而非表面捷径。

Method: 作者从四个架构家族训练14个不同规模（135M至14B参数）的模型在Psych-101数据集（来自160个实验的1070万试次人类选择）上微调。他们评估了分布内（已见实验类型的held-out参与者）和分布外（新任务结构）表现。此外，运行两个诊断实验：(1) 在27个实验中逐步剥离四个提示通道（任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈、选择历史）以确定各通道的信息贡献；(2) 置换试次顺序以测试模型对试次序贯依赖的敏感性。

Result: 分布内性能：规模几乎不影响性能，所有模型集中在一个窄带内（像碰到天花板），0.6B-1B模型即足以匹配70B基线。分布外性能：窄带打开呈更陡峭的规模扩展梯度，大型模型在泛化到新任务结构时明显占优。诊断结果：掩盖刺激和反馈内容摧毁75.7%的学习信息并将模型推至随机水平以下，表明选择历史不能单独解释性能；置换试次顺序后，模型在独立试次任务上不变，但在顺序依赖任务上表现敏感。

Conclusion: 小规模认知微调模型有潜力作为心理实验的噪声天花板估计器，但其适用范围受限于训练中看到的范式。模型的规模需求高度依赖评估条件：分布内推断只需不到1B参数，而分布外泛化则需要更大规模。同时，模型确实学习任务结构而非仅依赖统计捷径，但在何种任务上依赖序贯信息（选择历史）取决于任务的试次独立性。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [168] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Project2Task，一个图引导的项目级规划层，用于自主科研。它将项目说明书中的候选贡献表示为创新原子，构建有向谱系图，通过轻量级Bernoulli块模型选择水平、垂直或混合组合分解，生成带依赖感知的任务契约，确保任务边界清晰、无重叠且可执行。在10个项目说明书约30个任务的基准上，质量评分7.15（优于基线4.58和5.31），集成AutoResearchClaw后下游任务准确率从0.536提升至0.759。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单任务系统将整个科研项目当作一个超大任务处理，产生扁平、模糊或重叠的任务集合，任务边界和执行顺序依赖人工协调。科研项目本质上是长时间跨度的议程，需要通过多个有界任务推进，这些任务目标不同但相关、存在并行备选方案和依赖感知的执行顺序——现有方法无法有效处理这一复杂性。

Method: 1) 将项目说明书中的候选贡献表示为"创新原子"，构建有向谱系图（directed lineage graph）；2) 使用轻量级Bernoulli块模型目标函数，在水平、垂直和混合三种组合分解策略间进行选择；3) 生成具有明确贡献归属的有界任务，修复任务重叠和缺失执行字段；4) 输出依赖感知的任务契约，包含目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序；5) 契约独立于任何特定下游研究执行器，支持任务输出集成到连贯的项目级结果。

Result: 在包含10个项目说明书、约30个任务的基准上，基于手稿的组合评估显示Project2Task平均质量评分为7.15，优于Brief Baseline的4.58和Topic-only Setting的5.31。将契约集成到AutoResearchClaw后，下游任务平均准确率从0.536提升至0.759。

Conclusion: 显式的项目到任务规划对于生成连贯、无冗余且可执行的科研任务组合具有明确价值，Project2Task证明了图引导的结构化规划方法能够显著提升自主科研系统的整体产出质量。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [169] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriQua是一个用于LLM事实性评估的自适应分解-验证框架，用标准三元组表示简单声明、用超关系事实（带上下文物化符）表示复杂声明，配合TriQuaScore量化事实性，优于现有基于分解的验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"分解-验证"范式在LLM事实性评估中面临根本性权衡：原子事实简洁但常丢失关键上下文，而更宽泛的表述又缺乏精确评估所需的粒度。需要一个既能保留上下文又不牺牲原子性的灵活事实建模方式。

Method: TriQua框架根据事实复杂度进行灵活建模：简单声明提取为标准三元组（主语-谓语-宾语），复杂声明表示为超关系事实，通过附加辅助上下文限定符（qualifiers）来保留必要上下文。验证过程中直接在具体三元组和限定符上注释错误，提供细粒度可解释性。同时提出TriQuaScore用于量化这些结构化事实单元的事实性。

Result: 实验表明：TriQuaScore与人工标注的事实性分数高度一致；TriQua在分解质量上表现稳健；在基于证据的事实验证任务中优于现有基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: TriQua通过自适应地选择标准三元组或超关系事实来表示不同复杂度的声明，成功在原子性和上下文保留之间取得平衡，并凭借细粒度的错误注释和TriQuaScore量化指标，实现了更优的事实分解质量和证据驱动的事实验证效果。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [170] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV)系统，该系统将书面梦境描述转化为四个面板的时间序列图像。系统先用大语言模型将梦境描述拆分为四个按时间顺序的部分，再由文生图模型为每一部分生成图像，并保持序列间的视觉连贯性；若图像与文本不匹配则重新生成。作者在DreamBank中的50个梦境可视化上进行了评估，使用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL视觉语言模型通过客观指标报告了质量、保真度和连贯性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境情绪强烈但难以沟通，现有系统缺乏将书面梦境描述转化为视觉化时间序列的手段，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: DSV系统流程：1) 大语言模型将梦境描述拆分为四个时间顺序部分；2) 文生图模型为每部分生成图像，保持跨序列的视觉连贯性；3) 对与文本不匹配的图像进行重新生成。评估使用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL三种模型进行客观度量。

Result: 在DreamBank中50个梦境描述的可视化上进行了评估，通过CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL报告了质量、保真度和连贯性结果。

Conclusion: DSV系统展示了将梦境文本转化为连贯的四格时间序列图像可视化方案的可行性，为梦境沟通提供了一种视觉化辅助手段。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [171] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Search2Skill框架，通过自动识别LLM智能体的能力缺口、搜索外部来源获取证据并蒸馏为结构化可复用技能，并采用基于规则的强化学习方案同时优化搜索时机、搜索方式和技能生成，在8个专家级领域上优于搜索增强和基于轨迹的技能学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化技能方法仅从模型自身的参数化知识或轨迹中内部构造技能，受限于模型已有知识边界。然而专家领域的专业技能所依赖的领域惯例和标准流程往往超出这一边界，难以仅靠智能体本身引出，因此需要从外部来源获取知识来弥补能力缺口。

Method: 提出了Search2Skill框架，包含三个核心环节：自动识别智能体的能力缺口、搜索外部来源以弥补这些缺口、将检索到的证据蒸馏为结构化可复用技能。框架通过基于法则的强化学习方案（rubric-based reinforcement learning）联合优化何时搜索、如何搜索以及如何生成技能三个环节。

Result: 在来自三个基准的八个专家级领域上，Search2Skill在流式评估和保留评估两种协议下均一致优于搜索增强基线和基于轨迹的技能学习基线。进一步分析表明，性能提升来源于技能的抽象能力而非原始检索证据本身，且习得的技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill通过外部搜索突破模型知识边界来构建可复用技能，验证了外部证据经技能抽象后而非直接使用原始检索结果可带来更优性能，并且习得技能具有良好的跨模型规模迁移能力，为LLM智能体在专家领域的自我进化提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains. Existing self-evolving skill methods construct skills internally from the model's parametric knowledge or trajectories, and are therefore bounded by what the model already knows. However, the domain conventions and standard procedures underlying professional skills often lie beyond this boundary and are hard to elicit from the agent alone. To address this issue, we therefore propose a novel framework, Search2Skill, that automatically identifies the agent's capability gaps, searches external sources to address them, and distills the retrieved evidence into structured, reusable skills.
