For knowledge intensive tasks involving complex or evolving information, their reliability depends not only on retrieving relevant ev idence but also on using it to guide subsequent reasoning. However, existing methods primarily reward final-answer cor rectness or intermediate progress, without directly assessing whether post-retrieval actions are grounded in the retrieved evidence. This misalignment encourages prior-driven reason ing: agents form conclusions based on internal knowledge and use retrieval mainly to confirm them, resulting in confirma tion bias and inefficient evidenceuse.Toaddressthisissue, we propose Contextual Information Policy Optimization (CIPO), an evidence-oriented reinforcement learning framework that explicitly aligns policy optimization with external evidence use. CIPO assigns dense, turn-level credit to reasoning ac tions influenced by retrieved information, while combining this evidence-use signal with a global outcome reward to pre serveanswercorrectness.Withthismanner,CIPOdiscourages evidence-detached guesses and promotes reasoning trajecto ries in which retrieved facts can guide or revise subsequent reasoning. Importantly, CIPO requires neither human process annotations nor an additional reward model. Extensive exper iments on seven in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks show that CIPO reduces the prevalence of prior-driven rea soning and achieves excellent performance on most tasks.

</details>


### [237] [Schema-Guided Hierarchical Information Extraction and Semantic Evaluation Using Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06167)
*Modhurita Mitra,Jan-Willem Versteeg,Maarten D. Schermer,Shiva Nadi Najafabadi,Marie L. De Bruin,Lourens T. Bloem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模式（schema）的框架，利用生成式AI从非结构化文本中提取复杂的结构化信息，并通过路径匹配算法和生成式AI进行语义评估，在NICE健康技术评估文档上实现了12/14个属性F1>90%的表现，且提取速度比人工快约30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非结构化文本中提取复杂、嵌套的结构化信息（尤其是具有可变基数的属性）是一项具有挑战性的任务。现有方法缺乏统一的框架来同时处理信息提取和结果评估，且评估嵌套、可变基数的属性缺乏有效的对齐和语义比较方法。

Method: 提出一个基于schema的框架：(1) schema作为编码领域知识的信息模型，支持层次化、嵌套信息及可变基数属性的统一提取，单次调用零样本完成；(2) 引入基于路径的语义匹配算法来对齐提取结果与金标准中的嵌套属性；(3) 使用生成式AI进行语义比较，并定义四级分类规则（exact、semantic、useful、non-match）来评估结果。

Result: 在NICE发布的健康技术评估文档上，使用Claude Opus 3模型在14个属性中提取到12个，F1分数>90%；单文档提取时间比人类领域专家快约30倍；框架在不同生成式AI模型间展现泛化性，并在不同HTA组织和语言间展现可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该基于schema的框架能够高效且准确地从非结构化文本中提取复杂结构化信息，同时提供了自动化的语义评估方法，在速度上远超人工且具有良好的泛化性和可迁移性，适用于多个领域和场景。

Abstract: We present a schema-based framework for extracting complex, structured information from unstructured text documents using generative AI, followed by automated semantic evaluation of the extracted information against a gold standard. The schema, serving as an information model encoding domain knowledge, provides a unified, systematic, and consistent framework for extraction of hierarchical, nested information, with attributes of variable cardinality, and subsequent evaluation of the results. Information extraction from a document is performed in a single call to the model, in zero-shot mode.
  In the evaluation step, we introduce a path-based semantic matching algorithm to align the nested, variable-cardinality attributes in the extracted results with those in the gold standard. We use generative AI for semantic comparison of the extracted and gold standard values of an attribute, and introduce a rubric to classify the result of the comparison, according to domain-specific considerations, as an exact, semantic, useful, or non-match.
  We were able to extract 12 out of 14 attributes with an F1 score of $>$90\% from documents published by the health technology assessment organisation NICE, using the generative AI model Claude Opus 3. The time needed to extract the attributes from a document was $\sim$30 times lower than the time taken by a human domain expert. We further demonstrate generalisability of this framework across different generative AI models and transferability across different HTA organisations and languages.

</details>


### [238] [FinEvo-Bench: A Longitudinal Benchmark for Self-Evolving Agents in Professional Financial Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06144)
*Bo Deng,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Chongyang Tao,Xuanren Chen,Chenggang Xie,Renzhao Liang,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinEvo-Bench，这是一个包含120个真实案例、覆盖6个金融领域20个商业场景的纵向基准，用于评估智能体自我进化能力。实验表明，自我进化条件显著提升得分并减少合规问题，其中Letta得分最高（91.65），Codex自我进化增益最大（+19.37）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准大多独立评估任务，无法衡量经验积累是否有助于后续任务；现有自我进化基准也未同时覆盖专业工作流、开放式交付物和多维度评估

Method: 构建FinEvo-Bench：120个真实案例任务，20个商业场景覆盖6个金融领域；机构提供的专业流程定义操作要求和约束；每个场景包含6个相关但实质不同的案例，共享专业流程和人工审阅的评分规则。使用Qwen3.7-Max骨干网络比较四种自我进化智能体脚手架，采用三个独立洗牌、全局交错的任务流，配对的非进化对照估计自我进化增益，由Claude Code评分代理（基于Claude Opus 4.6）评估所有输出。

Result: Letta达到最高进化得分（91.65）和最少合规问题（每任务0.09）；Codex自我进化增益最大（+19.37）。所有脚手架中，进化条件提升得分9.33-19.37分，每任务合规问题减少0.12-0.44。场景内排名4-6的配对得分增益比排名1-3高6.10-8.70分。Claude Code中仅技能进化优于仅记忆进化和记忆-技能联合进化。所有脚手架中，rubric反馈优于参考答案反馈。

Conclusion: FinEvo-Bench能够同时衡量专业表现和自我进化能力，即智能体如何有效地将先前经验转化为后续改进。

Abstract: Most agent benchmarks evaluate tasks independently and cannot measure whether experience from one task helps with later tasks. Existing self-evolution benchmarks do not jointly cover professional workflows, open-ended deliverables, and multi-aspect evaluation. We introduce FinEvo-Bench, a longitudinal benchmark with 120 real-case-grounded tasks, 20 business scenes across six financial domains. Institution-provided professional procedures define the required operations and constraints. Eligible institution-provided and publicly documented cases supply the task facts. Each scene contains six related but substantively distinct cases that share a professional procedure and a manually reviewed rubric for task quality and financial compliance. We compare four self-evolving agent scaffolds using the same Qwen3.7-Max backbone and three independently shuffled, globally interleaved task streams. Paired non-evolving controls estimate each scaffold's self-evolution gain from retained experience, while an independent Claude Code scoring agent backed by Claude Opus 4.6 evaluates all outputs. Letta achieves the highest evolved score (91.65) and fewest compliance issues (0.09 per task); Codex achieves the largest self-evolution gain (+19.37). Across scaffolds, the evolving condition raises scores by 9.33-19.37 points and reduces compliance issues by 0.12-0.44 per task. Paired score gains at within-scene ranks 4-6 exceed those at ranks 1-3 by 6.10-8.70 points. In Claude Code, skill-only evolution produces higher task quality and fewer compliance issues than memory-only and combined memory-skill evolution. Across all four scaffolds, rubric feedback also yields higher scores and fewer compliance issues than reference-answer feedback. FinEvo-Bench measures both professional performance and self-evolution ability: how effectively an agent turns prior experience into later improvement.

</details>


### [239] [HarnessOpt-Bench: Evaluating LLMs at Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06301)
*Varun Ursekar,Apaar Shanker,Yash Maurya,Shehab Yasser,Vijay S. Kalmath,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessOpt-Bench，一个用于端到端harness（提示、工具、控制流、内存和编排代码）优化的基准测试，在昂贵且随机的评估条件下衡量LLM作为优化器改进目标agent harness的能力，并通过111次计分运行评估了5个前沿LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛应用于agent系统，其能力不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于围绕它们的harness（提示、工具、控制流、内存、编排代码）。然而，社区缺乏统一协议来衡量前沿LLM在自动harness优化任务上的表现，因此需要建立标准化的基准测试。

Method: 设计HarnessOpt-Bench基准：优化器（LLM+编码harness）接收种子harness、评分评估反馈和固定目标评估预算；编辑harness并提名最终候选，使用保留测试分区上的归一化增益评分；可信执行环境强制执行评估边界、计量资源使用并保留候选版本用于审计。在4个下游任务、111次计分运行中评估5个前沿LLM，分别在共享编码harness和原生harness下进行。

Result: 实验结果表明：优化器模型之间的差异大于它们所凭借的编码harness之间的差异；原生harness并不始终优于共享harness；增益在不同任务和种子机制间差异显著。

Conclusion: harness优化是一个可测量、可区分的能力，但仍有很大的提升空间。该基准为评估和推动这一能力提供了标准化的测量协议。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed within agentic systems, their capabilities depend not only on the model weights but also on the harness: the prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code surrounding them. This makes automated harness optimization -- the iterative and evaluation-guided improvement of a harness by an AI system -- both an important route to improving AI systems and a demanding capability for AI systems themselves. Yet the community lacks a common protocol for measuring how well frontier LLMs perform at this task. We introduce HarnessOpt-Bench, a benchmark for end-to-end harness optimization under expensive and stochastic evaluation. An optimizer, an LLM paired with a coding harness, receives a target agent's seed harness, graded evaluation feedback, and a fixed target-evaluation budget. It edits the harness and nominates a final candidate, which is scored by its normalized gain over the seed on a held-out test partition that remains inaccessible throughout search. A trusted execution environment enforces the evaluation boundary, meters target-agent resource use, and preserves candidate versions for audit. We evaluate 5 frontier LLMs as optimizers both under a shared coding harness and under their native harnesses across 4 downstream tasks, over 111 scored runs. Experiment results show that optimizer models separate more than the coding harnesses they act through, native harnesses are not consistently superior, and gains vary substantially across tasks and seed regimes. These results establish harness optimization as a measurable and discriminative capability with large space for improvement.

</details>


### [240] [PaDoc: Layout-Grounded Parallel Decoding for Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06146)
*Hao Yu,Jiabo Zhan,Kang Liu,Linnan Zhao,Dongxu Yue,Rui Chen,Jinglin Wang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PaDoc是一种布局引导的文档解析器，将预测的布局视为共享页面表示上的分支结构，通过前缀条件分解使布局流和区域内容分支并发推进，在单个MLLM中实现并行解码，显著提升端到端文档解析的吞吐量并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端文档解析器将页面布局和区域内容序列化为单一自回归序列，导致独立区域被迫按总内容长度串行解码；而两阶段裁剪解析器虽支持区域级并行，但需要重复的视觉预填充且丢失完整页面上下文。作者希望同时保留完整页面上下文并消除解码依赖，实现高效的文档解析。

Method: PaDoc采用布局引导的方法，将预测的布局作为共享页面表示上的分支结构。在区域充分性假设下推导出前缀条件分解，使布局流与区域内容分支能并发推进，将解码深度缩减为最长布局-内容路径。在单个MLLM中实现：打包变长祖先注意力保持标准next-token训练下的可见性，掩码并行解码创建分支，利用vLLM后端将分支作为并发请求处理并复用缓存共享前缀。

Result: 在OmniDocBench Full上，PaDoc的布局F1达到91.1，端到端解析器中Overall得分94.24排名顶级，Text Edit为0.038（最佳），Formula CDM为95.59（最佳）。在384页子集和一个A800 GPU上，五种并发级别下均是最快的端到端解析器，有效页面吞吐量提升67.4%-118%，P95延迟比同骨干的Sequential SFT基线降低39.2%-54.9%。

Conclusion: PaDoc通过布局引导的分支并行解码机制，在保持完整页面上下文的同时实现了区域级并行，在准确性、速度和延迟方面均显著优于现有端到端解析器，证明了布局结构作为并行化先验的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end document parsers provide a unified interface, but serialize page layouts and regional contents into one autoregressive sequence. This formulation forces independent regions onto a decoding path whose length grows with the total content, whereas crop-based two-stage parsers expose region-level parallelism at the cost of repeated visual prefills and fragmented page context. To retain full-page context while removing dependencies, we propose PaDoc, a layout-grounded parser that treats the predicted layout as a branching structure over a shared page representation. Under a region-sufficiency assumption, we derive a prefix-conditioned factorization in which the layout stream and regional content branches advance concurrently, reducing the decoding depth to the longest layout-content path. We realize this factorization within a single MLLM: packed variable-length ancestor attention preserves the visibility under standard next-token training, while masked parallel decoding creates branches that the evaluated vLLM backend serves as concurrent requests with cache-resident shared-prefix reuse. On OmniDocBench Full, PaDoc attains an Overall layout F1 of 91.1 and, among end-to-end parsers, a top-tier Overall score of 94.24 together with the best Text Edit (0.038) and Formula CDM (95.59). On a 384-page subset and one A800 GPU, it is the fastest end-to-end parser at five concurrency levels, improving valid-page throughput by 67.4-118% and reducing P95 latency by 39.2-54.9% relative to a same-backbone Sequential SFT baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Longin-Yu/Padoc

</details>


### [241] [Beyond Top-K: Replacing Black-Box Retrieval with Interpretable Agentic Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06305)
*Sagar Tamang,Ayush Vyas,Tabarakul Hazarika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出在长文档（如财务报表、审计报告等）上，基于嵌入向量的检索增强生成（RAG）存在结构性缺陷：大量表格行在同一嵌入空间中竞争。作者提出READ（可靠的免嵌入智能体文档搜索）系统，通过标准化词法搜索、结构导航和有界段落读取三个确定性操作来实现检索，在51个验证问题上准确率达58.8%，远超稠密检索的15.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于财务报表、审计报告、监管申报等以表格为主的长文档，传统"分块-嵌入-检索top-k"的RAG设计在结构上是不可靠的：86.8%的内容行是表格行，数千个近似相同的数字在同一嵌入空间中竞争；数字单位可能来自上方13行的表头，分块边界常常将数字与其单位（如lakh与crore，相差两个数量级）分离，导致数量级错误。

Method: 提出READ（Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search）系统：智能体通过三种确定性操作读取原始文档——归一化词法搜索、结构导航和有界跨度读取，这些操作通过模型上下文协议（MCP）暴露。整条轨迹是可重放的审计追踪，而非不透明的相似度分数。同时构建了一个表感知分块器（steelman）作为对照。

Result: 在51个验证问题上：READ准确率58.8% vs 稠密检索15.7%（p_Holm=2×10^-5）；即使在调优的稠密检索（35.3%）下，READ仍领先23.5个百分点（p_Holm=0.017）。表感知分块器虽修复了单位问题，但仍有27-30%的数字块缺乏财政年度表头。使用相同循环但top-k工具的智能体仅达27.5%。BM25与READ在统计上无显著差异，表明差异来自嵌入vs免嵌入而非智能体vs词法搜索。

Conclusion: 交互界面的选择（确定性确定性操作 vs 嵌入向量相似度搜索）比迭代智能体循环本身更能决定检索质量。针对表格密集型文档，嵌入式检索存在结构性缺陷，基于免嵌入的确定性检索在这类文档上更可靠。值得注意的是，结果不表明智能体方法优于词法搜索，而表明免嵌入方法优于嵌入方法。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over long documents is dominated by one design: chunk the text, embed the chunks, and surface the top-k nearest neighbours of the query. We argue that for an important class of documents -- financial statements, audit reports, regulatory returns -- this design is structurally unsound, and we make the argument measurable. On a 780-page government financial report, 86.8% of content lines are table rows, thousands of near-identical figures compete in one embedding space, and a figure inherits its unit from a header a median of 13 lines above it -- so a chunk boundary routinely separates a number from whether it is in lakh or crore, an error of two orders of magnitude. A table-aware chunker built as a steelman fixes the unit problem but leaves 27-30% of numeric chunks with no fiscal-year header at every chunk size we tried. We propose READ (Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search), in which an agent reads the raw document through three deterministic operations -- normalized lexical search, structural navigation, and bounded span reads -- exposed over the Model Context Protocol, so a trajectory is a replayable audit trail, not an opaque similarity score. On 51 verified questions READ answers 58.8% against dense retrieval's 15.7% (p_Holm = 2 x 10^-5) -- or 35.3% tuned, which READ still leads by 23.5 points (p_Holm = 0.017). An agent given the same loop but a top-k tool reaches only 27.5%, locating the gain in the interface rather than in iteration. We also report what the evidence does not support: BM25 is statistically indistinguishable from READ, so our result separates embedding-based from embedding-free retrieval, not agentic from lexical search.

</details>


### [242] [CogVis: Must Open-Vocabulary Change Detection Perceive the Scene Anew for Every Query?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06150)
*Zijie Wang,Chen Zhong,Wei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogVis是一个受人类视觉感知启发的认知记忆引导框架，将开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）重构为"感知-记忆-验证"范式，通过场景变化感知器（SCP）提取可复用的类别无关变化先验，语义记忆校准器（SMC）动态估计图像-查询特定的决策阈值，自适应区域过滤器（ARF）基于语义、时间和结构可靠性过滤候选区域，在七个基准数据集上均达到最先进性能，并将推理吞吐量提升28.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OVCD方法将时间感知、语义区分和区域验证纠缠在一起，导致结果不稳定且计算冗余。人类视觉变化感知的机制启发作者提出解耦这三者的认知记忆引导框架，以提升性能稳定性和推理效率。

Method: CogVis采用"感知-记忆-验证"三阶段范式：(1) SCP从冻结的双时相特征中提取可复用的类别无关变化先验，将时间证据与语义类别决策解耦；(2) SMC通过动态估计图像-查询特定决策阈值来补偿类别相关的分数偏移；(3) ARF利用学到的语义、时间和结构可靠性过滤连通候选区域。场景级变化感知可在多个查询间共享，避免重复计算。

Result: 在覆盖语义变化检测、二元变化定位和建筑损伤评估的七个基准数据集上，CogVis在所有数据集上均达到当前最先进性能；通过共享场景级变化感知，推理吞吐量提升28.50%。

Conclusion: CogVis通过解耦时间感知、语义判别与区域验证，成功提升了OVCD的稳定性与效率，验证了认知记忆引导范式在地表监测任务中的有效性，同时避免了跨查询的冗余时间感知计算。

Abstract: Earth-surface monitoring requires change detection models capable of recognizing arbitrary semantic categories. Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (OVCD) addresses this need. However, existing methods often entangle temporal perception, semantic discrimination, and region verification, causing unstable results and redundant computation. Inspired by human visual change perception, we propose CogVis, a cognitive memory-guided framework that reformulates OVCD as a perception-memory-verification paradigm. CogVis first employs a Scene Change Perceptron (SCP) to extract a reusable, category-agnostic change prior from frozen bi-temporal features, thereby decoupling temporal evidence from semantic category decisions. A Semantic Memory Calibrator (SMC) then compensates for category-dependent score shifts by dynamically estimating an image-query-specific decision threshold. Finally, an Adaptive Region Filter (ARF) filters connected candidates using learned semantic, temporal, and structural reliability. Experiments on seven benchmarks spanning semantic change detection, binary change localization, and building-damage assessment show that CogVis achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated datasets. By sharing scene-level change perception, CogVis further avoids repeating category-agnostic temporal perception across queries and improves inference throughput by 28.50%.

</details>


### [243] [iARCS: Iterative Agentic RL for Controllable 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06161)
*Saugat Adhikari,Ashok Prasad Neupane,Pramish Paudel,Ajad Chhatkuli,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iARCS是一个迭代式智能体强化学习框架，通过两阶段策略（通用奖励预训练+任务特定微调）将预训练的3D场景生成器适配到自然语言任务约束，提升合成场景在可通行性、可达性和空间规则等任务关键约束上的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成3D场景生成器往往只优化感知逼真度，无法可靠满足下游计算机视觉和具身AI任务的关键功能约束（如可通行性、可达性、空间规则合规），限制了合成数据的实际训练价值。

Method: iARCS采用两阶段策略：第一阶段进行通用奖励预训练，改善物理合理性和布局质量；第二阶段利用LLM生成的奖励程序进行任务特定微调，并根据训练反馈迭代优化奖励程序。该框架以迭代智能体强化学习方式自适应场景生成器到自然语言任务需求。

Result: 实验表明iARCS在可通行性、可达性和净空相关任务上提升了约束保真度，实现了有效的任务特定约束优化，同时保持了有竞争力的场景多样性。此外，iARCS生成的数据能够改进基础生成器本身。

Conclusion: iARCS验证了作为实用合成数据生成工具而非仅可控场景编辑方法的价值，通过将生成器适配到任务关键约束，弥合了感知真实性与功能约束之间的差距。

Abstract: Synthetic 3D scene generation is increasingly used as a data source for computer vision and embodied AI, but existing generators often optimize perceptual realism without reliably satisfying task-critical functional constraints. This mismatch limits the usefulness of synthetic data for downstream training, where accessibility, traversability, and spatial rule compliance are often essential. We present iARCS, an iterative agentic reinforcement learning framework that adapts a pretrained scene generator to natural-language task requirements. iARCS uses a two-stage strategy: universal-reward pretraining to improve physical plausibility and layout quality, followed by task-specific fine-tuning with LLM-generated reward programs that are iteratively refined from training feedback. Experiments show improved constraint fidelity on walkability, reachability, and clearance-focused tasks, effective task-specific constraint optimization, and competitive scene diversity. We further show that data generated by iARCS improves a base generator, supporting its value as a practical synthetic data generation tool rather than only a controllable scene editing method.

</details>


### [244] [MicroEvo: Knowledge-Guided LLM Sampling for Efficient Microarchitecture Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06183)
*Jia Xiong,Runkai Li,Chenxu Niu,Guangyuan Gao,Changwen Xing,Yifan Zhang,Xinlai Wan,Jieran Cui,Chen Bai,Yusheng Hua,Ying Wang,Ming Ling,Xi Wang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MicroEvo是一个知识引导的微架构设计空间探索框架，将大语言模型与蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)相结合，用于多目标微架构优化。通过LLM驱动的进化算子、Pareto感知的树策略、主动知识积累机制和状态感知指令，MicroEvo相比NSGA-II将Pareto前沿质量提升最多36.2%，搜索效率提升10.6倍，并能扩展到工业级复杂内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微架构设计空间探索面临搜索空间庞大和PPA评估昂贵的问题，可用的仿真预算很小。现有方法进行盲目搜索，未考虑微架构依赖关系，也无法从迭代搜索中有效学习，导致评估浪费和Pareto收敛性差。

Method: MicroEvo框架包含四个核心组件：(1) LLM驱动的进化算子，利用大语言模型生成候选微架构配置；(2) Pareto感知的树策略，平衡Pareto贡献与多样性；(3) 主动知识积累机制，从搜索过程中提取并重用优化见解；(4) 状态感知指令，在线自适应调整搜索行为。

Result: 在实验中，MicroEvo相比NSGA-II将Pareto前沿质量提升最多36.2%，搜索效率提高10.6倍，并且在复杂的工业级内核上展现出很强的可扩展性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MicroEvo证明了将LLM的知识推理能力与MCTS的结构化搜索能力相结合，能有效解决微架构设计空间探索中搜索效率低和Pareto收敛差的问题，为多目标微架构优化提供了一种新的知识引导范式。

Abstract: Microarchitecture design space exploration suffers from expansive search spaces and expensive PPA evaluation, leaving only a small simulation budget for design decision-making. Existing methods perform blind search without considering microarchitectural dependencies and fail to learn from the iterative search effectively, leading to wasted evaluations and weak Pareto convergence. In this paper, we propose MicroEvo, a knowledge-guided framework that couples off-the-shelf LLMs with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) for multi-objective microarchitecture optimization. MicroEvo combines LLM-driven evolutionary operators, a Pareto-aware tree policy that balances Pareto contribution and diversity, an active knowledge accumulation mechanism that extracts and reuses optimization insights, and state-aware directives that adapt the search behavior online. Experiments show that MicroEvo improves Pareto-front quality by up to 36.2% over NSGA-II and achieves 10.6x higher search efficiency, and also demonstrates strong scalability to a complex industrial-scale core. The code repository is available at: https://github.com/GEAR-SEU/MicroEvo-ICCAD-26.

</details>


### [245] [Comparative Approaches to Agent Retrieval over Large Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06196)
*Indivara Kolluru,Nathan Sportsman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型技能库中的技能检索与排序问题，对比混合排序器（词法+稠密嵌入检索）与类型化知识图谱两种系统在690个技能、117个真实查询上的表现。混合排序器在top-5中达到73.5%±8.0的准确率，而知识图谱在匹配token预算下显著更差（-11.2点）。核心发现是图谱的候选边与排序器已检索的嵌入邻域高度重叠（98.6%），导致拓扑预过滤限制了图谱的检索扩展能力，只能丰富关系语义而无法延伸检索范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型技能库驱动的智能体面临技能选择与排序的挑战，将整个技能库载入上下文代价高昂且缺乏自主排序结构。作者希望探究在强排序器基础上，额外的结构（如知识图谱）是否能提升技能检索能力，并给出机制层面的解释。

Method: 基于690个技能构建两个系统：（1）混合排名器，结合词法检索与稠密嵌入检索进行稀疏按需加载；（2）类型化知识图谱，编码前置条件、数据流和排序等工作流关系。在117个真实非回声查询上评估，并对比LLM生成的图谱边与本地嵌入邻域的贡献，同时考察基于作者编写查询的评估偏差。

Result: 混合排序器在top-5中达到73.5%±8.0的命中率；知识图谱在匹配token预算下显著更差（-11.2点，p=0.0007）；98.6%的类型化边连接的是排序器已同时浮出的技能，73%排序器未命中的查询在图谱中完全不可达；作者编写的查询会高估hit@5多达44点。

Conclusion: 知识图谱虽然能丰富关系语义，但由于其候选边源自与排序器相同的嵌入邻域，存在拓扑预过滤的固有局限，无法扩展检索覆盖范围。作者提供了一个机制性解释：为何在强排序器之上添加结构未必能提升检索性能，并指出了添加结构相互依赖在哪些条件下才是最优的。

Abstract: Agents backed by large skill libraries must decide which skills to load and in what order. Loading the entire library into context is expensive and provides no structure for autonomous sequencing. We study two systems for this problem over a corpus of 690 skills: a hybrid ranker combining lexical and dense-embedding retrieval for sparse, on-demand loading, and a typed knowledge graph encoding workflow relations such as prerequisites, data flow, and ordering. On a set of 117 realistic, non-echoing queries, the hybrid ranker retrieves the correct skill within the top five in 73.5% +/- 8.0 of cases, leaving roughly a quarter of queries unserved. When used as the design intended (substituting graph neighbours for additional ranked results at matched token budget), the graph is significantly worse (-11.2 points, p = 0.0007). Its LLM-generated edge layer adds nothing over neighbours obtained free from a local embedding pass, and 73% of the queries the ranker misses are not reachable through the graph at all. We attribute this to a pre-filter topology bound. Because the graph's candidate edges are drawn from the same embedding neighbourhood the ranker already searches, 98.6% of typed edges connect skills the ranker had already surfaced together. The graph can enrich relation semantics but cannot extend retrieval reach. We further show that evaluating on author-written queries overstates hit@5 by up to 44 points, which would have hidden these results entirely. Our contribution is a mechanistic account of why added structure does not improve retrieval over a strong ranker, and identify the conditions under which adding structural interdependence into the retrieval is optimal.

</details>


### [246] [EnvACE: Internalizing Environment Dynamics via World Rehearsal for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06197)
*Zishan Xu,Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Yifu Guo,Zhengxi Lu,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Xingshan Zeng,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnvACE是一种代理强化学习方法，通过"世界排练"（world rehearsal）替代外部环境交互来训练大语言模型代理进行长时程工具使用。策略在行动与排练之间交替：先生成工具调用，再扮演环境角色产生响应，并基于排练的响应进行后续决策，两者通过任务成功奖励进行端到端联合优化。在BFCL-v4、tau^2-Bench、VitaBench和FinMCP-Bench上取得强且可迁移的性能，测试时可通过内部化世界模型进行私有排练以进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大语言模型代理进行长时程工具使用通常依赖真实或合成的可执行环境交互，但这些环境的构建和验证成本高昂；外部模拟器又难以接地（grounding）。作者希望找到一种不依赖外部环境交互的替代训练路径，以突破环境约束对LLM代理训练扩展的瓶颈。

Method: EnvACE采用代理强化学习方法，训练时策略在"行动"和"排练"两个角色间交替：策略首先生成工具调用，然后扮演环境角色生成该行动所引发的结果响应，并基于排练出的响应来决定后续行动。两个角色（代理和环境模拟者）通过任务成功奖励进行端到端联合优化，使策略将行动与环境响应的关系内在化到模型参数中，形成代理世界模型支持决策。

Result: 在BFCL-v4、tau^2-Bench、VitaBench和FinMCP-Bench四个基准上，EnvACE取得强且可迁移的性能，在整体评估中超越环境扩展（environment-scaling）基线。受控实验表明世界排练在不同模型规模下一致提升策略学习效果。测试时，内部化的世界模型支持在提交执行前进行私有排练，在中等的排练预算下无需额外外部交互即可获得进一步性能增益。

Conclusion: 世界排练（world rehearsal）为超越外部环境约束扩展LLM代理训练提供了新路径。通过让策略在训练中自行模拟环境响应，可以消除对外部可执行环境或模拟器的依赖，同时获得的内部化世界模型还能在测试时通过私有排练进一步提升决策质量。代码已公开。

Abstract: Training large language model agents for long-horizon tool use typically relies on interactions with real or synthesized executable environments, whose construction and verification are costly, or on external simulators that are difficult to ground. We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [247] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-RAG框架，将检索增强生成（RAG）引入时间序列预测领域，通过设计专门的"参考令牌"来融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列信息，在多个真实世界基准上实现了一致的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然RAG在大语言模型中已被证明能通过引入相关外部信息显著增强模型能力，但在时间序列预测领域其应用仍十分有限。此外，时间序列模型受限于训练数据量小、参数规模小且缺乏LLM的生成能力，直接像语言模型那样将参考序列拼接到提示中可能无法达到预期效果，因此需要专门设计适合时间序列场景的RAG方法。

Method: 提出TS-RAG框架，核心是为检索到的相似时间序列设计专门的"参考令牌"（reference tokens），通过这些令牌有效地将输入序列的信息与检索到的相似序列信息进行融合，从而更稳健地捕捉复杂的时序动态。

Result: 在多个真实世界的时间序列预测基准数据集上，TS-RAG一致地取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能表现。

Conclusion: 专门设计的参考令牌机制能够有效将RAG的优势迁移到时间序列预测任务中，弥补了时间序列模型在数据规模和参数量上的不足，为时间序列预测提供了一种新的增强范式。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [248] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DASH（Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons），一种改进的on-policy自蒸馏方法，通过根据局部蒸馏信号与序列均值的差距自适应调整token级监督权重，以更好地利用rollout的时间结构，提升LLM的数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR使用序列级可验证信号存在稀疏性问题；OPSD虽通过特权教师在学生访问前缀上提供密集token级分布监督来缓解稀疏性，但标准OPSD未充分利用rollout的时间结构——它对所有局部散度分配相同权重，忽略了相同散度大小可能对应不同的历史散度演化轨迹，因此无法根据已实现的散度序列自适应调整token级权重。

Method: DASH将每个局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值之间的差距映射为自适应传播门控，再利用这些门控控制反向多步聚合。通过这种方式，DASH能根据生成过程中局部散度的演化动态调整token级监督权重。该方法复用OPSD已计算的教师和学生分布，无需额外的前向传播。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准、三个模型规模上，DASH在所有基准和所有规模下均优于匹配的vanilla OPSD重运行结果。

Conclusion: DASH通过引入基于散度演化序列的自适应监督机制，在不增加计算开销的前提下显著提升了OPSD在数学推理任务上的表现，验证了利用rollout时间结构对token级蒸馏的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [249] [Improving the Realism of Synthetic Clinical Benchmarks Under Utility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06265)
*Omid Bazgir,Md Nasir,Jacob Hoffman,Yang Yang,Manu Agrawal,Anusua Trivedi,Vinay Rao Dandin,Chris Gibbons,Christine Swisher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"效用约束的逼真度提升"框架来改进企业AI代理的合成临床基准，通过两种确定性修订策略在保持下游效用检测通过的前提下显著提升基准的结构现实性，并证明内部逼真度与源保真度是两个不同目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成临床基准虽能通过效用检查但在结构上仍不真实，尤其在隐私敏感的医疗环境中难以获取运营数据来验证，因此需要在不破坏既有效用检查的前提下提升基准的现实性。

Method: 将基准修订形式化为效用约束下的逼真度提升问题；在一个基于Synthea生成病人并通过EHR演示工作流处理的护理缺口基准上，从缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性、人群对齐四个维度度量逼真度；提出两种确定性修订策略并与朴素致密化对照比较。

Result: 基线基准极薄：样本对缺失率79.44%，仅12.75%行可操作，38.94%患者无可操作指标，前三token集中度达100%。两种确定性修订在保持效用底线前提下改善了这些面板指标，而朴素对照未能消除不真实模板化。内部逼真度与聚合运营参照的源保真度被证明是相关但不同的目标。

Conclusion: 合成基准质量应显式优化，效用应被视为约束条件而非逼真度的充分证据；基准的真实性需要在效用检查之外被单独关注和衡量。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [250] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果审计框架CauAudit，系统评估多模态大语言模型中"图像思考"（visual tool-use）的因果有效性。通过三层干预（策略层、轨迹层、步骤层）识别出两个失败模式：Calling Without Looking（返回的观察对答案无因果效应）和Looking Without Planning（观察有信息但调用计划不连贯）。研究发现视觉工具使用的准确性提升集中在少数校准样本上，大多数情况下并不因果有效，从而揭示了"视觉工具使用的错觉"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"thinking-with-images"范式通过裁剪-缩放等主动视觉操作增强多模态大语言模型，但这些操作往往相对直接推理仅带来边际甚至负的准确率提升，且消耗显著更高的token成本。模型还可能反复裁剪无关区域，或在直接推理能正确回答的问题上失败。核心疑问是：返回的视觉证据是否真的因果性地影响了答案？

Method: 将视觉工具使用形式化为因果图，区分观察中介路径与行动诱导捷径。在三个层面进行干预审计：(1) 策略层：对比工具使用与直接推理；(2) 轨迹层：在推理过程中破坏所有观察；(3) 步骤层：在固定前缀下反事实地替换单个观察。提出步骤级估计量Visual Evidence Gain来隔离每个返回观察的独立贡献。在六个代表性模型和五个细粒度感知基准上展开评估。

Result: 识别出两种策略误校准失败模式：Calling Without Looking（观察对答案无因果效应）和Looking Without Planning（观察有信息但调用时间安排不连贯）。轨迹级诊断将策略级准确率增益分解后发现增益集中在少数校准样本中，即存在"Calibrated minority"效应。

Conclusion: 尽管总体上存在聚合准确率增益，但视觉工具使用在广泛的rollout中不具备因果有效性。作者将这一现象称为"视觉工具使用的错觉"（illusion of visual tool-use），并开源了CauAudit代码库供后续研究使用。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [251] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QuanTiMedAI框架，结合agentic LLM进行临床特征发现与紧凑量子循环网络进行时序建模，用于ICU心脏骤停患者死亡率预测，以仅605个参数实现AUROC 0.852。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU心脏骤停患者死亡率预测研究多依赖早期入院的静态摘要，忽略了患者整个ICU住院期间生理恶化与恢复的时间动态变化。

Method: 采用agentic大语言模型进行临床知情特征发现，结合紧凑量子循环网络进行时间感知的死亡率预测，通过量子非线性特征增强在保持极低参数量的同时提升预测性能。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV心脏骤停患者队列上，AUROC达到0.852，仅使用605个参数，比当前最先进基线提升约2.9%；消融实验系统验证了各架构设计选择的贡献。

Conclusion: 量子增强的时序建模能够以显著更少的参数超越经典循环网络，体现了量子计算与agentic AI在临床预测中的潜力。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [252] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于概念激活向量（CAV）的偏差分析方法，应用于两种神经语音评估系统——基于BERT的文本评分器和基于Whisper的语音-文本多模态评分器，并探索使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在稀疏潜空间中学习更清晰的概念方向。研究发现概念的可恢复性和敏感性高度依赖于模型架构与表征层而非概念本身，SAE虽能提高概念的线性可恢复性但会削弱原始激活空间中的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动口语评估系统在高风险场景中广泛应用，其评分必须依赖语言能力而非说话者无关属性（如母语、年龄）。然而基于Transformer的评分模型是黑盒系统，使其公平性和可解释性分析变得困难，需要开发有效的偏差审计方法。

Method: 将基于CAV的概念激活向量分析方法从特征型评分器扩展到两种神经口语评估系统（文本BERT评分器和基于Whisper的语音-文本多模态评分器），利用CAV将人类可解释概念表示为模型激活空间中的方向，区分概念是否被编码于内部表示与是否影响预测分数（后者通过基于梯度的敏感性指标量化）。同时引入稀疏自编码器（SAE），在稀疏潜空间中学习CAV并将其映射回激活空间，以应对复杂神经嵌入空间中线性可分性不足的问题。

Result: 分析表明概念的可恢复性强烈依赖于被探测的表征和架构，而非仅取决于概念本身；概念敏感性同样具有架构依赖性；SAE能使概念更容易线性恢复，但会削弱原始激活空间中的敏感性，尤其是在低维层中。

Conclusion: 在审计语音评估系统的偏差时，必须区分概念的可恢复性与概念的影响力，二者不可混为一谈，这一发现对公平性分析具有重要意义。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [253] [A Unified Framework for Trajectory Prediction with Explicit Planning and Reaction Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05673)
*Jiaheng Chen,Jiaxing Li,Tinghe Zhang,Chaopeng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: INTraJ提出了一种将社交影响分解为两个阶段（规划在前、反应在后）的统一轨迹预测框架，在四个标准基准上实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法虽然采用了结构化建模和显式社交建模，但未能充分区分社交影响在轨迹规划中的功能性角色。作者观察到智能体通常先预判他人的未来行为形成运动计划，再做局部反应性调整，因此社交交互应扮演"规划先行、反应在后"的分阶段角色。

Method: INTraJ是一个两阶段统一框架：规划阶段利用未来社交信息构建参考轨迹；反应阶段从全上下文预测与参考轨迹之间的残差中恢复局部调整。该框架同时支持多目标与单目标预测范式。

Result: 在Argoverse 2、Argoverse 2-ped、ETH/UCY和SDD四个标准基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法在FDE和长时程一致性指标上一致提升，并在多个设置中达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: INTraJ将轨迹预测重新定义为规划驱动的两阶段过程，验证了分阶段社交建模对稳定预测的关键作用。代码已开源。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction has shifted toward structured formulations with explicit social modeling. However, existing methods inadequately distinguish the functional roles of social influence in trajectory planning. Observing that agents typically form motion plans by anticipating others' future behaviors before making local reactive adjustments, we identify social interactions as playing staged roles, namely planning precedes reaction. We propose INTraJ, a unified framework that decomposes social influence into two stages: a planning stage constructs reference trajectories using future social information, and a reaction stage recovers local adjustments from the residual between full-context prediction and the reference. INTraJ supports both multi-target and single-target paradigms. Extensive experiments on four standard benchmarks, including Argoverse 2, Argoverse 2-ped, ETH/UCY, and SDD, demonstrate consistent improvements, particularly in FDE and long-horizon consistency, with state-of-the-art performance achieved in several settings. INTraJ reframes trajectory prediction as a planning-driven two-stage process, validating that staged social modeling is critical for stable predictions. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/11isnotavailable/INTraJ.

</details>


### [254] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajDebug框架，用于在LLM代理系统的失败轨迹中定位关键错误步骤，通过多粒度历史压缩和基于证据的错误识别解决长轨迹调试难题，并构建了包含486条人工标注失败轨迹的基准TrajErrBench，实验证明其性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理系统在复杂领域表现出色，但存在级联错误和调试困难的问题。关键错误检测旨在定位导致最终失败的轨迹中的最早错误步骤，但面临两大挑战：一是长轨迹中证据分散导致难以识别单个错误；二是失败轨迹中常包含多个局部错误，只有部分错误对最终失败负责。现有方法难以有效区分关键错误和非关键错误。

Method: 提出TrajDebug框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 错误生命周期追踪框架，通过多粒度历史压缩与基于证据的错误识别来解决长轨迹错误发现，并通过追踪每个错误的解决状态和终端影响来进行关键归因；(2) 构建TrajErrBench基准，包含486条来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的人工标注失败轨迹，涵盖真实的工具使用和编码场景。

Result: 在多个代理基准上的实验表明，TrajDebug在整体性能上优于现有基线；应用研究进一步证明其诊断结果能为下游代理改进提供可操作的反馈，提升代理成功率。

Conclusion: TrajDebug有效解决了长轨迹代理系统中关键错误定位的难题，通过错误生命周期追踪实现了准确的关键错误归因，其诊断结果对下游代理性能改进具有实际价值，代码和数据将公开发布以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [255] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出可解释人工智能（XAI）在评估方面存在不足，通过DetoxAI图像识别系统进行说明，提出了人类基础评估方法、面向概念漂移的自适应解释方法（特别是反事实解释），并探讨了数据、模型与解释共同演化的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法缺乏充分评估，尤其是在实际应用中（如偏倚检测和概念遗忘场景）XAI的局限性未被系统检验。

Method: 以DetoxAI图像识别系统为案例，通过人类基础评估方法评估图像分类的解释方法；并探索将反事实解释适配到存在概念漂移的动态数据流中。

Result: 展示了人类基础评估在图像分类解释中的实践，提出了将反事实解释用于概念漂移场景的适配经验。

Conclusion: XAI评估不足是重要问题，需要进一步研究数据、模型和解释三者共同演化的追踪与适应方法。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [256] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于可执行事件轨迹的痕迹接地参数化剖析方法（trace-grounded parametric profiling），在三个受控视频任务（弹球碰墙、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换）共2190个视频中，系统地改变事件数量N和频率F。通过可执行事件轨迹实现时间戳级评估，揭示了分阶段的时序失败模式：持续性状态转换在80%可靠性阈值下最多可靠计数12个事件，而瞬态眨眼事件则完全没有可靠的正确计数区域。提高采样率虽能提升最终准确率（弹球任务从19.6%升至29.3%），但报告序列与真值一致率仅3.7%，说明额外的帧只会虚增最终得分而不产生忠实的事件恢复。该方法将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转向对时序推理失效位置的详细诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界视频基准虽然覆盖面广，但其固定片段将事件数量、速率、持续时间和视觉复杂度纠缠在一起，难以隔离具体的失败模式；已有的程序化基准虽提供更好的控制，但仅对最终答案评分，未能将报告的事件与可执行真值进行审计。因此需要一个既能参数化控制事件属性、又能基于可执行真值做细粒度审计的评估框架。

Method: 提出trace-grounded parametric profiling方法，在三个受控视频任务中系统变化事件数量N和事件频率F，同时保持渲染固定。每个视频附带可执行事件轨迹（executable event trace），支持能力曲面估计和时间戳级别的评估。评估中包括对不同采样率和不同提示策略的消融实验。

Result: 发现分阶段的时序失败：(1) Gemini 3.6 Flash对持续性状态转换在80%可靠性阈值下可可靠计数至12个事件（0.5和1.0 Hz），但对瞬态眨眼事件无可靠的正计数区域；(2) 高数量高频率区间仅0.2%的最终计数正确，仅恢复18.1%的真实事件；(3) 提高采样率将弹球准确率从19.6%提升到29.3%，但报告序列与真值一致率仅3.7%；(4) 提示策略不同带来的提升有限，真实世界视频评估同样显示成功集中在低事件数量区域。

Conclusion: 事件表征决定了模型最初能否访问证据，且该限制会随着事件数量和频率的增加而放大。痕迹接地剖析将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转变为对时序推理失效的详细诊断工具。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [257] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nimblemind多智能体系统(nMAS)，一种基于证据链接和评分标准的自动化心力衰竭特征工程管道，在500条模拟患者记录上生成了132个结构化特征和70个评分聚合特征，将HFrEF表型识别AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910，展示了复杂心血管EHR数据中自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录(EHR)特征工程是临床研究和AI中的主要瓶颈，占数据科学家39-45%的工作量。这在心力衰竭领域尤为突出，因为需要整合碎片化的EHR数据与基于指南的疾病特异性临床推理。现有基于规则和大型语言模型的方法仅能提供部分自动化，且可维护性和证据可追溯性有限。

Method: nMAS采用证据链接、评分标准驱动的多智能体管道，在匿名/模拟的500条患者记录（来自9个EHR源表）上自动进行心力衰竭特征工程。系统生成结构化特征和评分聚合特征，并通过受限LLM进行结构完整性、评分标准合规性、数据来源验证和审计。

Result: nMAS生成了132个结构化特征和70个评分聚合特征，全部通过结构完整性、评分标准合规性和来源可追溯性验证。加入聚合特征后，HFrEF表型识别的held-out AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910。独立LLM评分评估显示特征在证据支持和方法学健全性方面达到最高分的81.5%。

Conclusion: 研究证明了针对复杂心血管EHR数据实现自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性。但评估仅限于单一机构队列，需要外部验证来进一步确认其普适性和可靠性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [VLAff: Vision-Language-Affordance Model for Unified Actionable Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05215)
*Jihoon Oh,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLAff框架，从第一人称人类视频中提取与形态无关的物体中心可操作affordance（视觉、抓取、轨迹），构建包含204K集数的大规模EgoAffordance数据集，并训练统一的视觉-语言模型基础模型，实现零样本操纵和affordance引导的机器人学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频学习机器人操纵技能具有规模化潜力，但人类与机器人之间存在形态差异（embodiment mismatch）这一根本性挑战。现有方法难以将人类演示有效迁移到机器人上，需要一种与具体形态无关的可操作表示。

Method: (1) 利用3D运动恢复结构（SfM）和手部网格重建从第一人称人类视频中提取三种可操作affordance：视觉、抓取和轨迹affordance，显式编码交互位置、抓取方式和移动方式；(2) 构建EgoAffordance大规模数据集（204K集，5.6M视觉affordance，11.6M抓取/轨迹affordance）；(3) 提出VLAff——基于大型视觉-语言模型的统一基础模型，学习所有可操作affordance间的跨模态相关性；(4) 将生成的视觉热图、抓取姿态和轨迹结合3D场景信息转换为机器人可直接执行的行动。

Result: VLAff在视觉affordance预测任务上达到最先进水平，并能有效应用于真实机器人场景，包括零样本操纵和affordance引导的机器人学习。

Conclusion: 通过从人类视频提取形态无关的物体中心可操作affordance并利用大规模视觉-语言基础模型统一建模，可以有效克服人类与机器人之间的形态差异问题，实现从人类视频到机器人操纵技能的可扩展迁移。

Abstract: Learning manipulation skills from human videos is promising for scalable robot learning. However, the embodiment mismatch between humans and robots makes this challenging. One promising solution is to learn object-centric actionable affordances that are embodiment-agnostic. In this work, we propose a framework that leverages egocentric human videos with state-of-the-art 3D Structure-from-Motion and hand mesh reconstruction to extract actionable affordances such as visual, grasp, and trajectory affordances that explicitly encode where to interact, how to grasp, and how to move. We construct EgoAffordance, a large-scale dataset comprising 204K episodes with 5.6M visual affordances and 11.6M grasp and trajectory affordances. Building on this, we introduce VLAff, a large vision-language model-based unified foundation model that learns cross-modal correlations across all actionable affordances. Given a visual observation and instruction, VLAff generates visual affordance heatmaps, grasp poses, and trajectories, which are then converted into directly executable actions by utilizing 3D scene information. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that VLAff not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual affordance prediction, but can also be effectively applied to real robot applications such as zero-shot manipulation and affordance-guided robot learning.

</details>


### [259] [A System for Train Condition Monitoring and Structural Health Assessment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05221)
*Maximilian Posner,Martin Dazer,Daniela Lauer,Robert Winkler-Höhn,Mathilde Laporte,Tobias Herrmann,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将结构传感器技术与AI数据分析相结合的实时车辆状态监测与冲击检测方法，用于解决干线铁路应用中GoA4全自动运行面临的安全挑战，涵盖冲击检测、基于状态的维护和长期数据驱动的设计优化三大应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路系统数字化和AI应用正在改变轨道车辆的各个方面。虽然GoA4级全自动运行在地铁系统中已成熟，但由于严格的安全要求和开放运行环境的复杂性，其在干线铁路中的部署仍受限。现有基于摄像头、雷达和激光雷达的感知系统能有效检测物体，但难以可靠识别碰撞、撞击和碾压事件，需要新的解决方案来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 提出一种整合结构传感器技术与AI数据分析的新型实时车辆状态监测与冲击检测框架。该框架针对三个关键应用：(1)自动检测冲击、结构损伤和碾压事件；(2)通过连续监测实现基于状态的维护；(3)利用长期数据分析支持车辆设计优化。

Result: 结果显示所提方法可行，展示了其提升运营安全、实现预测性维护策略以及支持干线铁路系统向全自动运行过渡的潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够弥补现有感知系统的不足，为干线铁路实现完全自动化运营提供结构感知与AI分析相结合的技术支撑，有助于推进铁路行业的安全性与运维效率提升。

Abstract: The ongoing digitalization of rail systems and the increasing use of artificial intelligence (AI) are fundamentally transforming the design, operation, and maintenance of rail vehicles. While fully automated operation at Grade of Automation 4 (GoA4) is well established in metro systems, its deployment in mainline rail remains limited. This is primarily due to stringent safety requirements and the complexity of open operational environments. Current perception systems based on cameras, radar, and lidar are effective in detecting objects but provide limited capability for reliably identifying impacts, collisions, and driving-over events. This paper presents a novel approach for real-time vehicle condition monitoring and impact detection that integrates structural sensor technologies with AI-based data analysis. The proposed framework addresses three key applications: (1) automated detection of impacts, structural damage, and driving-over events, (2) condition-based maintenance enabled by continuous monitoring, and (3) long-term data analytics to support vehicle design optimization. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and highlight its potential to enhance operational safety, enable predictive maintenance strategies, and support the transition toward fully automated operation in mainline rail systems

</details>


### [260] [Failing Gracefully: Mitigating Impact of Inevitable Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05313)
*Duc M. Nguyen,Saad A. Ghani,Andrew Marshall,Allison Andreyev,Gregory J. Stein,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的机器人故障安全公式，通过评估故障期间机器人与周围实体之间高影响交互的概率及其后果严重程度，使机器人能够在安全性和任务效率之间做出平衡的规划决策。同时提出了FailBench仿真框架，基于MuJoCo支持多种故障模式下的机器人-环境交互系统性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在家庭环境中与人类、宠物和日常物品共享空间，极易遭遇软件崩溃、硬件退化或不可预测的交互等故障。虽然研究者努力减少故障，但某些故障不可避免，因此缓解其潜在后果对安全可靠的部署至关重要。

Method: 提出一个安全公式，同时评估故障期间机器人与周围实体间高影响交互的概率及后果严重程度；并构建FailBench，一个基于MuJoCo的仿真框架，支持多种故障模式（包括传感问题和执行器故障）下的机器人-环境交互系统评估。

Result: 该安全公式使机器人能够做出平衡安全与任务效率的明智规划决策；FailBench为在真实家庭环境中开发更安全稳健的运动规划和习得策略提供了基础支持。

Conclusion: 安全公式与FailBench共同为在真实家庭环境中开发更安全、更稳健的运动规划和学习策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Service robots operate in household environments shared with humans, pets, and everyday objects, where they are highly susceptible to failures such as software crashes, hardware degradation, or unpredictable interactions. While roboticists strive to minimize failures, some remain inevitable, making it critical to mitigate their potential consequences for safe and reliable deployment. This paper introduces a novel safety formulation that evaluates both the probability of impactful interactions between robots and surrounding entities during failures, and the severity of their outcomes. By quantifying the impact of failures on different entities, our approach enables robots to make informed planning decisions that balance safety with task efficiency. To support systematic evaluation, we also present FailBench, a MuJoCo-based simulation framework for studying robot-environment interactions under diverse failure modes, including sensing issues and actuator malfunctions. Together, our safety formulation and FailBench provide a foundation for developing safer and more robust motion plans and learned policies in real-world household environments.

</details>


### [261] [Unified Planning-Learning Framework for Robust UUV Navigation Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05365)
*Md Ether Deowan,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖观测的UUV自主导航框架，融合持续占用地图构建、全局清障感知规划与风险感知局部控制，通过行为树蒸馏和不确定性校准机制在部分可观测动态水下环境中实现鲁棒、安全的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下无人潜航器（UUV）在动态环境中面临部分可观测、传感器噪声大、环境动态变化等挑战。现有纯学习或纯规划方法难以兼顾全局导航结构的长期规划和局部避碰的实时性，且缺乏可重复的评估协议来验证鲁棒性。

Method: 采用三层混合架构：(1) 仅从声纳和深度图像构建持久占用地图；(2) 清障约束的全局规划器提供长期导航结构；(3) 强化学习策略处理短距离跟踪与反应式避障。引入行为树蒸馏配合分阶段监督以提升安全性和训练稳定性；通过不确定性校准蒸馏机制，利用在线潜模型的不确定性对教师指导重新加权；学习紧凑潜状态表征编码环境结构、障碍物动力学和不确定性；TTC和清障线索显式保留在规划和局部策略特征中。

Result: 在高保真GPU加速的NVIDIA Isaac Sim仿真中建立可重复的多种子评估协议，与BT-only和标准RL基线对比，结果表明框架在动态条件下鲁棒性和安全性均有提升。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种统一的混合规划-学习架构和可重复的方法论，为部分可观测动态水下环境中的鲁棒UUV自主导航提供了通用流水线。

Abstract: This paper presents an observation-only autonomy framework for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs) navigation in dynamic underwater environments that integrates persistent occupancy mapping, global clearance-aware planning, and risk-aware local control. The proposed pipeline constructs occupancy maps solely from onboard sonar and depth image observations, adapts a clearance-constrained global planner (GP) to provide long-horizon structure, and integrates a reinforcement learning (RL) policy to handle short-range tracking and reactive avoidance. To further support decision-making under partial observability, the system learns a compact latent state representation from onboard sensor data, encoding environmental structure, obstacle dynamics, and uncertainty. Behavior tree (BT) distillation with staged supervision is introduced to improve safety and training stability, while an uncertainty-calibrated distillation mechanism reweights teacher guidance using online latent-model uncertainty, emphasizing uncertain regimes during learning, with time-to-collision (TTC) and clearance cues remaining explicit in planning and local policy features. To demonstrate the efficacy of the framework, a reproducible multi-seed evaluation protocol is established in high-fidelity GPU-accelerated simulation using NVIDIA Isaac Sim, and performance is benchmarked against BT-only and standard RL baselines. The results obtained demonstrate improved robustness and safety under dynamic conditions, thus providing a general pipeline with a unified hybrid planning learning architecture and a reproducible methodology for robust UUV autonomy under partial observability.

</details>


### [262] [World-to-Wrist: Task-Conditioned Future Wrist Modeling for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05369)
*Yuhao Pan,Haosong Peng,Zhengshen Zhang,Zhengyang Yan,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Chujie Wang,Tianyu Qi,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: W2-VLA是一种视觉-语言-动作模型，通过任务条件化的未来腕部建模来提升精细机器人操作性能。它引入潜在建模token作为视觉语言模型与腕部预测器之间的紧凑接口，预测未来腕部潜在状态以生成未来感知上下文用于动作预测，并配套W2-CoT合成流程提供结构化标注辅助监督。在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实操作任务上验证了精细及接触敏感操作的改进，同时保持80Hz以上的动作生成速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常将主视角和腕部视角视为平行视觉输入，忽略了它们在机器人操作中的不同角色。精细操作需要预见腕部局部交互在全局任务上下文下如何演化，而现有方法未充分利用腕部视角的独特时序信息。

Method: (1) 提出W2-VLA架构，包含潜建模token作为视觉语言模型与腕部预测器之间的紧凑接口；(2) 腕部预测器基于接口和已观察腕部历史预测未来腕部潜在状态，并转化为未来感知上下文；(3) 引入W2-CoT合成流程，生成描述操作进度、物理转换线索和腕部局部证据的结构化标注，提供辅助监督以塑造任务条件化潜在接口。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务中，W2-VLA在单臂和双臂场景下均展现出改进的精细和接触敏感操作性能，同时保持超过80Hz的动作生成速率。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模腕部视角的未来演化并耦合任务上下文，W2-VLA有效解决了现有VLA模型忽视多视角信息角色差异的问题，在保持实时操作效率的前提下提升了精细接触操作的能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often treat main-view and wrist-view observations as parallel visual inputs, overlooking their distinct roles in robot manipulation. Fine-grained manipulation, however, benefits from anticipating how wrist-local interactions may evolve under the global task context. To address this limitation, we present World-to-Wrist VLA (W2-VLA), a VLA model for fine-grained robot manipulation with task-conditioned future wrist modeling. Given current multi-view observations and a task instruction, W2-VLA contextualizes a set of latent modeling tokens as a compact interface between the vision-language model and the wrist predictor. Conditioned on this interface and the observed wrist history, the predictor forecasts future wrist latents, which are transformed into future-aware context for action prediction. In addition, we introduce W2-CoT, a synthesis pipeline that produces structured annotations describing manipulation progress, physical transition cues, and wrist-local evidence. These annotations provide auxiliary supervision that shapes the task-conditioned latent interface. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved fine-grained and contact-sensitive manipulation across both single-arm and bimanual settings, while maintaining action-generation rates above 80 Hz.

</details>


### [263] [Sliding Sensors: Configurable Confidence in State Estimation for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05410)
*Ella Walsh,Spencer Teetaert,Eric Diller,Timothy D. Barfoot,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种机械可重构感知方案，通过将传感器在连续体机器人体内纵向滑动，实现在状态估计中对不确定性的空间整形，从而在任务相关位置获得足够的估计置信度，并相比固定尖端传感器降低了全身形状态估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在不确定环境中运行时，状态估计的准确性至关重要。然而，估计不确定性在空间上天然不均匀——置信度取决于测量可用的位置。全局估计精度并非总是首要目标，更重要的是在任务相关位置获得足够的置信度。这促使作者探索如何主动"整形"不确定性分布而非仅追求全局最优。

Method: 本文提出机械可重构感知的概念，设计了传感器在连续体机器人内部纵向平移的硬件方案，通过随时间滑动传感器位置来改变测量分布，从而对状态估计的不确定性进行空间整形。

Result: 验证了通过改变传感器位置可以重新配置状态估计的置信度分布，并展示了与固定尖端传感器相比，来回滑动传感器能够降低全身形状估计误差。

Conclusion: 机械可重构感知为连续体机器人提供了一种新的感知范式，能够基于任务需求主动整形估计不确定性，而非被动接受固定传感器带来的不均匀置信度分布。

Abstract: Continuum robots often operate in uncertain environments, where accurate state estimation is essential for safe interactions. Estimate uncertainty is inherently spatially non-uniform: confidence varies depending on where measurements are available. Global estimation accuracy is not always the top priority, but rather achieving sufficient confidence at task-relevant locations along the robot. This extended abstract introduces mechanically reconfigurable sensing enabling uncertainty-shaping in state estimation for continuum robots. We present a concept hardware design demonstrating the feasibility of longitudinal translation of a sensor within a continuum robot. We demonstrate that state estimation confidence can be reconfigured by varying the sensor location, and show a reduction of full-body shape estimation errors when sliding the sensor back and forth over time, compared to a single fixed tip sensor.

</details>


### [264] [ARGUS: Aligning Robot Scene Geometry Under Shifting Views with Large 3D Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05579)
*Rishik Sathua,Haonan Chen,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARGUS，一种观测预处理流水线，利用大规模3D视觉模型将任意相机视角的图像观测对齐到规范视角，然后再输入给下游视觉运动策略，从而解耦场景几何与视角之间的纠缠，实现从视角多样化数据中高效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视觉运动策略虽在机器人操作任务上表现优异，但常常将场景几何与对应视角纠缠在一起，学习的是物体在图像中的位置而非任务空间中的位置。这种纠缠限制了策略从视角多样化数据集（如DROID、BridgeV2）中学习的能力，也难以泛化到训练数据之外的视角。

Method: ARGUS作为观测预处理流水线，使用大规模3D视觉模型将来自任意相机视角的图像观测对齐到统一的规范视角（canonical viewpoint），然后传递给下游视觉运动策略进行处理。该方法不改变策略架构，仅通过标准化观测空间来简化学习任务。

Result: 在从固定多相机配置到高度变化相机摆放的多种训练数据集上进行了实验，ARGUS在有限视角和视角多样化训练场景下均一致优于先前方法。在效率对比中，ARGUS能够从视角多样化数据中学习，收敛到高成功率的速率比先前方法快4-6倍。

Conclusion: 利用大规模3D视觉模型可以减少下游视觉运动策略的学习负担，从而实现从大规模、视角多样化的机器人数据集中更高效地学习。这种方法有效地解开了视角与几何的纠缠，提升了策略的泛化能力和数据利用效率。

Abstract: Large-scale visuomotor policies have demonstrated impressive performance across a wide range of robot manipulation tasks. However, despite this success, manipulation polices often entangle scene geometry with the corresponding viewpoint, learning where objects lie in an image rather than where it lies in the task space. This entanglement inherently limits the corresponding policy's ability to learn from viewpoint-diverse datasets (ex. DROID, BridgeV2) and generalize beyond the viewpoints captured in their training data. In this work, we present ARGUS, an observation pre-processing pipeline that uses large-scale 3D vision models to align image observations from arbitrary camera viewpoints into a canonical viewpoint before passing it to downstream visuomotor policies. Experiments across training datasets with varying levels of viewpoint diversity, from fixed multi-view camera configurations to highly varied camera placements, show that our method consistently outperforms prior approaches across both limited-view and view-diverse training regimes. In efficiency comparisons, ARGUS demonstrates an ability to learn from view-diverse data, converging to high success rates 4-6x faster than previous methods by leveraging a simplified observation space. Overall, our findings show that leveraging large-scale 3D vision models reduces the learning burden on visuomotor policies, enabling more efficient learning from large-scale, viewpoint-diverse robot datasets.

</details>


### [265] [PathCover: A Fast Convex Decomposition along a Path via Randomized Iterative Space Partitioning (RISP) on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05586)
*Kunal S. Narkhede,Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Guoquan Huang,Ioannis Poulakakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PathCover框架，基于新型随机算法RISP，直接从原始点云数据以期望线性时间生成无碰撞凸多面体走廊，用于自主机器人导航中的实时轨迹规划，相比现有方法实现数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人导航需要快速生成无障碍区域用于轨迹规划，但现有走廊生成器难以满足实时、传感器速率的计算约束，成为系统瓶颈。

Method: PathCover框架采用RISP随机化算法，直接从原始点云数据构造凸多面体，在温和的概率消除条件下达到期望线性时间复杂度；生成重叠的无障碍多面体序列以约束下游MPC和轨迹优化，并数学保证算法在有限步内终止且沿任意无碰撞参考路径持续进展。

Result: 在合成和真实LiDAR数据集上的大量基准测试表明，相比最先进方法实现了一个数量级的加速，同时保持相当的走廊体积；通过高保真四旋翼仿真和四足机器人在受限环境中的实物部署验证了完整管线。

Conclusion: PathCover有效解决了走廊生成的实时计算瓶颈，在保证走廊质量的同时实现了数量级速度提升，并通过仿真和实物实验验证了其在实际机器人系统中的可行性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation requires the rapid generation of obstacle-free regions for trajectory planning. However, existing corridor generators struggle to meet real-time, sensor-rate computational constraints. To resolve this bottleneck, we introduce PathCover, a framework driven by RISP; a novel randomized algorithm that constructs convex polytopes directly from raw point cloud data in expected linear time under a mild probabilistic elimination condition. PathCover generates sequences of overlapping, obstacle-free polytopes that safely constrain downstream MPC and trajectory optimization. We mathematically guarantee that the algorithm terminates in finite steps while ensuring continuous progress along any obstacle-free reference path. Extensive benchmarks on synthetic and real-world LiDAR datasets demonstrate an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable corridor volumes. The complete pipeline is validated via high-fidelity quadrotor simulations and physical deployment on a quadrupedal robot navigating constrained environments using live LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [266] [Search-Aided Joint Agent-Environment Reinforcement Learning for Robust Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding with Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05588)
*He Jiang,Jingtian Yan,Yulun Zhang,Yimin Tang,Tanishq Duhan,Rishi Veerapaneni,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LMAPF-R2模型，该模型结合了鲁棒安全约束和原地旋转约束，用于模拟真实仓库系统中的终身多智能体路径规划问题，并提出了Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL)方法来解决该问题，实验表明该方法在多种高密度地图和混合现实仓库环境中取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身多智能体路径规划（LMAPF）方法大多采用过度简化的运动学假设，忽略了真实世界中对性能至关重要的运动约束。特别是鲁棒安全约束和原地旋转约束会显著增加协调难度，尤其是在高度受限的空间中。

Method: 提出两阶段方法SJRL：(1) 用单步搜索规划器Causal PIBT增强神经策略，解决智能体间的碰撞并传播其意图；(2) 引入统一的强化学习框架，联合优化智能体策略和环境策略，其中环境策略学习图边成本，通过反向Dijkstra搜索提供全局移动引导。

Result: 在多种高密度地图上，SJRL相对于强搜索基线Causal-PIBT取得了显著改进；此外在一个包含8台物理机器人和248台虚拟机器人的混合现实仓库环境中进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: SJRL通过结合搜索策略与联合RL优化，能够有效应对带有鲁棒安全和原地旋转约束的现实LMAPF问题，在仿真和物理混合现实环境中均展示了优越性能。

Abstract: Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding (LMAPF) requires repeatedly planning collision-free paths for agents that continuously receive new goals upon reaching their current ones. While many learning-based planners have been proposed for LMAPF, most rely on oversimplified kinematic assumptions that may overlook motion constraints critical to real-world performance. In this work, we study a more realistic LMAPF model derived from many real-world automated warehouse systems, termed LMAPF-R2, which incorporates robust safety constraints and in-place rotation constraints. These constraints substantially increase coordination difficulty, particularly in highly constrained spaces. To address these challenges, we propose Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL). We first augment neural policies with Causal PIBT, a single-step search-based planner that resolves agents' collisions and propagates their intentions. We then introduce a unified RL formulation that jointly optimizes agent and environment policies, where the environment policy learns graph edge costs to provide global movement guidance via backward Dijkstra search. Experiments demonstrate that SJRL achieves significant improvements over the strong search-based planner, Causal-PIBT, across multiple high-density maps. We further validate SJRL in a challenging mixed-reality warehouse environment with 8 physical robots and 248 virtual robots.

</details>


### [267] [KILVO: Kinematic-Inertial-LiDAR-Visual Odometry with Robust Multimodal Adaptation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05647)
*Jixin Gao,Fucheng Liu,Teng Zhang,Fusheng Zha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向人形机器人的运动学-惯性-激光-视觉里程计系统KILVO，通过异步-顺序混合误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波（ESIKF）框架融合关节编码器、IMU、LiDAR和相机多模态传感器数据，实现在传感器退化或故障情况下的高精度、高效率和高输出频率的状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人搭载多种传感器（关节编码器、IMU、LiDAR、相机），但现有融合方法未能充分利用这些传感器特性，且面对真实世界的复杂性（如传感器故障、退化）时鲁棒性不足，需要一个专门为人形机器人量身定制的高精度、高鲁棒性的多模态融合里程计方案。

Method: KILVO采用异步-顺序混合误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波（ESIKF）框架：IMU数据用于预测；腿部运动学以高频率异步处理提供本体感觉约束；外部感知按顺序更新，先通过LiDAR点云配准提供几何先验，再通过光度误差更新视觉分量。同时设计了多模态自适应机制以应对传感器故障，并开发了无需额外传感器的紧凑接触估计模块与状态估计共享信息。

Result: 在多个公开数据集和真实世界中多个人形机器人、多种步态模式和场景上的大量实验表明，KILVO在精度、效率和输出频率方面均具有高度竞争力，对传感器退化和故障表现出强鲁棒性，优于现有最先进的融合方法。

Conclusion: KILVO充分挖掘了人形机器人平台传感器的潜力，通过精心设计的异步-顺序多模态融合框架，在精度、效率和鲁棒性上均优于现有方法，更适合人形机器人在复杂真实环境中的应用，且代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: This article presents a kinematic-inertial-LiDAR-visual odometry for humanoid robots, called KILVO. Tailored to the platform features, requirements, and real-world complexity, it fully utilizes the sensors commonly equipped on humanoid robots, including joint encoders, IMU, LiDAR, and camera, within an asynchronous-sequential hybrid error-state iterated Kalman filter (ESIKF). Specifically, inertial data are used for prediction, leg kinematics are processed asynchronously at a high rate and provide proprioceptive constraints, while exteroception is updated sequentially, first by registering LiDAR points for geometric priors and then by updating the visual component via photometric errors. Moreover, the framework is elaborately designed with multimodal adaptation for resilience to sensor failures. A compact contact estimation module is also developed, sharing information with state estimation without additional sensors. Extensive experiments on public datasets and in the real world across multiple humanoid robots, gait patterns, and scenarios demonstrate that KILVO achieves highly competitive accuracy, efficiency, and output rates, with strong robustness against sensor degradation and failures, making it more suitable for humanoid robots than state-of-the-art fusion methods. Our code and datasets are released on GitHub.

</details>


### [268] [JoyAI-RA 0.5: Scaling Robot Manipulation Learning via Dual Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05674)
*JoyAI-RA Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JoyAI-RA 0.5是一个通用的视觉-语言-世界-动作(VLWA)框架，通过双动作对齐（隐式对齐推断潜在动作、显式对齐统一物理动作空间）将人类第一人称视频、仿真和真实机器人数据统一用于操作学习，并配合内外环强化阶段实现任务自适应与基础策略改进，在AgiBot真实基准上表现优异，且人类视频数据量增大时性能持续提升无饱和迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据稀缺，通用策略需从异构来源（人类手持视频、仿真、真实机器人）学习，这些数据在监督方式和具身形态上各异，动作标签缺失或互不兼容。人类数据规模最大但与机器人数据分布差异最大，朴素混合会导致负迁移而非知识共享。

Method: 1) 隐式动作对齐：从视觉转换推断潜在动作，使无动作标注的人类/仿真/机器数据引导潜在动作条件世界模型学习物理动力学；2) 显式动作对齐：通过规范动作表示和相机帧块相对末端执行器动作，将可靠的人类与机器人轨迹统一到物理动作空间；3) 内外环强化阶段：内环任务自适应，外环基础策略改进。

Result: 在真实世界AgiBot基准上，JoyAI-RA在已见任务和未见变体上均表现强劲。任务分数随人类第一人称预训练数据量的增加持续提升，且最大规模下未见平台期。

Conclusion: 丰富但弱标注的人类经验可转化为可迁移的训练信号，使人类视频不再仅是弱辅助数据源，而是操作能力可规模化扩展的主要轴心。

Abstract: Robot data is scarce, so generalist policies need to learn from heterogeneous sources, including human egocentric video, simulation, and real robots, which differ in supervision and embodiment, with action labels missing or mutually incompatible. Human egocentric data scale best but sit farthest from robot data, and naive pooling causes negative transfer rather than knowledge sharing. We propose JoyAI-RA 0.5, a generalist Vision-Language-World-Action (VLWA) framework that couples physical world-dynamics priors with visual semantics and scales manipulation learning across such data via dual action alignment. Implicit action alignment infers latent actions from visual transitions, enabling action-free human, simulation, and robot data to guide a latent-action-conditioned world model in learning physical dynamics. Explicit alignment grounds reliable human and robot trajectories in a unified physical action space through a canonical action representation and camera-frame chunk-relative end-effector actions. An inner-outer-loop reinforcement stage then pairs efficient task adaptation with foundation-policy improvement. On a real-world AgiBot benchmark, JoyAI-RA performs strongly on both seen tasks and unseen variations. The task score improves consistently as the volume of human egocentric pretraining data increases and shows no sign of plateauing at our largest scale. This suggests that abundant but weakly labeled human experience can be converted into a transferable training signal, making human video not merely a weak auxiliary source but a primary axis along which manipulation capability can be scaled. Project page can be found at https://joyai-ra-05.github.io/.

</details>


### [269] [Nonvisual Classification of Ground-Condition by Artificial Proprioception in an Amoeba-Inspired Autonomous Walking Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05684)
*Hyoto Yamaguchi,Zenji Yatabe,Seiya Kasai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究针对变形虫启发的自主行走机器人，提出了一种基于多模态传感的非视觉地面条件分类方法。通过集成三轴加速度计、八个足部压力传感器和储层计算（RC）实现人工本体感觉，即使四足机器人行走时传感器输出存在大幅波动，也能高精度区分平地与粗糙地面，并实现了根据地面条件实时切换行走步态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地面条件分类依赖于图像传感与处理，计算开销大且对环境依赖性强。本文旨在探索无需视觉传感的情况下，仅通过本体感觉（加速度计、足部压力传感器）结合储层计算实现非视觉地面分类，以增强机器人在动态行走中的适应能力。

Method: 采用三轴加速度计和八个足部压力传感器采集多模态传感数据，作为人工本体感觉的输入；利用储层计算（Reservoir Computing, RC）这一递归神经网络框架对传感器时序数据进行特征提取与分类，区分平地（flat）与粗糙地面（rough）两种地面条件。

Result: 系统在四足机器人动态行走导致传感器输出大幅波动的条件下，仍能以较高精度完成地面条件分类；机器人实现了根据地面条件实时切换行走步态；并分析了各传感器对分类结果的贡献程度。

Conclusion: 基于多模态传感与储层计算的非视觉地面分类方法可行且有效，能够在不依赖图像传感的情况下实现地面条件识别与自适应步态切换，为足式机器人在未知环境中的自主适应提供了轻量化解决方案。

Abstract: Nonvisual classification of ground condition based on a multimodal sensing approach was investigated for an amoeba-inspired autonomous walking robot. To classify ground condition without image sensing and processing, we implemented artificial proprioception by integrating a three-axis accelerometer, eight foot pressure sensors, and reservoir computing (RC). Even when large fluctuations in the sensor outputs are caused by dynamic motions of a four-legged robot in walking, our system can classify the ground condition, flat or rough, with high accuracy.
