We demonstrate on-site switching of walking gait depending on ground condition in the robot. We also discuss the contribution of each sensor to ground condition classification.

</details>


### [270] [Hijacking Robots with a Piece of Paper: A Systematic Study of Physical Prompt Injection in VLM-Controlled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05715)
*S. M . Bhagya P. Samarakoon,M. A. Viraj J. Muthugala,W. K. R. Sachinthana,Mohan Rajesh Elara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了针对VLM控制机器人的物理提示注入攻击：提出四分类攻击分类法，在5670次试验中攻击成功率分别为GPT-4o (27.0%)、Gemini 2.5 Flash (29.4%)和Qwen3-VL-32B (5.0%)，并验证了三种防御措施（提示防御、两阶段验证、文本掩码）可有效降低风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs日益被部署为机器人系统中的规划器，将自然语言指令转化为基于视觉场景理解的执行动作。这种感知与指令执行的紧密耦合引入了新的攻击面——机器人视野中的对抗性文本可作为间接提示注入，因此亟需系统性研究此类攻击的威胁程度与防御方案。

Method: 提出四类攻击分类法（间接指示、任务重定义、权威冒充、冲突注入），构建20个攻击提示的基准，覆盖3种物理场景布局和3种命令表述（目的地特异性与规则明确性不同）。在3个前沿VLM（GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-VL-32B）上进行共5670次试验，并对推理轨迹进行分析，最后评估三种简单防御方案。

Result: 攻击成功率分别为27.0%（GPT-4o）、29.4%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）和5.0%（Qwen3-VL-32B），权威冒充和否定类攻击跨模型迁移。99.9%的成功入侵被知觉且承认；不同模型防御机制不同（Gemini显式拒绝，GPT-4o感知忽视）。防御效果：提示防御75-100%、两阶段验证85-100%、文本掩码100%，但可能损害需要读取场景标签的任务。

Conclusion: VLM控制的机器人操作对人可读的物理标识存在实质性脆弱性；简单防御能显著降低风险但涉及权衡——防御在基准中保持通用任务能力，但可能削弱需要读取场景内标签的任务。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as planners in robotic systems, where they translate natural-language commands into executable actions grounded in visual scene understanding. This tight coupling between perception and instruction-following introduces a new attack surface: adversarial text placed within the robot's visual field can act as an indirect prompt injection into the VLM's reasoning stack. We present a systematic study of physical prompt injection attacks against VLM-controlled sorting, introducing a four-category taxonomy, indirect signage, task redefinition, authority impersonation, and conflict injection, instantiated as a benchmark of 20 attack prompts evaluated across three physical scene layouts and three command formulations that vary in destination specificity and rule explicitness. Across 5,670 trials on three frontier VLMs (GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen3-VL-32B), attacks succeed at 27.0%, 29.4%, and 5.0% respectively, with authority-impersonating and negation attacks transferring across all three models. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals that successful compromise is nearly always conscious (99.9% acknowledgment rate), and that models defend through structurally different mechanisms, explicit rejection for Gemini, perceptual inattention for GPT-4o. We evaluate three simple mitigations: prompt-based defense (75-100% effective, model-dependent), two-stage verification (85-100%), and pre-processing text masking (100%). Our findings show that VLM-controlled manipulation is meaningfully vulnerable to human-readable physical signage, and that simple defenses substantially reduce risk, though defense choice involves trade-offs. The defenses preserve general task capabilities in our benchmark, but they may impair tasks that require reading in-scene labels.

</details>


### [271] [ATP: Anatomical Torque with Passivity-based Control Framework for Safe Upper-Limb Exoskeleton Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05723)
*Yu Chen,Gong Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出解剖学力矩与无源性控制（ATP）框架，用于上肢外骨骼的安全辅助。通过可扩展的肌肉骨骼仿真框架训练统一强化学习肌肉控制器，生成解剖学参考力矩；结合在线力矩细化方案和交互力矩控制器，在缆驱柔顺外骨骼上实现无需预定义轨迹的辅助控制，并以能量罐保证系统无源性。仿真与真实实验验证了长时程运动序列的精确跟踪及对实时人体运动的泛化能力，EMG实验表明在动态多关节任务中目标肌肉活动最多降低48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖学辅助主要应用于下肢外骨骼，其周期性、承重运动对力矩精度要求较低；而将类似方法扩展到上肢复杂、非周期运动面临严峻挑战。该工作旨在实现泛化于多种上肢运动、具有安全性和舒适性的解剖学辅助控制。

Method: 1）构建可扩展的肌肉骨骼仿真框架，训练统一强化学习肌肉控制器，跨越多种上肢运动并生成解剖学参考力矩，无需复杂生物力学计算；2）设计在线力矩细化方案，适应不同运动、抑制肌腱诱发力矩尖峰，并引入学习到的异常分数以确保安全舒适；3）采用交互力矩控制器通过缆驱柔顺外骨骼传递辅助力，不约束运动至预定轨迹，同时以能量罐保持系统无源性，提供力矩跟踪和系统无源性理论保证。

Result: 仿真和真实实验显示：控制器能准确跟踪长时间运动序列，并泛化到实时人体运动；在保持无源性的前提下实现精确力矩跟踪，能量罐补充后可恢复跟踪。EMG实验（5名受试者）表明，在静态和动态任务中目标肌肉活动相比重力补偿和开环辅助均有降低，动态多关节任务中相对无外骨骼状态最多降低48%。

Conclusion: ATP框架成功将解剖学辅助从下肢拓展至复杂、非周期的上肢运动，实现了安全、舒适且具有泛化能力的外骨骼辅助，为上肢外骨骼的解剖学驱动控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Providing assistance across diverse movements is a central objective of exoskeletons, and anatomical knowledge can enable responsive support that generalizes across tasks. However, anatomical assistance has mainly been studied for lower-limb exoskeletons, where periodic, weight-bearing motions impose lower demands on torque precision. Extending such assistance to complex, nonperiodic upper-limb movements remains challenging. This paper proposes Anatomical Torque with Passivity-Based Control (ATP) for safe upper-limb exoskeleton assistance. First, a scalable musculoskeletal simulation framework trains a unified reinforcement-learning muscle controller that generalizes across upper-limb movements and generates anatomical reference torques without complex biomechanical computations. Second, an online torque-refinement scheme adapts the reference to diverse movements, suppresses tendon-induced spikes, and incorporates a learned anomaly score for safe and comfortable assistance. Third, an interaction torque controller delivers assistance through a cable-driven compliant exoskeleton without constraining motion to predefined trajectories, while an energy tank preserves passivity with theoretical guarantees on torque tracking and system passivity. Simulations and real-world experiments show accurate tracking of long-duration motion sequences and generalization to real-time human movements. The controller achieves accurate torque tracking while preserving passivity and resumes tracking after energy-tank replenishment. An EMG study with five participants further shows reduced target-muscle activity during static and dynamic tasks compared with gravity compensation and open-loop assistance, with reductions of up to 48% relative to movement without the exoskeleton in a dynamic multi-joint task.

</details>


### [272] [Near-sensor Computing for Rapid Visuotactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05725)
*Zhengying Zhu,Ruilin Zhang,Runze Hu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一种基于近传感器计算的视觉触觉传感器硬件流水线，包含全流式的谱泊松求解器。该流水线在166 MHz下以固定延迟0.211 ms处理128x128帧，功耗仅347 mW，重建精度为峰值接触深度的0.17%，并将机器人保护性反射回路的闭环时间从169.9 ms缩短至28.3 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉触觉传感器依赖主机端处理，导致功耗高、数据传输延迟大且存在可变调度延迟，限制了机器人系统的感知与响应速度，无法满足快速接触响应的需求。

Method: 实现近传感器计算框架，将光谱泊松求解器设计为全流式硬件流水线。该流水线无数据依赖性分支和迭代收敛过程，确保确定性的延迟；硬件在128x128帧尺度上以166 MHz运行，支持片上决策。

Result: 核心逻辑功耗估计为347 mW；流水线在166 MHz下，从接收第一个输入像素到产生第一帧深度值的固定延迟为35,107个周期（0.211 ms）；15种接触几何下重建深度与双精度参考结果偏差仅为峰值接触深度的0.17%；基于片上重建的机器人保护反射回路闭环时间为28.3±4.9 ms，远优于主机端实现的169.9±27.8 ms。

Conclusion: 近传感器重建能够在适合快速机器人接触响应的时间尺度上，提供准确、节能且确定性的触觉几何信息，为高速触觉感知驱动的机器人控制提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Visuotactile sensors reconstruct dense contact geometry from measured surface gradients, but host-based processing increases power consumption and introduces data-transfer delays and variable scheduling latency, limiting the sensing and response speed of robotic systems. To address these limitations, we implement a near-sensor computing framework that includes a spectral Poisson solver as a fully streaming hardware pipeline. The computational core logic has an estimated power consumption of 347 mW and achieves high throughput without data-dependent branching or iterative convergence, thereby providing deterministic latency. Operating at 166 MHz, the pipeline produces the first depth value of each 128x128 frame 35,107 cycles after receiving the first input pixel, corresponding to a fixed latency of 0.211 ms. Across 15 contact geometries, the reconstructed depths differ from a double-precision reference by 0.17 % of the peak contact depth. On-chip decisions based on these reconstructions close a robot protective reflex loop in 28.3 +/- 4.9 ms, compared with 169.9 +/- 27.8 ms for an equivalent host-based loop using the same actuator. These results demonstrate that near-sensor reconstruction can provide accurate, energy-efficient, and deterministic tactile geometry on timescales suitable for rapid robotic contact responses.

</details>


### [273] [In-Context VLA: Endowing Vision-Language-Action Models with Language via In-Context Post-Training and Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05738)
*Jiarui Yang,Wen Huang,Jiale Zhang,Maowei Hu,Hang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种VLA框架，通过上下文后训练和智能体工具接口赋予视觉-语言-动作模型语言理解能力而非语言生成能力，在模拟和真实机器人操作基准上超越CoT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型普遍使用行为克隆训练，但自由文本链式思维（CoT）会损害底层控制：推理无根据、延迟破坏闭环时序、推理与动作token目标冲突导致策略学会叙述而非行动。因此需要一种让VLA消费有根据的语言而非生成语言的方法。

Method: 提出框架包含两部分：(i) 上下文后训练——将感知证据注入为结构化上下文，仅在动作上监督模型；(ii) 智能体工具使用接口——策略主动查询开放词汇检测器、单目深度和视觉-语言模型获取任务相关信息。此外，数据引擎生成多样化、改写且基于证据的空间描述，使策略学会解释从未逐字见过的语言。

Result: 在RoboCasa-GR1、SimplerEnv、LIBERO模拟基准以及8个真实机器人操作任务上，与匹配配置下的CoT方法相比，该方法在性能和效率上均达到SOTA结果。

Conclusion: VLA需要的是语言理解能力而非语言生成能力。通过注入结构化感知证据作为上下文并仅在动作上监督，加上智能体工具接口主动获取信息，可以有效提升VLA在操作任务中的表现和效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become the dominant recipe for generalist manipulation, yet they are almost universally trained by behavior cloning: a policy imitates expert action chunks conditioned on a static image and a fixed instruction. A natural remedy is to inject explicit reasoning through textual chain-of-thought (CoT). We show, both empirically and analytically, that free-form textual CoT degrades low-level control: the reasoning it produces is ungrounded, its latency breaks closed-loop timing, and, crucially, the reasoning and action tokens are optimized against conflicting objectives so that the policy learns to narrate rather than to act. We argue that what a VLA needs is not the ability to generate language, but the ability to consume grounded language. To this end we introduce \textbf{\ourmethod{}}, a framework that endows a VLA with language competence through (i) in-context post-training, in which perceptual evidence is injected as structured context and the model is supervised only on actions, and (ii) an agentic tool-use interface, in which the policy queries open-vocabulary detectors, monocular depth, and a vision--language model to actively acquire task-relevant information. Rather than emitting a single templated caption, our data engine produces diverse, paraphrased, and evidence-conditioned spatial descriptions, so that the policy learns to interpret language it has never seen verbatim. Across the RoboCasa-GR1, SimplerEnv, and LIBERO simulation benchmarks, together with 8 real-world robot manipulation tasks, our method consistently achieves SOTA results in both performance and efficiency when compared with CoT-based approaches under matched configurations.

</details>


### [274] [Acoustic-driven millimetric helical robot: ultrasonic synergistic manipulation in confined fluidic environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05746)
*Hanlin Wang,Xin Wang,Xinwei Wei,Jiaxu Liu,Le Wang,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多声场协同驱动策略，通过声辐射力与声流流的协同作用，实现毫米级螺旋机器人在受限生物环境中的可控运动，包括平面导航、倾斜攀爬和垂直运动，并在猪静脉血管中验证了往复运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声场驱动的非接触操纵作为一种非侵入性微纳操控策略被广泛用于微纳尺度对象，但将其扩展到毫米级机器人时，在受限生物环境中推进效率不足，成为制约其应用的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出多声场协同方法，利用声辐射力和声流流的协同作用驱动毫米级螺旋机器人；采用多物理场仿真捕获联合声场下机器人的动力学行为，并通过实验验证其运动能力，包括平面导航、爬坡和垂直运动；开展半自主导航实验评估操控性，并在猪静脉血管中进行体外测试验证生物受限环境下的运动能力。

Result: 实验证实了毫米级螺旋机器人在多声场下的多种运动能力；半自主导航实验表明超声协同显著改善了机器人的操控性；猪静脉血管体外实验证实协同声场可支持单向和往复运动。

Conclusion: 研究为将声学微操纵扩展至毫米尺度提供了机理见解，支持需要灵活可控机器人运动能力的生物医学应用。

Abstract: Acoustic field-driven manipulation provides a non-contact and non-invasive strategy for controlling microscale and nanoscale objects, yet its extension to millimeter-scale robots was limited by insufficient propulsion efficiency in confined biological environments. Here, a coordinated multi-acoustic-field approach is introduced, which harnesses the synergistic action of acoustic radiation forces and acoustic streaming flows to enable controlled locomotion of millimeter-scale helical robots and enhance propulsion. Multiphysics simulations captured the dynamics of millimeter-scale helical robots under combined acoustic fields, and experimental validation demonstrated their locomotion capabilities, including planar navigation, inclined climbing, and vertical motion. Semi-autonomous navigation experiments further confirmed that ultrasonic synergy substantially improved maneuverability. In vitro tests in porcine venous vessels demonstrated that coordinated acoustic fields supported both unidirectional and reciprocating motion under biologically relevant confinement. These findings provide mechanistic insight into scaling acoustic micromanipulation to the millimetre regime and support biomedical applications requiring versatile and controllable robotic mobility.

</details>


### [275] [XEWorld: Can Action-Conditioned World Models Generalize to Unseen Robot Embodiments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05799)
*Yixiang Chen,Jiabing Yang,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Keji He,Peiyan Li,Kai Wang,Ziheng He,Xiangnan Wu,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出XEWorld交叉具身测试平台，系统揭示当前action-conditioned世界模型本质上是2D视觉模式匹配器，其跨具身泛化由视觉相似性而非物理运动学相似性驱动。模型无法将抽象数值关节动作转化为连贯视觉轨迹，也难以从静态初始观测预测动态视觉变化；即便通过少样本适应绕过零样本障碍，外观恢复也会导致对已见具身的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型的评估仅在训练机器人上进行，无法区分模型是否真正捕捉了物理动力学还是仅记忆了视觉模式。为了回答"模型能否忠实地渲染从未见过的机器人"这一核心问题，需要一个能将具身因素隔离的受控测试平台来揭示模型的真实泛化能力。

Method: 构建XEWorld（受控跨具身测试台），在物理场景完全相同的条件下评估未见过的机器人，实现具身因素的隔离。通过系统性分析不同架构模型的表现，考察其跨具身泛化行为，并对比零样本与少样本适应两种评估范式。

Result: 发现所有当前模型共享同一架构瓶颈：它们本质上作为2D视觉模式匹配器工作，泛化由视觉相似性而非物理运动学相似性支配。模型难以将数值关节动作翻译为连贯视觉轨迹，且无法从静态初始观测预测动态视觉变化。零样本呈现未见具身必须依赖像素空间动作和显式时空对齐这类强接地线索。通过少样本适应绕过零样本障碍时，强制外观恢复引发了已见具身的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 这些失败暴露了模型无法将已学习的物理动力学应用于新视觉外观的根本缺陷，表明实现真正的跨具身泛化需要将视觉外观与底层物理动力学解耦的架构创新。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are promising learned simulators for robotic manipulation, yet evaluating them exclusively on training robots fails to reveal whether they capture physical dynamics or merely memorize visual patterns. To answer whether a model can faithfully render a robot it has never seen, we introduce XEWorld, a controlled cross-embodiment testbed for world models that isolates embodiments by evaluating held-out robots within physically identical scenes. Our systematic analysis uncovers a shared architectural bottleneck: current models act primarily as 2D visual pattern matchers whose generalization is governed by visual similarity rather than physical kinematic similarity. Driven by this limitation, they struggle to translate abstract numeric joint actions into coherent visual trajectories, and fail to predict dynamic visual changes from static initial observations. Consequently, successfully rendering an unseen embodiment zero-shot strictly requires heavily grounded cues, specifically pixel-space actions and explicit spatial-temporal alignment. Even when bypassing this zero-shot barrier via few-shot adaptation, the forced appearance recovery triggers catastrophic forgetting of seen embodiments. Together, these failures expose a critical inability to apply learned physical dynamics to novel visual appearances, highlighting that achieving true cross-embodiment generalization requires architectural innovations that decouple visual appearance from underlying physical dynamics.

</details>


### [276] [Coordinated Multi-Robot Disassembly for Makespan Optimization of Large-Scale Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05830)
*Niklas Hargus,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoMuDi是一种用于多机器人拆卸任务的协同任务与运动规划方法，通过创建设备的复合任务、传播时间约束，并集成ST-RRT*规划器，在狭窄工作空间中协调多机器人运动，最小化整体完工时间。实验表明在多达49个零件和9个机器人的场景下，CoMuDi-SRRT*组合具有高成功率、低空闲时间和低完工时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人拆卸任务需要机器人在狭窄工作空间中协同运动，同时避免碰撞。传统的多机器人任务与运动规划方法难以充分利用不同机器人时间的协同优势，导致整体完工时间（makespan）较慢且成功率较低。

Method: 提出CoMuDi方法：输入为机器人团队、物体装配体和依赖图；生成拾取、放置和退出运动等复合任务；通过传播时间约束确保每个机器人尽可能早地开始和结束任务，同时避免与附近机器人碰撞；集成ST-RRT*规划器以确保单个任务最小化到达时间，从而降低整体完工时间。

Result: 对比CoMuDi结合ST-RRT*和RRT*规划器在不同时间限制下的表现，结果表明CoMuDi与ST-RRT*的结合具有更高的成功率和更小的完工时间。在多达49个零件和9个机器人的六种装配场景中，CoMuDi返回的机器人路径表现出低空闲时间，能可靠解决大规模装配问题。

Conclusion: CoMuDi通过时间约束传播与ST-RRT*的集成，能有效协调多机器人拆卸任务，在复杂大规模场景下实现高成功率、低完工时间和低空闲时间，证明了该方法在多机器人拆卸规划中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-robot task and motion planning for disassembly tasks requires robots to operate in confined workspaces while coordinating their motions with other robots. To tackle this problem, we propose a planning method called coordinated multi-robot disassembly (CoMuDi). CoMuDi coordinates a team of robots for disassembly tasks. The input is a team of robots, an assembly of objects, and a dependency graph. Based on this information, we create compound tasks for pick, place, and exit motions. By propagating temporal constraints, we ensure that each robot can start and end their tasks as early as possible while avoiding collisions with nearby robots. By integrating the space-time RRT* planner (ST-RRT*) into CoMuDi, we ensure that individual tasks minimize arrival time and thereby help us minimize overall makespan. We compare the performance of CoMuDi using both ST-RRT* and RRT* planners with varying time bounds, demonstrating that the combination of CoMuDi and ST-RRT* leads to a higher success rate while minimizing makespan. Finally, we evaluate CoMuDi on six assemblies with up to 49 pieces and up to 9 robots. In those scenarios, we show that CoMuDi returns robot paths that exhibit low idle times, thereby demonstrating that CoMuDi can reliably solve large-scale assemblies.

</details>


### [277] [SkillMemo: Expert-guided Skill Memory Framework for Compositional Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05970)
*Changyuan Wang,Chubin Zhang,Zhenyu Wu,Runhao Li,Angyuan Ma,Ke Chao,Yinan Liang,Xiuwei Xu,Ziwei Wang,Yansong Tang,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMemo框架，通过隐式分解长程示教轨迹为潜在原子技能并整合到动态情景记忆库中，解决具身视觉运动模型（如DP和VLA）因大规模轨迹数据稀缺而导致的组合泛化不足问题，在模拟和真实世界操作任务中均取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉运动模型（DP、VLA）在机器人操作基准上表现优异，但其潜力受限于大规模具身轨迹数据的稀缺性，导致在分布外（OOD）场景下组合泛化能力不足，难以捕获可复用的技能结构。

Method: SkillMemo框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）架构的专家引导轨迹分割模块，通过学习的门控系数隐式地将长程示教轨迹划分为不同的技能原语；(2) 技能级情景记忆架构，以键值对形式存储紧凑的技能表示。推理时，记忆库检索最相关的技能原语并与模型当前门控分布融合，提供上下文先验以优化动作预测。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实世界操作任务上的大量实验表明，SkillMemo一致性地增强了DP和VLA骨干模型，达到SOTA性能并超越π_0.5，在未见过的任务配置上展现出强大的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillMemo通过隐式技能分解和动态情景记忆检索有效地弥补了具身轨迹数据稀缺的问题，为提升视觉运动模型的组合泛化能力提供了一种通用且有效的框架。

Abstract: Embodied visuomotor models, including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, have demonstrated promising performance on robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, their potential remains fundamentally constrained by the scarcity of large-scale embodied trajectory datasets, leading to insufficient compositional generalization in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios with limited capability to capture reusable skill structures. To address this limitation, we propose Skill-Based Memory (SkillMemo) framework that implicitly decomposes long-horizon demonstrations into latent atomic skills and integrates skill-level features into a dynamic episodic memory bank for solving compositional tasks. Specifically, we first introduce an expert-guided trajectory segmentation module built upon a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture, which implicitly partitions trajectories into distinct skill primitives represented by learned gating coefficients. We further design a skill-level episodic memory architecture that stores compact skill representations as retrievable key-value pairs. During inference, the memory bank retrieves the most relevant skill primitives which are subsequently fused with the model's current gating distribution, providing a robust contextual prior to refine action predictions. Extensive experiments on the simulation benchmark and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that SkillMemo consistently enhances both DP and VLA backbones, achieving state-of-the-art performance and outperforming $π_{0.5}$, while exhibiting strong compositional generalization to unseen task configurations.

</details>


### [278] [TRACE: Learned Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots under Unreliable Contact Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05975)
*Taehyeon Kong,Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE（Tokenized Robust Attention for Contact-Aware Estimation），一种用于腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件下进行本体感受里程计估计的端到端学习方法。该方法通过足部感知交叉注意力模块自适应加权IMU和腿部运动学token，结合物理启发的辅助损失、策略随机化及部分真实世界微调，在多样化地形上实现了比经典滤波、混合和纯学习方法更低的位姿漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件（如打滑、接触失效）下，传统的接触感知里程计依赖人工定义的接触或滑移阈值，鲁棒性不足；而纯学习或混合方法在仿真到现实的迁移中也存在泛化困难。因此需要一种无需显式接触假设、能够自适应融合多源感知信息的端到端里程计估计方法。

Method: TRACE采用端到端学习框架，直接从机载IMU和关节测量的近期历史中预测相对位移、相对旋转和机体坐标系速度。核心组件包括：(1) 足部感知交叉注意力模块，自适应对IMU和逐腿运动学token加权，无需依赖手工定义的接触或滑移阈值；(2) 直接监督与两种物理启发的辅助损失，分别促进运动学一致性和腿部信息的可靠利用；(3) 训练策略包含策略随机化以提高仿真到现实的鲁棒性，并对时间编码器和预测头进行部分真实世界微调。

Result: 在多样化的室内外地形实验中，TRACE相比经典滤波方法、混合方法及纯学习方法在位置漂移方面均实现了一致的降低。消融研究进一步验证了所提出的训练目标、策略随机化和真实世界微调在不可靠接触和仿真到现实不匹配场景下的各自贡献。

Conclusion: TRACE证明了通过token化的注意力机制和物理感知的训练策略，可以在不依赖显式接触假设的情况下显著提升腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件下的里程计估计精度，同时缓解了仿真到现实的迁移问题，为腿式机器人本体感觉里程计提供了鲁棒且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present TRACE (Tokenized Robust Attention for Contact-Aware Estimation), an end-to-end learned proprioceptive odometry estimator for legged robots under unreliable contact conditions. The proposed estimator directly predicts relative displacement, relative rotation, and body-frame velocity from a recent history of onboard inertial and joint measurements. To improve robustness under unreliable contact conditions, we introduce a foot-aware cross-attention module that adaptively weights IMU and leg-wise kinematic tokens without relying on manually defined contact or slip thresholds. The estimator is trained with direct supervision and two physics-inspired auxiliary losses that promote kinematic consistency and reliable use of leg information. To reduce policy-specific overfitting and consequently improve sim-to-real transfer, simulation training incorporates policy randomization, followed by partial real-world fine-tuning of the temporal encoder and prediction head. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor terrains demonstrate consistent reductions in position drift compared with classical filtering-based, hybrid, and purely learning-based baselines. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of the proposed training objectives, policy randomization, and real-world fine-tuning, particularly under unreliable contacts and sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [279] [Beyond Flat Policies: Hierarchical Post-Training for Embodied Agents in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05999)
*He Kong,Zengjue Chen,Qi Wang,Qianli Xing,Runliang Niu,Peidong Liu,Jiawei Li,Shiqi Wang,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiRoC，一个用于VLA模型的分层后训练框架，将高层任务规划与低层动作执行解耦，通过强化学习持续改进动作生成，并在RL前对齐执行器与规划器生成的子目标以缓解分布不匹配，在多个机器人操作基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型后训练方法大多优化为扁平策略，难以显式建模任务进度并进行稳健的长程操作；虽然分层方法引入任务分解，但主要依赖离线示范的监督学习，无法通过在线交互有效改进执行。

Method: HiRoC分层后训练框架包含两个模块：(1) 规划器将复杂任务分解为可执行子目标，提供显式语义指导；(2) 执行器通过强化学习持续改进子目标条件下的动作生成。在RL之前，先将执行器与规划器生成的子目标对齐，以缓解规划与执行之间的分布不匹配，促进两模块有效协作。

Result: 在多个多样化的机器人操作基准上，HiRoC一致性地优于强基线方法。综合分析进一步验证了分层后训练的有效性以及每个关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 分层后训练范式能够有效提升VLA模型在长程机器人操作任务中的鲁棒性，规划-执行解耦结合RL在线优化和子目标对齐是关键所在。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robotic manipulation by leveraging pretrained vision-language models. However, existing post-training methods predominantly optimize VLA models as flat policies, making it difficult to explicitly model task progression and perform robust long-horizon manipulation. Although hierarchical approaches introduce task decomposition, they mainly rely on supervised learning from offline demonstrations and cannot effectively improve execution through online interaction. To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Robotic Control (HiRoC), a hierarchical post-training framework that decouples high-level task planning from low-level action execution. The planner decomposes complex tasks into executable subgoals to provide explicit semantic guidance, while the executor continuously improves subgoal-conditioned action generation through reinforcement learning. To enable effective collaboration between the two modules, we further align the executor with planner-generated subgoals before reinforcement learning, mitigating the distribution misalignment between planning and execution. Extensive experiments across diverse robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that HiRoC consistently outperforms strong baselines. Comprehensive analyses further validate the effectiveness of hierarchical post-training and the contribution of each key component.

</details>


### [280] [Adaptive-WAM: Quality-Guided Early-Exit Planning from Intermediate Video-Diffusion Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06008)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive-WAM，一种基于Wan2.2-5B骨干网络的质量感知多出口规划器。它通过在选定的DiT块上附加轨迹扩散头，并用轻量级轨迹质量评分器动态终止推理，避免了视频扩散模型迭代式去噪和VAE解码的高昂成本。在NAVSIM上达到90.8 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上获得89.9 EPDMS，并在A100上将端到端规划延迟降至170ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频扩散模型为自动驾驶提供了丰富的时空先验，但现有的世界-动作模型即使部署时只需要一条自车轨迹，仍继承了对未来视频进行迭代生成的高昂计算成本。作者提出一个更基本的问题：执行视频扩散模型的多少部分才能做出可靠的驾驶决策？

Method: 首先通过控制视频去噪时间步长和DiT深度进行受控实验，发现规划性能对视频噪声水平不敏感，且中间层已可解码出强轨迹。基于此观察，在选定的DiT块上附加轨迹扩散头，并设计一个轻量级轨迹质量评分器：当当前解码的最佳轨迹满足质量阈值时提前终止推理；否则从缓存的隐藏状态继续到更深的多出口。

Result: 在NAVSIM上，自适应单轨迹规划器达到90.8 PDMS；固定出口变体以64个提案达到92.6 PDMS；在NAVSIM v2上获得89.9 EPDMS，为对比的前视视频世界模型规划器中的最佳结果。无需目标域微调即可迁移至nuScenes，m平均L2误差0.88m，碰撞率0.08%。在A100上自适应路由将PDMS从90.62提升至90.79，平均端到端规划延迟170ms，比固定block-15规划器（190ms）低约10%，比固定全深度规划器（320ms）低47%。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型的大部分计算（迭代去噪、VAE解码、全深度骨干）在规划任务中是冗余的；通过质量感知的多出口动态路由，可以在保持甚至提升规划精度的同时大幅降低推理延迟，实现高效且可迁移的自动驾驶轨迹规划。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models provide rich spatiotemporal priors for autonomous driving, but existing world-action models often inherit the cost of iterative future-video generation even though deployment only requires an ego trajectory. We ask a more basic question: how much of a video diffusion model must be executed to make a reliable driving decision? Through a controlled study of video denoising timesteps and Diffusion Transformer (DiT) depth, we find that planning performance is largely insensitive to the tested video-noise levels, whereas strong trajectories can already be decoded from intermediate layers. Based on this observation, we introduce Adaptive-WAM, a quality-aware multi-exit planner built on a Wan2.2-5B backbone. Trajectory diffusion heads are attached to selected DiT blocks, and a lightweight trajectory-quality scorer terminates inference once the best trajectory decoded so far satisfies a quality threshold; otherwise, computation continues from the cached hidden state to a deeper exit. The deployed planner therefore avoids the iterative classifier-free denoising loop and VAE decoding required for future-video synthesis, while dynamically allocating backbone depth according to trajectory quality. On NAVSIM, the adaptive single-trajectory planner achieves 90.8 PDMS; a separate fixed-exit variant reaches 92.6 PDMS with 64 proposals. It further obtains 89.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, yielding the best reported results among the compared front-view video world-model planners. Without target-domain fine-tuning, Adaptive-WAM transfers to nuScenes with 0.88 m average L2 error and a 0.08\% collision rate. On an A100, adaptive routing improves PDMS from 90.62 to 90.79 while averaging 170 ms end-to-end planning latency, approximately 10\% below the 190 ms fixed block-15 planner and 47\% below the 320 ms fixed full-depth planner. Code will be released.

</details>


### [281] [Topometric Autonomous Vehicle Localization by Combining Visual Embeddings and Feed-Forward 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06021)
*Eulogio Quemada-Torres,Alberto Jaenal,Francisco-Angel Moreno,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合概率视觉位置识别（VPR）与前馈神经网络三维几何（FF3D）度量的拓扑度量定位框架，通过自动离线建图工具建模场景中位姿-外观交互，并利用在线粒子滤波将FF3D度量估计融入概率外观定位，在三个基准上显著优于现有外观方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉定位（VL）需要同时满足紧凑性、外观变化鲁棒性和度量精度，但VPR虽能通过低维图像嵌入实现紧凑性和外观鲁棒性，却缺乏度量精度；而基于局部特征或神经表示的VL方法精度虽高但扩展性受限。作者旨在通过融合VPR与FF3D模型来克服这一局限。

Method: 提出一种序列外观定位的拓扑度量框架，包含两部分：(1) 自动离线映射工具，建模场景各区域的拓扑度量位姿-外观交互；(2) 在线粒子滤波，从里程计和地点置信度估计位姿，并用于指导FF3D推理，从而将神经度量估计融入概率外观定位框架中。框架具有模块化特性，描述符提取器和FF3D模型可互换。

Result: 在三个已知基准数据集上进行了广泛评估，结果表明该方法大幅优于现有基于外观的定位方法。深入分析还表明，序列置信度能够在感知混淆（perceptual aliasing）情况下缓解严重失败。

Conclusion: 通过将概率VPR与FF3D神经度量估计在拓扑度量框架中迭代结合，成功弥补了VPR度量精度不足的缺陷，实现了紧凑、鲁棒且精确的视觉定位；框架的模块化设计使其具有良好的可扩展性与可替换性。

Abstract: Effective Visual Localization (VL) requires a map of the environment that combines compactness for efficient scalability with robustness against visual appearance changes and metric precision. Through low-dimensional image embeddings, Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is able to successfully meet the first two requirements, but its low metric accuracy makes it less suitable than standard VL approaches based on local features or neural representations. This limitation can be overcome by integrating VPR with the accurate local trajectory estimates produced by feed-forward neural 3D geometry (FF3D) models. In this paper, we address sequential appearance-based localization through a topometric framework that iteratively combines probabilistic VPR with FF3D metric pose estimation in controlled image sets. Our approach proposes an automatic offline mapping tool that models the topometric pose-appearance interaction in the different parts of the scene. This map is later employed by an online particle filter that estimates the pose from odometry and belief over places for FF3D inference, successfully incorporating neural metric estimation into probabilistic appearance-based localization. We extensively evaluate the framework on three known benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over existing appearance-based methods. The modularity of our approach allows the descriptor extractor and FF3D model to remain interchangeable, and a focused analysis further shows that sequential belief can mitigate severe failures under perceptual aliasing.

</details>


### [282] [IcFuzz: Fuzzing Isaac Sim with Semantic Stage Guidance and Multi-level Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06088)
*Zhixiang Chen,Zhuangbin Chen,Ruoxi Jia,Zeqin Liao,Wei Li,Jinyang Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了IcFuzz，首个针对NVIDIA Isaac Sim模拟器的模糊测试方法。通过LLM语义阶段分割、多层级变异算子和多臂老虎机算法实现自适应调度，在代码覆盖率和漏洞发现方面显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Isaac Sim作为机器人模拟器基础设施具有高复杂度，存在软件缺陷风险。现有模糊测试方法难以有效测试Isaac Sim，主要面临三个挑战：上下文相关的物体语义、层级化的模拟控制、以及巨大的模拟状态空间。

Method: IcFuzz包含三个核心组件：1) 基于LLM的语义阶段分割，将模拟程序分解为结构化阶段以捕获上下文感知的物体语义；2) 设计多层级变异算子，在层级粒度上系统性地测试模拟器；3) 采用多臂老虎机算法自适应调度变异算子，高效导航庞大的模拟状态空间。

Result: 实验结果表明IcFuzz优于基线方法：代码覆盖率达到基线的190%-205%；三次12小时测试中平均检测到3.7个独特崩溃而基线检测为零；在约四个月内发现11个bug，其中9个已被开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: 通过结合LLM语义分析与自适应调度策略，IcFuzz有效解决了Isaac Sim中复杂模拟环境的模糊测试难题，验证了语义感知模糊测试在机器人模拟器领域的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Robotics simulators serve as a foundational infrastructure for embodied AI, facilitating safe and scalable robotic system development. NVIDIA Isaac Sim has emerged as one of the most popular simulators, distinguished by its GPU-accelerated physics engine and photorealistic rendering, which enable high-fidelity modeling of complex environments. However, its inherent complexity inevitably introduces software bugs that can compromise simulation reliability. Existing fuzzing approaches struggle to test Isaac Sim effectively due to challenges of context-aware object semantics, hierarchical simulation control, and a vast simulation state space.
  In this paper, we propose IcFuzz, the first fuzzing approach for Isaac Sim. IcFuzz first performs an LLM-based semantic stage segmentation, decomposing simulation programs into structured stages that capture context-aware object semantics. Guided by this information, IcFuzz designs multi-level mutation operators to systematically exercise the simulator across hierarchical granularities. To efficiently navigate the vast simulation state space, IcFuzz employs a multi-armed bandit algorithm to adaptively schedule mutation operators. Experimental results show that IcFuzz outperforms the baselines in terms of both code coverage and bug detection. Specifically, IcFuzz achieves approximately 190\%--205\% of the code coverage of the baselines and detects an average of 3.7 unique crashes over three rounds of 12-hour tests, while no crashes are detected by the baselines. Moreover, IcFuzz has uncovered 11 bugs over approximately four months, 9 of which have been confirmed or fixed by the developers.

</details>


### [283] [Visual Grounding in Zero-Shot Vision-Language Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06154)
*J. de Curtò,Dayani Plasencia,Diego Sánchez,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过输入消融实验系统评估了视觉语言模型（VLMs）作为零样本控制器的可靠性，发现直接控制结果大多负面——多数模型对图像不敏感或输出恒定，且无法在镜像条件下保持对称性。但通过后验的对称一致性守护机制（symmetry-consensus guardian）选择模型并冻结2-of-4危险投票，可在272个保留帧上达到0.954平衡准确率。结论是当前VLM应作为有边界的选择性危险辅助工具，而非整体零样本控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLM越来越多地被用作零样本控制器，但成功的轨迹并不能证明决策真正基于视觉输入——模拟器动态和保守的动作先验可能在没有有意义的感知的情况下产生良好的分数。因此需要系统验证VLM感知与决策之间的因果关系。

Method: 设计了输入消融测试套件，包括盲图像控制、重复相同输入、车道轴对称反射、非视觉基线和流水线完整性检查。评估了九个直接动作模型、六个结构化局部VLM以及一个探索性的VLM-MPC层级，在32,874次评分调用中覆盖两个实体和三个模拟器。还提出了一个后验的、泄漏控制的对称一致守护机制，从16个校准帧中选择模型并冻结2-of-4风险投票。

Result: 直接控制结果大多负面：恒定SLOW策略优于脚本几何控制器；多个模型图像不变或近似恒定；能识别纵向危险但无法在反射下变换LEFT和RIGHT。确定性正对照组以0.090 m MAE估计领先距离并具有精确镜像对称性。守护机制在272个保留帧上达到0.954平衡准确率（95% CI [0.895,0.990]），12折嵌套留一集验证均恢复相同模型对和阈值。弃权时承诺平衡准确率达0.973，覆盖率为0.824。离线模块重放实现0.934动作一致性和精确镜像对称性。

Conclusion: 当前VLM应被视为有边界的选择性危险辅助工具，而非单调的零样本控制器。感知能力不足是模块化的而非普遍的，通过适当的守护和校验机制可以在保留视觉权威的前提下实现可靠的危险检测辅助。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as zero-shot controllers, but successful trajectories do not necessarily show that decisions are grounded in visual input: simulator dynamics and conservative action priors can produce favourable scores without meaningful perception. We investigate this with an input-ablation battery: blind-image controls, repeated identical inputs, lane-axis reflection, non-visual baselines, and pipeline-integrity checks. Across nine direct-action models, six structured local VLMs, and an exploratory VLM-MPC hierarchy, we analyse 32,874 scored calls over two embodiments and three simulators. The direct-control results are largely negative: a constant-SLOW policy outperforms a scripted geometric controller, several models are image-invariant or nearly constant, and models that recognize longitudinal hazards still fail to transform LEFT and RIGHT under reflection. No local VLM meets the joint longitudinal and lateral grounding criteria. However, an image-only deterministic positive control estimates the lead gap with 0.090 m MAE and exact mirror equivariance, confirming the stimuli carry sufficient visual information; the failures are modular, not universal. A post-hoc, leakage-controlled symmetry-consensus guardian selects two models from 16 calibration frames and freezes a 2-of-4 hazard vote across original and reflected views. On 272 held-out frames it reaches 0.954 balanced accuracy (episode-cluster bootstrap 95% CI [0.895,0.990]); nested leave-one-episode-out recovers the same pair and threshold in all 12 folds. Abstaining on ties raises committed balanced accuracy to 0.973 at 0.824 coverage. With deterministic perception retaining lateral authority, offline modular replay achieves 0.934 action agreement and exact mirror equivariance. These results support current VLMs as bounded, selective hazard assistants, not monolithic zero-shot controllers.

</details>


### [284] [Prior-SG: Task and Prior Driven Region Segmentation for Scene Graphs in Arbitrarily-Structured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06170)
*Giorgio Tonetti,Laurent Kneip,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Prior-SG是一种任务与先验驱动的层次化三维场景图生成框架，将场景图生成建模为概率对齐问题。它利用多尺度开放词汇特征融合构建实例图，通过大语言模型动态合成逻辑先验图，并以最大后验估计优化马尔可夫随机场，融合视觉、几何和离散物体等多种专家信息，在开放平面和任意结构环境中实现了SOTA的语义区域分割精度，同时提供零样本本体论灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维场景图提取框架依赖纯局部视觉聚类或严格几何启发式（如墙分隔的房间），在开放平面或任意结构环境中表现不佳，难以支持移动机器人的高层空间推理。

Method: Prior-SG采用概率对齐框架：1）利用多尺度开放词汇特征融合策略将RGB-D传感器流持续聚合成实例图；2）通过大语言模型动态合成先验图（对环境结构逻辑预期和任务相关词汇）；3）以最大后验估计推断高层次功能语义，通过优化融合视觉、几何和离散物体专家的马尔可夫随机场来解决局部感知歧义。

Result: 在多样化模拟住宅数据集和大型开放平面真实环境上验证，Prior-SG在语义区域分割精度上达到SOTA，在无物理墙壁条件下能稳健地划定远距离功能边界，并提供零样本本体论灵活性。

Conclusion: Prior-SG通过引入任务和先验驱动的概率对准机制，突破了传统几何启发式的局限，使机器人能够基于高层任务需求完全重构空间划分，为任意结构环境中的自主移动平台空间理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs are a promising representation for high-level spatial reasoning in autonomous mobile platforms. However, existing extraction frameworks typically rely on purely local visual clustering or strict geometric heuristics, such as wall-separated rooms, which fail in open-plan or arbitrarily-structured environments. We propose Prior-SG, a task- and prior-driven framework that casts scene graph generation fundamentally as a probabilistic alignment problem. As the robot explores, it continuously aggregates an incoming RGB-D sensor stream into a physically grounded Instance Graph utilizing a multi-scale, open-vocabulary feature fusion strategy. The system then infers the high-level functional semantics of this map through a Maximum A Posteriori (MAP) estimate, guided by a Prior Graph-a logical expectation of the environment's structure and task-relevant vocabulary synthesized dynamically by a Large Language Model. By optimizing a Markov Random Field that fuses heterogeneous experts (visual, geometric, and discrete objects) with these topological priors, the system resolves local perceptual ambiguities. We validate this approach across diverse simulated residential datasets and large, open-plan real-world environments. Prior-SG achieves state-of-the-art semantic region segmentation accuracy compared to recent baselines, robustly delineates distant functional boundaries in the absence of physical walls, and uniquely provides zero-shot ontological flexibility, enabling the robot to entirely restructure its spatial partitioning based on a given high-level task.

</details>


### [285] [ErgoSurf: Ergodic Control for the Coverage of Unknown Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06208)
*Stefan Schneyer,Timo Bachmann,Maged Iskandar,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Freek Stulp,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的在线遍历控制框架，利用高斯过程隐式曲面（GPIS）模型和触觉传感，在未知表面上同时实现系统性的曲面覆盖与在线几何重建，无需事先获取表面几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遍历控制方法在执行接触类任务（如检查、清洁、打磨、抛光）时，依赖先验的表面几何知识或需要通过视觉传感器预扫描表面，这在未知或动态环境中限制了其实用性。因此需要一种无需预先几何信息、能在执行过程中同时学习表面形状并实现覆盖的新方法。

Method: 采用GPIS模型从执行过程中的内在触觉传感学习全局表面几何；通过接触点处的切平面采样的点云局部近似曲面，并迭代拟合到高斯过程；该近似同时作为目标分布和覆盖分布的采样域；利用热扩散类比计算势场以引导遍历探索，将空间覆盖目标转化为平滑的机器人轨迹。

Result: 通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证了该框架，实现了同步的遍历覆盖与在线表面几何学习，重建误差接近真实值。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了在未知表面上无需视觉预扫描即可同时进行系统覆盖和几何重建，为接触类任务在未知/动态环境中的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Contact-centric tasks on surfaces, ranging from inspection and cleaning to sanding and polishing, require robots to systematically cover the surface while maintaining stable contact. Ergodic control generates trajectories that spend time at a location proportional to a desired, task-specific spatial distribution, enabling efficient information gathering and coverage. However, traditional ergodic control methods rely on prior knowledge of surface geometry or require a vision sensory input to scan the geometry beforehand, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios with unknown or dynamic environments. This paper introduces a novel online ergodic control framework that achieves systematic surface coverage while simultaneously reconstructing unknown surface geometry. We employ a Gaussian Process Implicit Surface (GPIS) model that learns global surface geometry from intrinsic tactile sensing during execution. For efficient online planning, we approximate the surface locally using point clouds sampled from tangent planes at observed contact points and iteratively fit them to the Gaussian Process. This approximation simultaneously serves as the sampling domain for both the target and the coverage distributions. We employ a heat-diffusion analogy to compute potential fields that guide ergodic exploration, translating spatial coverage objectives into smooth robot trajectories. We demonstrate our framework through simulation and real-robot experiments, validating simultaneous ergodic coverage and online surface geometry learning with reconstruction errors approaching the ground truth.

</details>


### [286] [VIDP: Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy for Compliant Robot Manipulation from Diverse Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06210)
*Hisham Khalil,Neil Fernandes,Thomas M. Kwok,Hsiu-Chin Lin,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIDP（可变阻抗扩散策略），一种基于模仿学习的可变阻抗控制框架，利用任务参数化的方向感知混合模型（TP-DAMM）从多样演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布，并将其映射为刚度分布，从而无需力传感器即可联合预测位姿动作和任务顺应性。实验表明VIDP在任务成功率、交互力和跟踪误差方面均优于固定阻抗基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要精确跟踪和机械顺应性，但静态顺应性无法适应变化的接触约束。可变阻抗技能可从演示中学习以避免复杂建模，然而顺应性是力无关运动学数据中的隐藏变量。现有的从轨迹变化推断顺应性的方法，在空间布局变化时，轨迹变化可能反映几何适应而非有意顺应，因此难以准确提取真实的顺应性意图。

Method: 提出VIDP框架，主要包括：(1) 采用任务参数化的方向感知混合模型（TP-DAMM）从多样化演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布；(2) 将轨迹分布映射为刚度分布剖面；(3) 利用扩散策略联合预测位姿动作与任务顺应性，全程无需力传感器。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，VIDP相比固定阻抗基线在任务成功率上显著提升；相比高刚度控制器降低了交互力；相比低刚度基线降低了跟踪误差。

Conclusion: VIDP通过从运动学演示中提取并建模可变阻抗，实现了无需力传感器的联合位姿-顺应性控制，兼顾了任务成功率、交互力控制和跟踪精度，验证了利用轨迹分布推断顺应性的可行性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise tracking and mechanical compliance, where variable impedance control can improve robustness in task success, whereas static compliance cannot adapt to varying contact constraints. Variable impedance skills can be learned from demonstrations, avoiding complex modeling, but compliance is a hidden variable in force-agnostic kinematic data. While existing methods infer compliance from trajectory variations, these variations may reflect geometric adaptation and not intentional compliance when subject to changing spatial layouts. Therefore, this letter introduces Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy (VIDP), an imitation learning-based variable impedance control framework leveraging a Task-Parameterized Directionality-Aware Mixture Model (TP-DAMM) to extract physically consistent trajectory distributions from diverse demonstrations. By mapping distributions to stiffness profiles, VIDP jointly predicts pose actions and task compliance without force sensors. Real-world experiments show that VIDP significantly outperforms fixed-impedance baselines in task success rate while reducing interaction forces with respect to high stiffness controllers and tracking errors with respect to low stiffness baselines.

</details>


### [287] [Design and Evaluation of a Touchscreen-Based Teleoperation Interface for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06219)
*Juan José García Cárdenas,Alperen Kenan,Hamidreza Raei,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani,Arash Ajoudani,Adriana Tapus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究设计并评估了一种新颖的触屏遥操作界面，用于核工业中的表面接触任务（如拭子采样）。与传统的操纵杆相比，该触屏界面实现了53.5%的任务完成时间减少、更高的路径覆盖率和更低的超调量，同时降低了认知负荷。远程跨国控制实验（Franka Emika Panda机械臂）验证了其在实际场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核工业环境中的表面接触任务（如拭子采样）需要精确的路径和力跟踪、避障以及持续的操作员注意力，而传统的操纵杆界面难以有效支持这些需求。研究旨在通过设计更自然、直观的遥操作界面来克服现有控制方式的局限性。

Method: 设计了一种将连续手指运动直接映射到机械臂运动的触屏遥操作界面，提供更精细的速度控制并集成可视化与控制功能。随后进行了包含20名参与者的比较性用户研究，评估了三种控制条件（触屏界面、传统操纵杆、单击自主模式）下的任务性能和认知负荷。实验使用Franka Emika Panda机械臂，在跨国远程控制条件下模拟真实表面操作，并记录了运动学、生理和行为数据。

Result: 触屏界面使任务完成时间减少53.5%（中位数2.50 vs 5.38分钟），正弦路径覆盖率更高（90.7% vs 84.1%），两种路径几何下的超调量均更低。认知负荷（NASA-TLX）从操纵杆的52降至触屏的43（-17.3%），自主模式最低为31（较操纵杆-40.4%，较触屏-27.9%）。

Conclusion: 该触屏遥操作界面易于实现，能显著提升远程表面操作任务的性能，同时降低操作员的认知负荷，为核工业及其他复杂环境中的遥操作提供了优于传统操纵杆的替代方案。

Abstract: Intuitive teleoperation interfaces are crucial for the safe and effective operation of robotic manipulators in challenging environments. In the nuclear industry, surface contact tasks such as swab sampling require precise path and force tracking, obstacle avoidance, and sustained operator attention, which conventional joystick interfaces struggle to support effectively.
  This study designs and evaluates a novel touchscreen teleoperation interface that maps continuous finger movements directly to robotic manipulator motions, provides finer velocity control, and integrates control with visualization, enabling more natural, precise, and intuitive surface interaction than conventional controllers. A comparative user study with 20 participants evaluated task performance and workload using the proposed touchscreen, a conventional joystick, and a single-click autonomous mode. Tasks simulated realistic surface manipulation using a Franka Emika Panda arm, remotely controlled from another country. Kinematic, physiological, and behavioral data were recorded to comprehensively assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator trust across each control condition.
  Participants completed teleoperation tasks more efficiently and accurately with the touchscreen interface, achieving a 53.5% reduction in completion time (median: 2.50 vs. 5.38 min), higher in-area coverage on the sinusoidal path (90.7% vs. 84.1%), and lower overshoot on both path geometries compared with the joystick. Cognitive load, quantified via NASA-TLX (0-100), decreased from joystick to touchscreen (mean TLX 52 to 43; -9 points, -17.3%) and was lowest under the autonomous one-click mode (31; -21 points vs. joystick, -40.4%; -12 vs. touchscreen, -27.9%). This research presents an easy-to-implement touchscreen interface that improves performance in teleoperated surface tasks while reducing cognitive load.

</details>


### [288] [Robot Learning from Human Demonstrations: Handwritten Alphabet Trajectories and Human-Likeness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06221)
*Alperen Kenan,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从示教学习（LfD）的框架，用于生成类人机器人运动。框架涵盖数据采集、概率轨迹学习和感知用户评估三个环节。基于高斯混合模型（GMM）和高斯混合回归（GMR），通过引入力和归一化时间维度，并适配非连续多段轨迹，实现更丰富的人体动力学表示。实验从22名参与者采集了3,142条手写演示数据（覆盖52种拉丁字母大小写组合），经21名用户评估，生成轨迹的类人感知得分达71.50（SD=22.56），表明大多数轨迹被认为更接近人类运动。数据集以开源形式发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人编程方式对专业技能依赖强，而人形运动被证明是建立人机信任和自然协作的关键因素。现有LfD方法通常忽略力维度和轨迹的非连续性，缺乏对类人运动的系统评估，因此需要一套涵盖数据、算法和感知评估的完整框架。

Method: 框架包括三部分：(1) 数据采集——通过触摸屏遥操作界面从22名参与者采集3,142条手写演示，记录平面位置、接触力和时间信息；(2) 轨迹学习——在经典GMM/GMR基础上引入力和归一化时间维度，并扩展以处理非连续、多段轨迹以实现跨演示的泛化；(3) 感知评估——21名参与者使用连续量表（0-100，50为中性点）对生成轨迹的类人程度进行评分。

Result: 生成轨迹的整体类人感知得分为71.50（SD=22.56），表明大多数轨迹被感知为更接近人类。参与者认为几何定位和轨迹序列是最具影响力的感知因素，并对类人机器人行为持积极态度。数据集已开源发布。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了机器人轨迹的类人感知程度，证实了在LfD中引入力和时间维度以及处理多段轨迹的有效性。开源数据集为类人机器人运动方法的开发和评估提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) provides a developmental framework through which robots can develop motor skills by observing and imitating human dynamics, reducing reliance on explicit programming to teach a skill to a robot. The resulting human-like robot motion is recognised as a key factor in building trust and enabling natural collaboration in human-robot interaction. This paper presents a framework for learning human-like robot motion from demonstration, including data collection, probabilistic trajectory learning, and perceptual user evaluation. A dataset of 3,142 handwriting demonstrations was collected from 22 participants across all 52 Latin alphabet character-case combinations via a touchscreen teleoperation interface, capturing planar position, contact force, and timing. Building on the widely used Gaussian Mixture Model and Gaussian Mixture Regression approach for learning from demonstration, the framework is extended in this work by incorporating force and normalised time dimensions to enable richer representation of human dynamics, and adapting it to handle non-continuous, multi-segment trajectories, enabling generalisation across demonstrations. A user study with 21 participants evaluated the perceived human-likeness of the generated trajectories using a continuous scale anchored between robotic and human-like motion, normalised to 0-100 where 50 represents the neutral midpoint. The generated trajectories achieved an overall human-likeness score of 71.50 (SD=22.56), indicating that the majority of trajectories were perceived as more human-like. Participants identified geometric positioning and trajectory sequence as the most influential perceptual factors, and reported positive attitudes toward human-like robot behaviour. The datasets are released as open-source, providing a reproducible benchmark for developing and evaluating human-like robot motion methods.

</details>


### [289] [A Master-Salve Robot Manipulator for Needle-Based Teleoperation in MRI Chamber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06354)
*Omar Curiel,Jing-Yuan Huang,Po-Chih Chen,Ji Ma,Qing Dai,Wenqi Zhou,David Lu,Holden H. Wu,Tsu-Chin Tsao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于MRI腔内腹部介入手术的MR安全主从式机器人操纵器。该系统通过流体传动实现人工主控制器（2+1自由度）与从端操纵器的运动及力传递，并集成了数字主控制器以支持手动、数字、混合及协作等多种控制模式。初步MRI扫描仪实验验证了床边手动控制模式下在活体猪实验中的功能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中的混合人机控制器多采用分裂轴或模式切换配置，缺乏多模态控制能力。为在MRI环境中实现更灵活、安全的腹部介入手术，需要一种既能提供高透明度力/运动传递，又能支持多种协作控制模式的MRI安全操纵器。

Method: 采用主从式架构：人工操作的主控制器（2+1自由度）通过流体传动将运动和力传递给从端操纵器。系统设计中，高输入阻抗、低泄漏的弹性体流体致动器负责远端角度控制，而低摩擦石墨活塞气缸负责针插入轴的远程驱动，以实现亚牛顿级力透明度和亚毫米级运动传递。同时集成数字主控制器，提供超越传统分裂轴或模式切换配置的多模态控制能力，支持手动、数字、混合和协作四种控制模式。

Result: 初步MRI扫描仪结果表明，该操纵器在床边手动控制模式下具备功能可行性，并已在活体猪实验中成功验证。协作任务（如辅助组织穿透、故障驱动的虚拟夹具和运动补偿）也通过反馈控制实现。

Conclusion: 该MR安全主从式流体驱动操纵器为MRI引导下的腹部介入手术提供了可行的多模态控制平台，兼具高力/运动透明度和协作控制能力，为未来临床应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a MR safe, master-slave robot manipulator for abdominal interventions in the MRI chamber. A human operated 2+1-DoF master controller manipulator transmits motion and force to a 2+1-DoF slave manipulator via fluid transmission. Jointly, a digital master controller provides multimodal control capability beyond common split axis or mode switchable hybrid human-digital controller configurations found in previous studies. High input impedance, low-leakage, elastomeric fluid actuators are delegated to remote angulation control. Low-friction graphite piston cylinders are delegated to needle insertion axis remote actuation given the sub-newton force transparency and sub-millimeter motion transmission over bedside fluid piping lengths. The device enables real-time MRI guided interventions allowing manual, digital, hybrid, and collaborative control modes. Collaborative tasks such as assisted tissue penetration, fault-driven virtual fixture, and motion compensation through feedback control are presented in this paper. Preliminary MR scanner results demonstrate manipulator functional viability for an in-vivo pig experiment in bedside, manual control mode configuration.

</details>


### [290] [DyPES-VLA: Learning Shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific Control for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06374)
*Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,Yangyang Zheng,Pingyue Sheng,Jiayu Dong,Ruixin Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Runze Yu,Wen Chen,Liuqing Yang,Yuxiang Gao,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DyPES-VLA，一种跨具身机器人操作的通用VLA模型，通过学习共享的动力学先验和具身特定控制来实现异构机器人体的跨具身泛化，在LIBERO、RoboCasa-GR1和RoboTwin 2.0基准上均达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在异构机器人体上的跨具身泛化存在两大问题：一是未充分利用跨不同视觉和交互数据中共享的动力学先验，限制了跨具身迁移能力；二是需要大量手动预处理将不同具身特有的动作转换为统一格式，增加了工程负担和适应性难度。

Method: (1) 通过在未来预测目标下训练视觉语言模型（VLM）于跨具身数据上，学习共享的动力学先验，使共享查询表示能够捕捉物体运动、接触和交互引起的场景变化；(2) 设计具身特定的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）动作头，直接在每种具身的原生动作空间中生成可执行控制，无需手动预对齐异构动作。该动作头共享注意力层以捕捉通用的时间动作结构，同时使用具身特定的前馈专家网络来解决不同具身独特的运动学约束和控制语义。

Result: 作为通用策略，DyPES-VLA在仿真和真实世界评估中均达到最先进水平：LIBERO上98.0%的成功率，RoboCasa-GR1上59.25%，RoboTwin 2.0上89.02%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦共享动力学先验与具身特定控制，DyPES-VLA有效解决了异构机器人体的跨具身泛化问题，消除了繁琐的手动动作对齐需求，验证了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a powerful paradigm for robot manipulation, but training a single generalist policy for heterogeneous robot embodiments remains an open problem. Existing methods have two main limitations. First, they underuse dynamics priors shared across diverse visual and interaction data, limiting cross-embodiment transfer. Second, they require extensive manual preprocessing to convert embodiment-specific actions into a common format. To overcome these limitations, we propose DyPES-VLA, a cross-embodiment VLA that learns shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific control. First, we learn shared dynamics priors by training the vision-language model (VLM) with a future-prediction objective on cross-embodiment data, driving the shared query representation to capture object motion, contact, and interaction-induced scene changes. Second, an embodiment-specific Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) action head translates these shared dynamics priors into executable controls directly in each embodiment's native action space, without manually pre-aligning heterogeneous actions into a common format. This head shares attention layers to capture common temporal action structures, while its embodiment-specific feed-forward experts resolve the unique kinematic constraints and control semantics of distinct embodiments. As a generalist policy, our \ourmethod achieves state-of-the-art performance across simulation and real-world evaluations, reaching 98.0% success on LIBERO, 59.25% on RoboCasa-GR1, and 89.02% on RoboTwin~2.0.

</details>


### [291] [$ω$-0: A Latent Predictive World Action Model for Concurrent Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06375)
*Zhe Li,Zhenzhe Zhang,Yangyang Wei,Wenjie Zhang,Xichen Yuan,Peiyuan Zhi,Gen Li,Xinying Guo,Fengjie Gao,Jianfei Yang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ω-0，一个用于真实世界人形机器人并发移动-操控（loco-manipulation）的潜在预测全身世界动作模型。它直接将语言指令、视觉观测和本体感受状态映射为控制器兼容的全身动作隐变量，通过学习未来观测嵌入的轻量预测目标，耦合潜在视觉预见与基于扩散的全身动作生成，并在11个家庭任务上超越多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的家庭任务通常需要并发移动-操控（loco-manipulation），即机器人必须同时完成移动、姿态调整、保持平衡和物体操控，作为一个协调统一的行为。然而现有的人形机器人策略通常将运动和操控解耦处理，而近期世界动作模型要么以手臂为中心，要么以视频为中心，无法有效支持全身协调的并发移动-操控任务。

Method: ω-0模型接收语言指令、当前视觉观测和机器人本体感受状态，直接预测控制器兼容的全身动作隐变量用于真实机器人执行。它不重建未来视频，而是学习紧凑的未来观测嵌入作为轻量预测目标，将潜在视觉预见与基于扩散的全身动作生成耦合。模型支持第一人称RGB、第三人称RGB和第三人称深度输入，并通过基于控制器的仿真回放将人类/公共视觉运动先验锚定到机器人可执行的动作隐变量中。此外，作者收集了ω-HOME数据集，包含40+小时的真实世界家庭人形机器人数据，同步多视角观测、全身SMPL运动、机器人状态和动作隐变量。

Result: 在11个真实世界家庭任务上的实验表明，单一的ω-0模型能够产生平滑的边移动边操控行为，并持续优于代表性的模仿学习、VLA、人形机器人和世界动作模型（WAM）基线方法。

Conclusion: ω-0证明了一个统一的潜在预测全身世界动作模型可以有效地解决人形机器人并发移动-操控问题，为真实世界的家庭人形机器人全任务提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Humanoid household tasks often require concurrent loco-manipulation, where the robot must move, adjust posture, maintain balance, and manipulate objects as a single coordinated behavior. Yet existing humanoid policies typically decompose locomotion and manipulation, while recent world-action models remain either arm-centric or video-centered. We present $ω$-0, a latent predictive whole-body world-action model for real-world humanoid concurrent loco-manipulation. Given a language instruction, current visual observation, and robot proprioceptive state, $ω$-0 directly predicts controller-compatible whole-body action latents for real-robot execution. Rather than reconstructing future videos, $ω$-0 learns compact future observation embeddings as a lightweight predictive objective, coupling latent visual foresight with diffusion-based whole-body action generation. The model supports egocentric RGB, exocentric RGB, and exocentric depth inputs, and leverages controller-based simulation replay to ground human/public visual-motion priors into robot-executable action latents. We further collect $ω$-HOME, a 40+ hour real-world household humanoid dataset with synchronized multi-view observations, whole-body SMPL motions, robot states, and action latents. Real-world experiments on 11 household tasks demonstrate that a single $ω$-0 model can produce smooth manipulate-while-moving behaviors and consistently outperform representative imitation learning, VLA, humanoid, and WAM baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRAFTER，一种通过挖掘冻结预训练预测器残差中的可解释特征，驱动轻量级事后修正器的方法。相比微调，该方法更高效，在六大数据集上显著优于现有特征工程系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器会以结构化、可重复的方式出现错误，通过微调修复成本高昂。作者提出矫正性特征发现（corrective feature discovery），主张建模"模型失败过程"而非数据生成过程，从而以低代价修复预测器缺陷。

Method: CRAFTER将骨干模型保持冻结，通过两个互补的生成器挖掘其残差：一是对原始输入通道的组合搜索；二是基于大型语言模型（LLM）提出命名特征组合、二元标志和短可执行代码。所有候选特征均由统一的基于验证集的闸门接受或拒绝，并由验证集选择的修正器应用被接受的特征或保持预测不变。该源无关管线还可使其他特征工程系统在相同条件下评估。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨干上，CRAFTER在每种特征预算下均超越所有专用特征工程系统，将仅修正器的改进效果约翻倍，并使最弱骨干的误差降低高达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端下均稳健，并在微调后的骨干上依然有效。

Conclusion: 矫正性特征发现是一种有效的预测器修复范式，CRAFTER通过源无关的特征挖掘管线不仅能提升性能，还能作为工具来归因预测改进的来源，表明特征来源本身即可带来显著增益。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [293] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编程基于大语言模型（LLM）流程的概率语言，使开发者能够在整个应用流程中量化和传播不确定性，并在不修改流程逻辑代码的情况下实验不同的推理缩放技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的应用难以建立信任，因为LLM输出缺乏准确性和置信度度量。在多次LLM调用和工具调用组成的流程中，这种不确定性会累积放大，使开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。

Method: 引入一种概率编程语言来构建LLM流程，支持在流程中量化并传播不确定性，同时允许开发者通过配置而非修改代码来实验不同的推理缩放（inference scaling）技术。

Result: 提供了实验研究证明该能力，并展示了一个针对Rocq定理证明器构建的定理证明智能体的案例研究。

Conclusion: 该概率语言能够帮助开发者构建更可靠、可信任的LLM应用，通过显式的不确定性量化和传播机制，在不增加代码复杂度的前提下提升流程的可靠性。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [294] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一个4B参数的时序-语言多模态模型，通过将感知与描述解耦来解决多模态时序对齐中的自监督陷阱：用确定性代码计算真实多变量时序数据的统计量，由LLM对这些预计算事实进行语言化描述。CGTime在多变量理解基准上超越了更大的通用模型（包括GPT系列），并在生成描述中更准确地陈述可验证的数值事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练多模态模型对齐时序与语言存在自监督陷阱：让LLM读取序列并生成描述，标签质量受限于模型自身的感知能力，数据无法教会模型超出标签者已知范围的知识。此外，多数数据集仅使用单变量，但真正重要的模式（跨通道相关性、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）只出现在多变量场景中，恰好暴露了标签LLM的局限性。这两个问题造成三角困境：现有方法只能兼顾可靠、真实与可扩展中的两项，无法三者兼得。

Method: 将感知与描述解耦：确定性代码从真实开源多变量序列中计算统计量，LLM仅负责将这些预计算的事实转化为语言描述。感知（LLM不擅长的部分）由计算处理，LLM负责表达。由此构建4B参数的CGTime模型。

Result: CGTime在多变量理解任务上超越更大的通用模型：在保留基准上取得最佳多变量事实分数（0.283，对比GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203），该差距通过Holm校正配对显著性检验对每个基线均保持显著。同时，生成的描述中可验证数值事实更准确，覆盖更广泛的统计属性范围。

Conclusion: 通过计算与语言能力的分工架构，CGTime解决了多模态时序-语言对齐中的自监督陷阱和三角困境，证明在感知层面用计算替代LLM推理、让LLM专注表达是一种可行且高效的方法，能以较小的模型规模获得卓越的多变量时序理解能力。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [295] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSR框架，将3D感知与空间推理显式解耦：用现成的感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据，再通过LoRA微调LLM仅基于几何证据进行推理，在不需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具策略的情况下取得竞争性性能，同时提升可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型3D-VQA端到端模型需要大规模训练来隐式地同时获取3D感知和推理能力，这既低效又缺乏可解释性。作者观察到感知模型擅长连续3D几何估计，而LLM擅长组合与符号推理，二者具有互补优势，因此提出将二者解耦结合的范式。

Method: DiSR包含两步流程：(1) 使用开箱即用的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化的3D证据（显式几何信息）；(2) 使用LoRA对LLM进行微调，使其仅基于这些显式几何证据执行空间推理，不依赖大规模3D VQA预训练或复杂的工具使用策略。

Result: 在主流空间推理基准上，DiSR取得了与端到端方法相当的竞争性性能，同时显著提升了可解释性、模块化程度和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是空间智能领域中一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，比起端到端建模更具优势，为空间推理提供了一条模块化、可解释且高效的新路径。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [296] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中一个常见误区：将潜在风格变量 z_s 的边缘分布匹配到高斯先验，并不能保证其与类别信息独立。仅匹配边缘分布对类别条件分布没有约束，风格变量仍可能高度预测标签。文章推导出精确分解，证明消除边缘-条件分布不匹配只是因子化采样的四个必要条件之一（必要但不充分），并通过大量实验证实了这一论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型普遍将潜在风格变量的边缘分布正则化到固定高斯先验，并据此声称风格表示与类别信息无关。作者认为这一解读是错误的，因为边缘分布匹配对类别条件分布不施加任何约束，风格变量在整体上看似高斯、却仍可能携带大量类别可预测信息。该工作旨在揭示这一理论缺陷，并实证验证其严重性。

Method: 作者首先推导了一个精确分解，表明因式化采样需要四个条件，而"匹配边缘分布"与"匹配每个类别条件分布"之间的不匹配只是其中之一。在实证方面，作者构建了一个案例研究模型和四个代表性潜在变量基线，测量全局MMD（接近零）的同时，用线性探针恢复类别标签（74%-100%准确率）。此外，作者测试了六种扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督信号、两个数据集），并评估了四种缓解策略以及两种后处理方案（条件先验和经验风格库）。

Result: 案例模型达到99.15%的聚类准确率，而外部评估的类别条件生成仅成功16%。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本未变。后处理条件先验在MNIST上达到0.97的类别生成成功率，但CIFAR-10仅0.41；经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。所有结果均表明：仅依赖风格潜在变量边缘分布的散度无法证明与类标签的独立性。

Conclusion: 仅报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型通常声称的性质。消除边缘-条件分布不匹配虽然是因子化采样的必要条件，但远非充分条件。研究者必须对类别条件分布本身进行验证，而不能仅凭边缘分布的高斯拟合就宣称风格表示与类别信息独立。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [297] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"规则理解"（rubric comprehension）范式，将多模态模型从"被评分者"转变为"规则执行者"：给定图像和带优先级的规则列表，模型需逐条验证后才给出整体判断。作者提出四阶段数据合成框架PRISM和配套评估基准PRISM-Eval，仅用10K合成样本即将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且增益可迁移至四种开源多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态指令通常捆绑多条重要性不等的要求，但现有多模态训练数据仍将指令遵循简化为回答单个自包含问题，缺乏对多规则、带优先级指令遵循能力的系统性研究与数据支撑。

Method: (1)提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：生成persona-task对、前缀引导的规则集、质量过滤的规范（rubric）及结构化验证轨迹；(2)提出PRISM-Eval评估基准，包含Loose和Strict两种指标，采用对固定标签的确定性匹配，无需推理时的评判模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能不降；增益可迁移至四种额外的开源MLLM（覆盖稠密和MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化规则监督是通往多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径，验证了从"被评分"到"执行规则"的训练范式转变的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [298] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT是一个参数高效的、可感知过拟合的多任务微调框架，将MSFT中基于全模型检查点的调度状态重构为紧凑可合并的适配器状态，在冻结预训练骨干的情况下仅训练注入的适配器，实现任务级回滚和合并，且无需存储全模型检查点。在保留骨干的对比协议下，AuroSFT在5个骨干网络上平均准确率61.36%，优于MSFT的59.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSFT通过任务级roll-out、排除和回滚揭示了多任务SFT中不同任务在不同时间达到最优泛化的问题，但其原始实现将调度器状态具体化为全模型检查点，导致阶段切换在存储、恢复和部署方面代价高昂。作者希望将承载状态从全模型检查点转变为紧凑、可合并的适配器状态，以降低开销。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练的骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器。在每个任务达到峰值时回滚相应的适配器检查点，并继续在剩余的活跃混合任务上训练。在层级别，每个适配器采用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层，作用于低秩权重因子而非样本表示，使得更新对输入保持线性、秩有界，并可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干的对比协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达到61.36%，优于对应MSFT参考行的59.85%，并且在全部5个骨干网络上均获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT证明了将过拟合感知的多任务SFT调度状态参数化地压缩为可合并适配器状态不仅可行，还能在降低存储与部署开销的同时提升性能，为多任务微调提供了更高效且更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [299] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroOFT，一种在量化正交微调（qoft）基础上增加零启动门控低秩非线性残差分支的参数高效微调方法，在保持量化兼容性的同时引入输入依赖的非线性校正，在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B模型上相比qoft提升1.30-2.70% Macro-6，相比QLoRA提升6.52-10.62%，并节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qoft虽然能实现低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其任务特定更新局限于线性正交变换，无法提供输入依赖的非线性校正，限制了模型的表达能力。

Method: AuroOFT在保留qoft作为稳定的量化兼容分支的同时，为每个适配的线性层附加零启动的门控低秩非线性残差分支。该分支将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜空间，使用自适应非线性基并采用有界或token相关的门控机制。零初始化的上投影使AuroOFT在初始化时与qoft功能完全一致，正交性仅作为分支级稳定性属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B设置上相比匹配的qoft提升Macro-6 1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并在代表性规模下相比QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过在qoft基础上引入零启动非线性残差分支，有效增强了低比特模型微调的非线性表达能力，同时保持量化兼容性和参数效率。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [300] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的、完全构建且经过集成测试的税收感知型个性化投资组合管理应用。该系统由FastAPI后端和Web仪表板组成，通过自然语言理解用户投资目标，路由至六种投资任务之一，并由一个三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、基于MoE的分配策略与学习意图路由器、个性化LoRA适配器）生成实时、券商集成的投资组合建议。系统功能完整并通过Alpaca模拟交易API进行了端到端集成测试，但尚未对真实用户开放，仅以14天前向回测作为预部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者无法获得机构客户所享有的个性化、税收感知型投资组合管理服务：现有机器人投顾采用静态规则分配，机构级系统又要求账户最低额度和个人投资者无法获得的技术栈。作者旨在构建一个弥合这一差距的完整应用。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：(1) FastAPI后端与Web仪表板，支持自然语言描述投资目标并路由至六个投资任务；(2) 三阶段强化学习系统，包括自监督跨资产编码器、带学习意图路由器的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）分配策略、以及无需重训共享模型即可基于个人券商行为进行个性化推荐的轻量LoRA适配器；(3) 完整的工程集成，含多用户认证、预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计的操作完整性链，并通过Alpaca模拟交易API进行端到端集成测试。

Result: 系统功能完整且通过真实的券商API（Alpaca模拟模式）进行了端到端集成测试。14天前向回测（含自助法置信区间）作为预部署验证提供了初步证据。此外报告了若干工程经验教训，包括静默失效的集成路径、挂起的第三方API调用，以及端到端经验验证优于信任检查点元数据的价值。

Conclusion: 该系统是一个诚实地报告为预部署阶段的、新兴的应用，具备明确的完整部署路径。其工程经验教训被认为可推广到其他构建于外部实时数据源之上的应用型强化学习系统。作者坦承当前验证仅为预部署性质的初步结果，而非生产性能。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [301] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN）用于野火后泥石流预测，通过重复分层交叉验证、SHAP特征重要性分析和合成数据增强三方面解决模型选择、可解释性和数据不足三大挑战。TabPFN以0.637的威胁评分取得最佳无增强性能，SHAP揭示短期降雨强度是最重要特征，合成数据增强使除CNN外所有模型性能提升（深度学习模型平均威胁评分提升+0.041）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流的准确预测对减轻社区、基础设施和资源危害至关重要，但面临三大挑战：泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间中高度重叠导致模型识别困难、模型可解释性需求、以及训练数据有限。现有研究缺乏对这些挑战的系统性评估。

Method: 使用美国西部流域尺度的野火后泥石流观测数据，系统比较15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN）。采用重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能，使用SHAP分析特征重要性，并利用TabPFN生成的合成样本进行数据增强以缓解泥石流观测数据稀缺问题。

Result: TabPFN在无增强条件下取得最高威胁评分0.637，紧随其后的是最优树模型。SHAP分析显示短期降雨强度和风暴累积量排名最高，而燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较小。合成数据增强提升了除CNN外所有模型的性能，深度学习模型的平均威胁评分提升最大（+0.041）。

Conclusion: 通过严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强相结合，本研究为野火后泥石流预测提供了综合框架，有助于在数据稀缺和特征重叠的复杂条件下选择可靠模型并提升预测性能。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models.
